50static CODE_TEX_EXT: &str = ".code.tex";

52/// regex for *.tex and *.bib
53static TEX_OR_BIB_EXT_RE: Lazy<Regex> = Lazy::new(|| Regex::new(r"\.(tex|bib)$").unwrap());
54/// Used in conversion to scaled points.
55///
56/// These are the float `sp`-per-unit ratios, kept for *display / scaling*
57/// consumers (pgf/graphics/hyperref divide by them). For dimension
58/// **construction** use [`convert_unit_ratio`] + `numeric_ops::fixpoint_unit`
59/// instead — exact integer arithmetic, bit-faithful to TeX (issue #127). Each
60/// value here equals `65536·num/den` of the matching `convert_unit_ratio` entry.
61pub static UNITS: Lazy<HashMap<String, f64>> = Lazy::new(|| {
62  map!(
63    "pt" => 65536.0,
64    "pc" => 12.0 * 65536.0,
65    "in" => 72.27 * 65536.0,
66    "bp" => 72.27 * 65536.0 / 72.0,
67    "px" => 72.27 * 65536.0 / 72.0,   // Assume px=bp ?
68    "cm" => 72.27 * 65536.0 / 2.54,
69    "mm" => 72.27 * 65536.0 / 2.54 / 10.0,
70    "dd" => 1238.0 * 65536.0 / 1157.0,
71    "cc" => 12.0 * 1238.0 * 65536.0 / 1157.0,
72    "sp" => 1.0
73  )
74});

76/// installation scope in the state_tables
77#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
78pub enum Scope {
79  /// globally visible, does not expire
80  Global,
81  /// globally visible, but expires at the end of the current group
82  Local,
83  /// a named scope - visible only when explicitly activated
84  Named(SymStr),
85  /// in-place: replace the value in the frame it was last bound in, WITHOUT
86  /// recording a new undo entry (or globally, at the locked base frame, if it
87  /// was never bound). Perl `State.pm:175` `$scope eq 'inplace'` ("Special case
88  /// for `\box` & friends"). This is Knuth's "same level" reassignment / what
89  /// @xworld21 tentatively called `scope => 'definition'`: the binding keeps its
90  /// save-stack level, so a mutation rides exactly as long as the current
91  /// binding — persisting past the current group if the binding was made above
92  /// it, reverting with the group if it was made locally. Distinct from Global
93  /// (which promotes + wipes lower-frame undo) and Local (which pushes an outer
94  /// binding down a level). The Value-table fast path is `assign_value_inplace`.
95  InPlace,
96}
97
98/// the kinds of tables bookkept in the State
99#[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
100pub enum TableName {
101  /// token meaning
102  Meaning,
103  /// all stateful values
104  Value,
105  /// catcode bindings
106  Catcode,
107  /// mathcode bindings
108  Mathcode,
109  /// sf code bindings
110  Sfcode,
111  /// lc code bindings
112  Lccode,
113  /// uc code bindings
114  Uccode,
115  /// del code bindings
116  Delcode,
117  /// stash of inactive named values
118  Stash,
119  /// active stash of named values
120  StashActive,
121}
122impl Display for TableName {
123  fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
124    write!(f, "{}", match self {
125      TableName::Meaning => "Meaning",
126      TableName::Value => "Value",
127      TableName::Catcode => "Catcode",
128      TableName::Mathcode => "Mathcode",
129      TableName::Sfcode => "Sfcode",
130      TableName::Lccode => "Lccode",
131      TableName::Uccode => "Uccode",
132      TableName::Delcode => "Delcode",
133      TableName::Stash => "Stash",
134      TableName::StashActive => "StashActive",
135    })
136  }
137}
138impl TableName {
139  /// provides all TableName variants. useful for iterating over all tables
140  pub fn variants() -> &'static [TableName] {
141    use self::TableName::*;
142    &[
143      Meaning,
144      Value,
145      Catcode,
146      Mathcode,
147      Sfcode,
148      Lccode,
149      Uccode,
150      Delcode,
151      Stash,
152      StashActive,
153    ]
154  }
155}

157/// High-level catcode profiles
158#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
159pub enum Catcodes {
160  /// the usual mainmatter catcodes (e.g. @ is other)
161  Standard,
162  /// the usual style catcodes (e.g. @ is letter)
163  Style,
164  /// left unspecified
165  None,
166}

168/// Ledger for stacked assignments
169pub type AssignmentCount = HashMap<SymStr, usize>;
170/// The `(table_name, key, value)` contents of a stored table of assignments
171pub type StashTable = Vec<(TableName, SymStr, Stored)>;
172#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
173/// For each of several tables (being "value", "meaning", "catcode" or other space of names),
174/// each table maintains the bound values, and "undo" defines the stack frames
175pub struct UndoFrame {
176  /// The frame's identity, unique to it (an assignment journal tells the frames it began with from later ones at the
177  /// same depth: `AssignmentJournalGuard::rollback`).
178  id:           u64,
179  locked:       bool,
180  meaning:      AssignmentCount,
181  value:        AssignmentCount,
182  catcode:      AssignmentCount,
183  mathcode:     AssignmentCount,
184  sfcode:       AssignmentCount,
185  lccode:       AssignmentCount,
186  uccode:       AssignmentCount,
187  delcode:      AssignmentCount,
188  stash:        AssignmentCount,
189  stash_active: AssignmentCount,
190}
191
192/// The next `UndoFrame::id`.
193static NEXT_FRAME_ID: std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64 = std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64::new(1);
194
195impl Default for UndoFrame {
196  fn default() -> Self {
197    UndoFrame {
198      id:           NEXT_FRAME_ID.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed),
199      locked:       false,
200      meaning:      AssignmentCount::default(),
201      value:        AssignmentCount::default(),
202      catcode:      AssignmentCount::default(),
203      mathcode:     AssignmentCount::default(),
204      sfcode:       AssignmentCount::default(),
205      lccode:       AssignmentCount::default(),
206      uccode:       AssignmentCount::default(),
207      delcode:      AssignmentCount::default(),
208      stash:        AssignmentCount::default(),
209      stash_active: AssignmentCount::default(),
210    }
211  }
212}
213
214impl UndoFrame {
215  /// borrow the undo assignment counts for a given table name
216  pub fn table(&self, name: TableName) -> &AssignmentCount {
217    use self::TableName::*;
218    match name {
219      Meaning => &self.meaning,
220      Value => &self.value,
221      Catcode => &self.catcode,
222      Mathcode => &self.mathcode,
223      Sfcode => &self.sfcode,
224      Lccode => &self.lccode,
225      Uccode => &self.uccode,
226      Delcode => &self.delcode,
227      Stash => &self.stash,
228      StashActive => &self.stash_active,
229    }
230  }
231  /// mutably borrow the undo assignment counts for a given table name
232  pub fn table_mut(&mut self, name: TableName) -> &mut AssignmentCount {
233    use self::TableName::*;
234    match name {
235      Meaning => &mut self.meaning,
236      Value => &mut self.value,
237      Catcode => &mut self.catcode,
238      Mathcode => &mut self.mathcode,
239      Sfcode => &mut self.sfcode,
240      Lccode => &mut self.lccode,
241      Uccode => &mut self.uccode,
242      Delcode => &mut self.delcode,
243      Stash => &mut self.stash,
244      StashActive => &mut self.stash_active,
245    }
246  }
247}
248
249/// The type of values that are storable by the different namespaced "tables" in State.
250///
251/// There are tables for:
252///
253///  catcode: keys are char;
254///     Also, `math:char` =1 when `char` is active in math.
255///  mathcode, sfcode, lccode, uccode, delcode : are similar to catcode but store
256///    additional kinds codes per char (see TeX)
257///  value: keys are anything (typically a string, though) and value is the value associated with it
258///  meaning: The definition assocated with `key`, usually a control-sequence.
259///  stash & stash_active: support named scopes
260///      (see also activateScope & deactivateScope)
261pub type Table = HashMap<SymStr, VecDeque<Stored>>;
262
263/// The bindings of every key assigned since an assignment journal began ([`begin_assignment_journal`]), as they were
264/// then: taken at the key's first assignment after, the key's values in its table and its assignment counts in each
265/// frame then open (by the frame's id), so that [`AssignmentJournalGuard::rollback`] can put them back. A key's first
266/// assignment is the first time anything touches it, so the frames opened since hold none of it.
267#[derive(Debug, Default)]
268pub struct AssignmentJournal {
269  /// The meanings assigned since the log was last read, in order, each with whether globally ([`begin_meaning_log`]);
270  /// `None` while no one reads it.
271  meaning_log:    Option<Vec<MeaningAssigned>>,
272  /// the ids of the frames open when it began, from the bottom of the stack
273  frames:         Vec<u64>,
274  saved:          rustc_hash::FxHashMap<(TableName, SymStr), JournalEntry>,
275  /// each meaning's standing as a fallback guess, a kernel stub or a borrowed package's then (`fallback_meanings`),
276  /// which an assignment changes beside the meaning and no group end puts back: the meaning a rehearsal `\let`s and
277  /// rolls back keeps it (an OmniBus `\AND`, which algorithmic's `\newcommand{\AND}` may replace — siamart220329,
278  /// 2409.10200; FM S2a.2 r9)
279  fallback: rustc_hash::FxHashMap<SymStr, Option<crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin>>,
280  /// each box whose `\\wd`, `\\ht` or `\\dp` was set, the property's value then, oldest first: a property of the box itself, which
281  /// a register's binding shares ([`journal_box_dimension`])
282  box_dimensions: Vec<(Digested, &'static str, Option<Stored>)>,
283  /// the watch held when it began ([`watch_state`]), which a rollback puts back
284  watch:          u64,
285}
286/// A key's values in its table (`None`: unbound) and its assignment counts by frame id.
287type JournalEntry = (Option<VecDeque<Stored>>, Vec<(u64, usize)>);
288
289/// The state efficiently bookkeeps the bindings in a TeX-like fashion.
290///
291/// Bindings associate data with keys (eg definitions with macro names)
292/// and respect TeX grouping; that is, an assignment is only in effect
293/// until the current group (opened by \bgroup) is closed (by \egroup).
294pub struct State {
295  // Tables
296  /// bookkeeps arbitrary Stored values
297  value:                       Table,
298  /// The definition assocated with a key, usually a control-sequence.
299  meaning:                     Table,
300  stash:                       Table,
301  stash_active:                Table,
302  catcode:                     Table,
303  mathcode:                    Table,
304  sfcode:                      Table,
305  lccode:                      Table,
306  uccode:                      Table,
307  delcode:                     Table,
308  // Table bookkeeping
309  undo:                        VecDeque<UndoFrame>,
310  // stateful runtime - data structures
311  /// the schema-derived model used for the current document
312  prefixes:                    HashMap<SymStr, bool>, // ?
313  pub tag_properties:          HashMap<SymStr, TagOptions>,
314  /// an optional indirect model for long-distance relationships
315  pub indirect_model:          Option<IndirectModel>,
316  /// Document-related resources declared during core conversion, pending until XML is finalized
317  pub pending_resources:       Vec<Resource>,
318  // stateful runtime - simple fields
319  // TODO: Maybe group these in a "SessionFlags" struct?
320  //       we can then reset that if we reimplement a daemon app
321  pub verbosity:               i32,
322  pub input_encoding:          Option<String>,
323  // strict: bool,
324  // include_comments: bool,
325  /// Seed only for the group-scoped `SEARCHPATHS` value-table list (read once at
326  /// construction to seed it); live lookups go through [`get_search_paths`].
327  pub search_paths:            VecDeque<String>,
328  /// Seed only for the group-scoped `GRAPHICSPATHS` value-table list (read once at
329  /// construction to seed it); live lookups go through [`get_graphics_paths`].
330  pub graphics_paths:          VecDeque<String>,
331  // include_styles: bool,
332  /// flag to disable math parsing
333  pub nomathparse:             bool,
334  /// Perl `Conditional.pm:61-62`: the `if_count` Value entry, assigned
335  /// `'global'` on every `\if…` so the per-conditional id survives group
336  /// exit. A value only ever assigned globally collapses to one base-frame
337  /// binding, so a plain monotonic field is the same counter without the
338  /// per-conditional `assign_internal(Global)` undo-frame walk (measured
339  /// 4.4 % of instructions on `\ifnum`-dense pgf input). Runtime-only, so
340  /// it never enters a dump. Micro-divergence: `\globaldefs<0` demoted
341  /// Perl's assignment to local (ids could repeat after a group); the id is
342  /// the `IfFrame`'s diagnostic tag, never a matching key, so it is unaffected.
343  pub if_count:                i64,
344  /// Perl `Conditional.pm:63` `unshiftValue('if_stack', $ifframe)`: the active conditionals,
345  /// innermost last. Perl edits the Value in place (`unshiftValue`/`shiftValue`, never grouped
346  /// or undone), so a typed field is the same stack without the per-`\if`/`\else`/`\fi`
347  /// value-table lookups. Runtime-only, so it never enters a dump.
348  pub if_stack:                Vec<Rc<RefCell<crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame>>>,
349  /// Perl `$LaTeXML::IF_LIMIT` (`latexml.sty.ltxml:101`): a plain global,
350  /// never a State value. `0` = unlimited.
351  pub if_limit:                i64,
352  /// Perl `Object.pm:165` / `Box.pm:145`: the `absorb_count` Value, assigned
353  /// `'global'` per absorbed box — the same mechanism as [`Self::if_count`]
354  /// (a Perl global-only Value becomes a typed State scalar).
355  pub absorb_count:            i64,
356  /// Perl `$LaTeXML::ABSORB_LIMIT` (`latexml.sty.ltxml:104`), `0` = unlimited.
357  pub absorb_limit:            i64,
358  /// flag enabling source-locator (`--source-map`) tracking + emission.
359  /// Off by default; gates BOTH the per-token start capture and the
360  /// per-element `data-sourcepos` stamping so a normal conversion pays
361  /// nothing. See `docs/performance/SOURCE_PROVENANCE.md`.
362  pub source_map:              bool,
363  /// Document-level `sources` table for the source-map feature: ordered
364  /// list of source files seen, index = the integer `tag` emitted in
365  /// `data-sourcepos` (Source-Map-v3 `sources` style — never an inlined
366  /// path). Populated lazily via `source_tag()` only when `source_map` is
367  /// on. See `docs/performance/SOURCE_PROVENANCE.md` §0.1.
368  pub source_table:            Vec<SymStr>,
369  /// Read-log of every *named* source opened through `Mouth::create`
370  /// (file paths and cached-content names; literal/anonymous mouths are
371  /// not named, so not recorded). Distinct from `source_table`, which
372  /// is populated lazily at *document-construction* time and filters to
373  /// user sources — this log is complete and available right after a
374  /// digest, which the LSP server's warm-cache dependency snapshot
375  /// relies on (`lsp_server/overlay.rs::warmup_dep_snapshot`).
376  pub opened_sources:          HashSet<SymStr>,
377  // TODO: We can make this a Vec<BindingDispatcher> if we want to accumulate more definitions
378  /// The installed binding-resolution chain — reports the source path it loaded
379  /// from (for a `.rhai` file binding), or `None` for a compiled-in binding.
380  pub bindings_dispatch:       Option<ResolvingBindingDispatcher>,
381  /// Auxiliary convenience -- extra dispatch
382  pub extra_bindings_dispatch: Option<BindingDispatcher>,
383  /// All `(name, ext)` pairs for compile-time bindings the dispatchers can
384  /// load, stacked one slice per registered dispatcher. Populated at
385  /// startup by each binding crate via `add_binding_names`, so both
386  /// `latexml_package` and `latexml_contrib` contribute their classes/
387  /// styles/defs/pools to the fallback pool. Consumed by:
388  /// - `find_file(notex=true)` to resolve compile-time bindings without touching the filesystem.
389  /// - `load_class`'s Perl-parity prefix-match fallback (Package.pm L2702-2706) via the
390  ///   `get_class_binding_names()` filtered view.
391  pub binding_names:           Vec<&'static [(&'static str, &'static str)]>,
392  /// Perl: LABEL_MAPPING_HOOK — closure mapping (label, counter, norefnum) -> (refnum, id)
393  pub label_mapping_hook:      Option<LabelMappingHook>,
394  /// The names whose current meaning a fallback class installed (`DefinitionOrigin::Fallback`, OmniBus), a kernel stub
395  /// or a borrow, with that origin, until something else assigns them: a document's `\newcommand` may define them
396  /// (`is_fallback_meaning`, `fallback_meaning_origin`).
397  pub fallback_meanings: rustc_hash::FxHashMap<SymStr, crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin>,
398  /// The names assigned while a provide-only load lives ([`ProvideOnlyGuard`](crate::definition::origin::ProvideOnlyGuard)):
399  /// the load's own definitions, which it may define again (`provide_only_declines`).
400  pub provide_only_assigned:   rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
401  /// The files first marked loaded while a provide-only load lives: loaded partly (their definitions of names defined
402  /// before were declined), so the guard unmarks them when it ends and a later request loads them whole
403  /// (`end_provide_only`).
404  pub provide_only_loaded:     Vec<String>,
405  /// The files first marked loaded while a binding borrows a package (`DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed`,
406  /// `binding::content::require_package_borrowed`): the borrowed package and what it loads (amsthm's amsgen), which the
407  /// borrow unmarks again, as LaTeX never loaded them.
408  pub borrowed_loaded:         Vec<String>,
409  /// The paper's copies of author files read after their stand-in bindings, by number less one
410  /// ([`crate::definition::origin::copy_provides`]; [`note_copy_provides_assignment`]).
411  pub copy_provides_copies:    Vec<CopyProvidesRecord>,
412  /// The frames of the stand-in copies' provides-mode in force, innermost last
413  /// ([`enter_copy_provides_frame`]).
414  pub copy_provides_frames:    Vec<CopyProvidesFrame>,
415  /// The names assigned while a stand-in binding loaded ([`crate::definition::origin::StandInLoadGuard`]): its own, which
416  /// the paper's copy read after it only provides ([`note_copy_provides_assignment`]).
417  pub stand_in_meanings:       rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
418  /// The names whose meaning a class the stand-in binding loads assigned last
419  /// ([`crate::definition::origin::stand_in_base_class`]): its base class's, whose text defaults are not the binding's
420  /// (`unowned_text_default`).
421  pub stand_in_base_defaults:  rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
422  /// The names each stand-in binding loading now has assigned in its own code, innermost last ([`begin_stand_in_load`]).
423  pub stand_in_loads:          Vec<Vec<SymStr>>,
424  /// The names the stand-in binding loaded last assigned in its own code ([`end_stand_in_load`]): the public ones are
425  /// the command set the paper's copy must define to be that binding's file (`binding::content` `read_stand_in_copy`).
426  pub stand_in_last_load:      Vec<SymStr>,
427  /// The files each stand-in binding loading now loads from its own code (its `LoadClass!`/`RequirePackage!`),
428  /// innermost last ([`note_stand_in_file`]).
429  pub stand_in_load_files:     Vec<Vec<String>>,
430  /// The files the stand-in binding loaded last loaded from its own code ([`end_stand_in_load`]): the files it is
431  /// built on, which the paper's copy may declare itself (`binding::content` `copy_is_the_stood_in_file`).
432  pub stand_in_last_files:     Vec<String>,
433  /// The names LaTeXML's own code (`Unknown`, `Pool`, `Binding` origins) `\let`, recorded as it makes them
434  /// ([`let_i`]): a `\let` copies another name's definition, so the name a binding gave it is no longer on the object
435  /// (`owned_by_the_semantic_layer`; aas_support_sty.rs's `\let\subjectheadings\keywords`).
436  pub binding_lets:            rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
437  /// The frontmatter setters whose calls are observed ([`crate::definition::origin::watch_setter_calls`]).
438  pub watched_setters:         rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
439  /// Which names [`watch_meanings`] watches; `None` once one has changed, or with no watch.
440  meaning_watch:               Option<MeaningWatch>,
441  /// The first of [`Self::meaning_watch`]'s names to change.
442  watched_meaning_changed:     Option<SymStr>,
443  /// Each class loaded, with the names its load frame assigned and the classes loaded inside it: what that class
444  /// defines, as its real file does ([`ClassLoadFrame`], `copy_base_class_defines`, `class_layer_definition`).
445  pub class_loads:             Vec<ClassLoadRecord>,
446  /// The class loads open now, as indices into [`State::class_loads`], innermost last ([`ClassLoadFrame`]).
447  pub class_load_frames:       Vec<usize>,
448  /// The stand-in copies whose own `\LoadClass` is open now, innermost last, each with the classes the class load
449  /// open when it began had loaded: a class loaded inside it is a base class of that copy too ([`ClassLoadFrame`]; the
450  /// reviewer's probe nc_chain, `\LoadClass{mybase}` over article), and a copy's first entry holds the classes its
451  /// binding's load loaded ([`open_class_load_classes`]).
452  pub copy_class_loads:        Vec<(u32, Vec<String>)>,
453  /// The open assignment journal, if any ([`begin_assignment_journal`]).
454  journal:                     Option<AssignmentJournal>,
455  /// The open meaning recorders, innermost last ([`begin_meaning_recorder`]): each the meanings assigned since it
456  /// began, with the value each had then.
457  meaning_recorders:           Vec<rustc_hash::FxHashMap<SymStr, Option<Stored>>>,
458}

460/// One class loaded ([`ClassLoadFrame`]): its file name (`article.cls`), the names its load frame assigned a macro (the
461/// macro itself, to tell it is still the meaning: compared by [`Rc::ptr_eq`]) or had a definition refused by a lock
462/// (`None`), and the classes loaded while its frame was open, innermost included (a binding's `LoadClass!` of its base
463/// class: ceurart_cls.rs → OmniBus → article).
464#[derive(Debug, Default)]
465pub struct ClassLoadRecord {
466  pub file:    String,
467  pub names:   rustc_hash::FxHashMap<SymStr, Option<Rc<Expandable>>>,
468  pub classes: Vec<String>,
469}
470
471/// What [`note_copy_provides_assignment`] keeps for one stand-in's copy: the copy's file, its own definitions of names
472/// the binding owns (in force only inside its code), and the names and deferring seams logged.
473#[derive(Debug, Default)]
474pub struct CopyProvidesRecord {
475  pub file:          String,
476  /// The stand-in binding the copy is read after (`binding::content::read_stand_in_copy`): a package that binding's
477  /// load loaded first is the copy's own load (`<file>:loaded_by_stand_in`).
478  pub binding:       String,
479  pub overlay:       rustc_hash::FxHashMap<SymStr, Stored>,
480  pub declined:      rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
481  /// The meaning each declined name took back when the copy's frame ended ([`leave_copy_provides_frame`]): the one its
482  /// overlay stands beside, while still in force ([`copy_kept_meaning`]).
483  pub restored:      rustc_hash::FxHashMap<SymStr, Stored>,
484  pub deferred:      rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
485  /// The copy's own `\maketitle`/`\@maketitle` bodies, refused or overlaid as they may be
486  /// ([`copy_provides_title_pages`]).
487  pub title_page:    Vec<Tokens>,
488  /// The classes the copy's own code loaded (`\LoadClass`, and the classes loaded inside it: [`ClassLoadFrame`]): the
489  /// real class has the names they define ([`copy_provides_text_default_clashes`]).
490  pub loads_classes: Vec<String>,
491  /// Whether the copy's `\Provides` line routed its request to the binding (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 12):
492  /// the declared text names it defines stand (rule 14, [`routed_copy_text_name_yields`]).
493  pub routed:        bool,
494}
495
496/// One frame of a stand-in copy's provides-mode ([`enter_copy_provides_frame`]): the copy (`0`: a suspension), whether
497/// it repeats the frame in force (a hook chunk wrapped twice), and the names whose meanings its code replaced, with
498/// the meanings kept aside.
499#[derive(Debug, Default)]
500pub struct CopyProvidesFrame {
501  pub id:        u32,
502  pub repeat:    bool,
503  pub kept:      Vec<(SymStr, Stored)>,
504  pub kept_keys: rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
505  /// The other names the copy's code assigned in this frame, which may be `\let` to its own version of a kept name
506  /// ([`leave_copy_provides_frame`]).
507  pub assigned:  rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr>,
508}
509// SAFETY: `State` holds `Rc`/`RefCell`/`libxml::tree::Node` (!Send). Marked
510// Send so callers can build it on one thread and then transition to another
511// thread before any use. After first use, State MUST NOT cross thread
512// boundaries (all `use_*_state()` helpers use a `#[thread_local]` switcher).
513// Violating this contract would race libxml2's reference counts → UAF/UB.
514// State is deliberately NOT Sync: no two threads may alias the same State.
515unsafe impl Send for State {}
516
517impl Default for State {
518  fn default() -> Self {
519    let top_frame = UndoFrame {
520      locked: true,
521      ..UndoFrame::default()
522    };
523    let mut undo_vdq = VecDeque::new();
524    undo_vdq.push_front(top_frame);
525
526    State {
527      // Tables — pre-size the two largest to absorb dump load. The
528      // `meaning` table receives 109,863 entries from latex.dump
529      // alone, so without pre-sizing it doubles 5+ times during
530      // dump load (each rehash is O(N)). `value` receives several
531      // thousand register/state-key entries through the lifecycle.
532      // Effective capacity (FxHashMap, 0.875 load factor): 131072 → ~115k.
533      value:                   HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(8_192, Default::default()),
534      meaning:                 HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(131_072, Default::default()),
535      stash:                   HashMap::default(),
536      stash_active:            HashMap::default(),
537      // Char-keyed tables: ASCII alphabet + a smattering of high-codepoint
538      // entries get installed (textcomp + ts1enc.dfu populate ~200-300
539      // entries each). Pre-size to 512 to skip the 8→16→…→256→512
540      // doubling chain on startup.
541      catcode:                 HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(512, Default::default()),
542      mathcode:                HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(512, Default::default()),
543      sfcode:                  HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(512, Default::default()),
544      lccode:                  HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(512, Default::default()),
545      uccode:                  HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(512, Default::default()),
546      delcode:                 HashMap::with_capacity_and_hasher(512, Default::default()),
547      // Table bookkeeping
548      undo:                    undo_vdq,
549      // stateful runtime - data structures
550      prefixes:                HashMap::default(),
551      tag_properties:          HashMap::default(),
552      indirect_model:          None,
553      pending_resources:       Vec::new(),
554      // stateful runtime - simple fields
555      verbosity:               0,
556      input_encoding:          None,
557      // strict: false,
558      // include_comments: true,
559      search_paths:            VecDeque::new(),
560      graphics_paths:          VecDeque::new(),
561      // include_styles: false,
562      nomathparse:             false,
563      if_count:                0,
564      if_stack:                Vec::new(),
565      if_limit:                0,
566      absorb_count:            0,
567      absorb_limit:            0,
568      source_map:              false,
569      source_table:            Vec::new(),
570      opened_sources:          HashSet::default(),
571      bindings_dispatch:       None,
572      extra_bindings_dispatch: None,
573      binding_names:           Vec::new(),
574      label_mapping_hook:      None,
575      fallback_meanings:       rustc_hash::FxHashMap::default(),
576      provide_only_assigned:   rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default(),
577      provide_only_loaded:     Vec::new(),
578      borrowed_loaded:         Vec::new(),
579      copy_provides_copies:    Vec::new(),
580      copy_provides_frames:    Vec::new(),
581      stand_in_meanings:       rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default(),
582      stand_in_base_defaults:  rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default(),
583      stand_in_loads:          Vec::new(),
584      stand_in_last_load:      Vec::new(),
585      stand_in_load_files:     Vec::new(),
586      stand_in_last_files:     Vec::new(),
587      binding_lets:            rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default(),
588      watched_setters:         rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default(),
589      meaning_watch:           None,
590      watched_meaning_changed: None,
591      class_loads:             Vec::new(),
592      class_load_frames:       Vec::new(),
593      copy_class_loads:        Vec::new(),
594      journal:                 None,
595      meaning_recorders:       Vec::new(),
596    }
597  }
598}
599
600#[thread_local]
601static STD_STATE: Lazy<RefCell<State>> = Lazy::new(|| {
602  RefCell::new(State::new(StateOptions {
603    catcodes: Some(Catcodes::Standard),
604    ..StateOptions::default()
605  }))
606});
607#[thread_local]
608static STY_STATE: Lazy<RefCell<State>> = Lazy::new(|| {
609  RefCell::new(State::new(StateOptions {
610    catcodes: Some(Catcodes::Style),
611    ..StateOptions::default()
612  }))
613});
614#[thread_local]
615static STATE: Lazy<RefCell<State>> = Lazy::new(|| {
616  RefCell::new(State::new(StateOptions {
617    catcodes: Some(Catcodes::Standard),
618    ..StateOptions::default()
619  }))
620});
621
622/// Eagerly initialize this thread's `STD_STATE`/`STY_STATE` catcode-regime
623/// templates. They are accessed mid-conversion on catcode switches
624/// (`\makeatletter`, verbatim, …); each one's `Lazy` initializer runs
625/// `State::new`, which interns via the arena. Forcing them at conversion
626/// entry — AFTER [`arena::force_init`](crate::common::arena::force_init) —
627/// keeps them from initializing re-entrantly mid-expansion, the macOS
628/// `#[thread_local]` hazard behind issue #217. (The active `STATE` is
629/// already forced by `set_state` in `Core::new`.) No behavioral change on
630/// Linux.
631pub(crate) fn force_init() {
632  Lazy::force(&STD_STATE);
633  Lazy::force(&STY_STATE);
634}
635
636macro_rules! state {
637  () => {
638    (*STATE).borrow()
639  };
640}
641macro_rules! state_mut {
642  () => {
643    (*STATE).borrow_mut()
644  };
645}
646macro_rules! sty_state_mut {
647  () => {
648    (*STY_STATE).borrow_mut()
649  };
650}
651macro_rules! std_state_mut {
652  () => {
653    (*STD_STATE).borrow_mut()
654  };
655}
656
657/// state fields allowed for customization during construction
658#[derive(Default)]
659pub struct StateOptions {
660  pub model:            Option<Model>,
661  pub verbosity:        Option<i32>,
662  pub strict:           Option<bool>,
663  pub include_comments: Option<bool>,
664  pub include_styles:   Option<bool>,
665  pub nomathparse:      Option<bool>,
666  pub source_map:       Option<bool>,
667  pub documentid:       Option<String>,
668  pub search_paths:     Option<Vec<String>>,
669  pub graphics_paths:   Option<Vec<String>>,
670  pub catcodes:         Option<Catcodes>,
671  pub input_encoding:   Option<String>,
672}
673
674// Public interface: package-access methods, for an implied thread-local singleton STATE
675
676// Private interface: struct-access methods, for a concrete piece of State data
677
678impl State {
679  pub fn new(options: StateOptions) -> Self {
680    use crate::token::Catcode::*;
681
682    // Setup default catcodes.
683    let catcode_profile = match options.catcodes {
684      None => Catcodes::Standard,
685      Some(cp) => cp,
686    };
687
688    let mut catcodes: HashMap<char, Catcode> = HashMap::default();
689    match catcode_profile {
690      Catcodes::Standard | Catcodes::Style => {
691        catcodes.insert('\\', ESCAPE);
692        catcodes.insert('{', BEGIN);
693        catcodes.insert('}', END);
694        catcodes.insert('$', MATH);
695        catcodes.insert('&', ALIGN);
696        catcodes.insert('\r', EOL);
697        catcodes.insert('#', PARAM);
698        catcodes.insert('^', SUPER);
699        catcodes.insert('_', SUB);
700        catcodes.insert(' ', SPACE);
701        catcodes.insert('\t', SPACE);
702        catcodes.insert('%', COMMENT);
703        catcodes.insert('~', ACTIVE);
704        // NUL (`\^^@`, U+0000): Perl LaTeXML's default is catcode 12 (OTHER),
705        // NOT the TeXbook's 9 (IGNORE). We follow Perl (ground truth) so that
706        // `\^^@`/`` `^^@ `` reads code 0 (TeXbook 9 would *drop* the NUL token,
707        // making `` `^^@ `` skip to the next token — `\relax` etc. — and return
708        // a bogus code; xint's `\romannumeral`&&@` expansion idiom needs 0).
709        // Real-world bbl files (e.g. astro-ph0004127's spie4012-01a.bbl) carry
710        // stray NULs from BibTeX `\"u`-mangling; as OTHER they become harmless
711        // literal chars (stripped at XML serialization), matching Perl —
712        // crucially NOT ESCAPE, so no bogus `\uninger`-style CS forms. An
713        // explicit `\catcode`^^Q=9` (user/package) is still honored; only the
714        // *default* changes.
715        catcodes.insert('\0', OTHER);
716        catcodes.insert('\u{000c}', ACTIVE);
717        for c in "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ".chars() {
718          catcodes.insert(c, LETTER);
719        }
720      },
721      Catcodes::None => {},
722    }
723    if catcode_profile == Catcodes::Style {
724      catcodes.insert('@', LETTER);
725    }
726
727    let mut value_table = HashMap::default();
728    let mut specials_vdq = VecDeque::new();
729    specials_vdq.push_front(Stored::Chars(Box::new([
730      '^', '_', '~', '&', '$', '#', '\'',
731    ])));
732    value_table.insert(arena::pin_static("SPECIALS"), specials_vdq);
733
734    let mut catcodes_typed: Table = HashMap::default();
735    for (k, v) in catcodes {
736      let mut vdq = VecDeque::new();
737      vdq.push_front(Stored::Catcode(v));
738      catcodes_typed.insert(arena::pin_char(k), vdq);
739    }
740
741    // Basic defaults
742    if let Some(model) = options.model {
743      model::set_model(model);
744    };
745    let verbosity = options.verbosity.unwrap_or(0);
746    // let strict = options.strict.unwrap_or(false);
747    // INCLUDE_COMMENTS: Perl defaults to true (Core.pm L143).
748    // T_COMMENT tokens are now properly converted to XML comment nodes
749    // via Document::insert_comment using raw libxml2 FFI.
750    // Note: Only set when explicitly specified, because STY_STATE/STD_STATE
751    // use default options and state rotation (swap) would overwrite the
752    // main state's INCLUDE_COMMENTS value.
753    let include_comments = options.include_comments;
754    // let include_styles = options.include_styles.unwrap_or(false);
755    let nomathparse = options.nomathparse.unwrap_or(false);
756    let source_map = options.source_map.unwrap_or(false);
757
758    // Perl Core.pm L50-52: `SEARCHPATHS => [map { pathname_absolute(...) } '.',
759    // @searchpaths]` — the current working directory is ALWAYS searched, and
760    // FIRST (it takes precedence over the `--path` dirs). A `--path` entry
761    // ending in `//` is the kpsewhich recursive-search marker; preserve it
762    // across canonicalization (strip it, canonicalize the base, re-append) so
763    // `candidate_pathnames` can expand the marker to the whole subtree.
764    let search_paths: VecDeque<String> = std::iter::once(String::from("."))
765      .chain(options.search_paths.into_iter().flatten())
766      .map(|p| match p.strip_suffix("//") {
767        Some(base) => format!("{}//", pathname::absolute(&pathname::canonical(base))),
768        None => pathname::absolute(&pathname::canonical(&p)),
769      })
770      .collect();
771    let graphics_paths = match options.graphics_paths {
772      None => VecDeque::new(),
773      Some(paths) => paths
774        .iter()
775        .map(|p| pathname::absolute(&pathname::canonical(p)))
776        .collect(),
777    };
778
779    let mut state = State {
780      value: value_table,
781      catcode: catcodes_typed,
782      verbosity,
783      // strict,
784      // include_comments,
785      search_paths,
786      graphics_paths,
787      // include_styles,
788      input_encoding: options.input_encoding,
789      nomathparse,
790      source_map,
791      ..State::default()
792    };
793    // INITEX-equivalent defaults — mirror Perl `State.pm:128-137`.
794    // Sets letter/digit mathcodes (class 7, family 1 for letters / 0 for digits),
795    // upper/lowercase mappings, and sfcode=999 for uppercase letters. Without
796    // these, dump-load path leaves letter mathcodes unset (plain.dump.txt only
797    // captures the 57 plain.tex OVERRIDES), so `\cal abc` math falls through
798    // the text path and loses meaning/role attributes. NODUMP path used to set
799    // these via plain_base.rs L17-41 only — not Perl-faithful since INITEX
800    // owns these (TeXbook ch.17 p309). Setting them here makes both paths
801    // consistent and matches Perl's State::new behaviour.
802    for c in b'0'..=b'9' {
803      state.assign_internal(
804        TableName::Mathcode,
805        arena::pin_char(c as char),
806        Stored::Charcode(0x7000 + c as u16),
807        None,
808      );
809    }
810    for c in b'a'..=b'z' {
811      let big = c - 32;
812      state.assign_internal(
813        TableName::Mathcode,
814        arena::pin_char(c as char),
815        Stored::Charcode(0x7100 + c as u16),
816        None,
817      );
818      state.assign_internal(
819        TableName::Mathcode,
820        arena::pin_char(big as char),
821        Stored::Charcode(0x7100 + big as u16),
822        None,
823      );
824      state.assign_internal(
825        TableName::Uccode,
826        arena::pin_char(c as char),
827        Stored::Charcode(big as u16),
828        None,
829      );
830      state.assign_internal(
831        TableName::Lccode,
832        arena::pin_char(big as char),
833        Stored::Charcode(c as u16),
834        None,
835      );
836      state.assign_internal(
837        TableName::Sfcode,
838        arena::pin_char(big as char),
839        Stored::Charcode(999),
840        None,
841      );
842    }
843    // TODO: should these be *fields* in state or really as in Perl - globally assigned values?
844    state.assign_value(
845      "DOCUMENTID",
846      options.documentid.unwrap_or_default(),
847      Some(Scope::Global),
848    );
849    // Perl Core.pm L143: assignValue(INCLUDE_COMMENTS => ..., 'global')
850    // Only set when explicitly specified (not for STY_STATE/STD_STATE defaults)
851    if let Some(ic) = include_comments {
852      state.assign_value("INCLUDE_COMMENTS", ic, Some(Scope::Global));
853    }
854    // Perl Core.pm L47: INCLUDE_PATH_PIS (default true) — emit searchpath PIs
855    state.assign_value("INCLUDE_PATH_PIS", true, Some(Scope::Global));
856    // Perl Core.pm L43: STRICT (default false)
857    if let Some(strict) = options.strict {
858      state.assign_value("STRICT", strict, Some(Scope::Global));
859    }
860    // Perl Core.pm L55-57: `--includestyles` fans out to BOTH INCLUDE_STYLES
861    // and INCLUDE_CLASSES (both default 0), so it raw-loads .sty packages AND
862    // .cls classes from the search path. Faithful to Perl — its own
863    // `# … accept both classes and styles?` hedge notwithstanding, the code
864    // sets both, and INCLUDE_CLASSES is what unlocks raw .cls in LoadClass.
865    if let Some(include_styles) = options.include_styles {
866      state.assign_value("INCLUDE_STYLES", include_styles, Some(Scope::Global));
867      state.assign_value("INCLUDE_CLASSES", include_styles, Some(Scope::Global));
868    }
869    // Perl Core.pm L62: NOMATHPARSE
870    state.assign_value("NOMATHPARSE", nomathparse, Some(Scope::Global));
871    // Perl Core.pm L61: PERL_INPUT_ENCODING (default utf-8)
872    let enc = state.input_encoding.as_deref().unwrap_or("utf-8");
873    state.assign_value(
874      "PERL_INPUT_ENCODING",
875      Stored::String(arena::pin(enc)),
876      Some(Scope::Global),
877    );
878
879    // Perl Core.pm L53: $state->assignValue(GRAPHICSPATHS => [map {…} @{$opts{graphicspaths}}])
880    // Mirror with a VecDequeStored of String entries; subsequent push/unshift
881    // operations in `\graphicspath`, `\svgpath`, and Core.pm-equivalent source
882    // directory prepends will append/prepend to this same list.
883    if !state.graphics_paths.is_empty() {
884      let vdq: VecDeque<Stored> = state
885        .graphics_paths
886        .iter()
887        .map(|p| Stored::String(arena::pin(p)))
888        .collect();
889      state.assign_internal(
890        TableName::Value,
891        arena::pin("GRAPHICSPATHS"),
892        Stored::VecDequeStored(vdq),
893        Some(Scope::Global),
894      );
895    }
896
897    // SEARCHPATHS mirrors GRAPHICSPATHS: a group-scoped value list (Perl
898    // `AssignValue(SEARCHPATHS…)`, local-by-default) rather than a plain field,
899    // so an `\import`/`\subimport` group reverts its change at `}` while a
900    // package's global add persists (#561). Seeded Global from the `search_paths`
901    // field (`.` + the `--path` dirs); `get_search_paths` reads the value table.
902    {
903      let vdq: VecDeque<Stored> = state
904        .search_paths
905        .iter()
906        .map(|p| Stored::String(arena::pin(p)))
907        .collect();
908      state.assign_internal(
909        TableName::Value,
910        arena::pin("SEARCHPATHS"),
911        Stored::VecDequeStored(vdq),
912        Some(Scope::Global),
913      );
914    }
915
916    state
917  }
918
919  /// borrow/get the named table
920  pub fn table(&self, name: TableName) -> &Table {
921    use self::TableName::*;
922    match name {
923      Meaning => &self.meaning,
924      Value => &self.value,
925      Catcode => &self.catcode,
926      Mathcode => &self.mathcode,
927      Sfcode => &self.sfcode,
928      Lccode => &self.lccode,
929      Uccode => &self.uccode,
930      Delcode => &self.delcode,
931      Stash => &self.stash,
932      StashActive => &self.stash_active,
933    }
934  }
935  /// mutably borrow/get the named table
936  pub fn table_mut(&mut self, name: TableName) -> &mut Table {
937    use self::TableName::*;
938    match name {
939      Meaning => &mut self.meaning,
940      Value => &mut self.value,
941      Catcode => &mut self.catcode,
942      Mathcode => &mut self.mathcode,
943      Sfcode => &mut self.sfcode,
944      Lccode => &mut self.lccode,
945      Uccode => &mut self.uccode,
946      Delcode => &mut self.delcode,
947      Stash => &mut self.stash,
948      StashActive => &mut self.stash_active,
949    }
950  }
951
952  /// Perl DumpFile equivalent: Take a snapshot of the current state.
953  /// Returns a HashMap mapping (table_name, key) → Stored value.
954  /// Only captures the front (current) value of each key.
955  /// Used before processing latex.ltx to diff what changed.
956  pub fn snapshot(&self) -> rustc_hash::FxHashMap<(TableName, SymStr), Stored> {
957    let tables = [
958      TableName::Value,
959      TableName::Meaning,
960      TableName::Catcode,
961      TableName::Mathcode,
962      TableName::Sfcode,
963      TableName::Lccode,
964      TableName::Uccode,
965      TableName::Delcode,
966    ];
967    let mut snap = rustc_hash::FxHashMap::default();
968    for &tname in &tables {
969      let table = self.table(tname);
970      for (key, values) in table {
971        if let Some(front) = values.front() {
972          let value = match front {
973            // A font's parameter array is written in place: the snapshot
974            // keeps a copy of its own, or no later change would show.
975            Stored::FontDimens(params) => {
976              Stored::FontDimens(Rc::new(RefCell::new(params.borrow().clone())))
977            },
978            value => value.clone(),
979          };
980          snap.insert((tname, *key), value);
981        }
982      }
983    }
984    snap
985  }
986
987  /// Perl DumpFile equivalent: Compute the diff between current state and a snapshot.
988  /// Returns only entries that CHANGED since the snapshot was taken.
989  /// Skips entries that contain closures (Primitive, Constructor, Conditional)
990  /// since those can't be serialized — they come from Rust engine code.
991  pub fn diff_from_snapshot(
992    &self,
993    snap: &rustc_hash::FxHashMap<(TableName, SymStr), Stored>,
994  ) -> Vec<(TableName, SymStr, Stored)> {
995    let tables = [
996      TableName::Value,
997      TableName::Meaning,
998      TableName::Catcode,
999      TableName::Mathcode,
1000      TableName::Sfcode,
1001      TableName::Lccode,
1002      TableName::Uccode,
1003      TableName::Delcode,
1004    ];
1005    let mut diff = Vec::new();
1006    for &tname in &tables {
1007      let table = self.table(tname);
1008      for (key, values) in table {
1009        if let Some(current) = values.front() {
1010          let key_pair = (tname, *key);
1011          let changed = match snap.get(&key_pair) {
1012            None => true, // new entry
1013            Some(prev) => {
1014              // Perl diffs the SERIALIZED dump form against the bootstrap
1015              // snapshot (TeX_Job.pool.ltxml:126-200: a key whose `Dump`
1016              // string equals the `<fmt>_bootstrap` baseline is omitted), so
1017              // a raw redefinition to an identical body — latex.ltx:18386
1018              // `\gdef\@currnamestack{}` over the bootstrap's empty macro —
1019              // is NOT dumped. A `Debug` comparison also sees definition
1020              // metadata (the `:redefined@nargs` lock records of batch 56z)
1021              // and reported such entries as changed; the dumped empty
1022              // `\@currnamestack` then clobbered the live file stack of the
1023              // preload being read (a4wide 3 → 35 errors, cortex 2026-09-17).
1024              match (
1025                crate::dump_writer::serialize_stored(current),
1026                crate::dump_writer::serialize_stored(prev),
1027              ) {
1028                (Some(a), Some(b)) => a != b,
1029                _ => format!("{:?}", current) != format!("{:?}", prev),
1030              }
1031            },
1032          };
1033          if changed && is_serializable(current) {
1034            diff.push((tname, *key, current.clone()));
1035          }
1036        }
1037      }
1038    }
1039    diff
1040  }
1041
1042  // needed for assign_internal, so keeping it as a object method
1043  /// gets the current value of a named prefix
1044  pub fn get_prefix(&self, prefix: &str) -> bool { self.get_prefix_sym(arena::pin(prefix)) }
1045
1046  /// `get_prefix` variant with a pre-pinned SymStr (see `crate::pin!`) —
1047  /// `assign_internal` probes the `global` prefix on every unscoped
1048  /// assignment, so the per-call `arena::pin` there was pure overhead.
1049  pub fn get_prefix_sym(&self, prefix: SymStr) -> bool {
1050    match self.prefixes.get(&prefix) {
1051      Some(b) => *b,
1052      _ => false,
1053    }
1054  }
1055
1056  /// Record `key`'s bindings in the open assignment journal before its first assignment since the journal began, and
1057  /// end the watch held ([`watch_state`]).
1058  #[inline]
1059  fn journal_touch(&mut self, table_name: TableName, key: SymStr) {
1060    if table_name == TableName::Meaning && !self.meaning_recorders.is_empty() {
1061      self.record_meaning(key);
1062    }
1063    if WRITES_WATCHED.get() {
1064      self.journal_save(table_name, key);
1065    }
1066  }
1067
1068  /// [`Self::journal_touch`], for an assignment whose scope is known: `global`, whether it is global, goes to the meaning
1069  /// log ([`begin_meaning_log`]), when a field's token is being invoked. The log lives on the journal, a rehearsal's, so an
1070  /// assignment outside one pays nothing for it: the test sits past the guard's.
1071  #[inline]
1072  fn journal_touch_assignment(&mut self, table_name: TableName, key: SymStr, global: bool) {
1073    if table_name == TableName::Meaning && !self.meaning_recorders.is_empty() {
1074      self.record_meaning(key);
1075    }
1076    if WRITES_WATCHED.get() {
1077      if table_name == TableName::Meaning
1078        && JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.get()
1079        && let Some(log) = self
1080          .journal
1081          .as_mut()
1082          .and_then(|journal| journal.meaning_log.as_mut())
1083        && let Some(by) = get_current_token().filter(|t| t.prov != 0)
1084      {
1085        log.push(MeaningAssigned { key, global, by });
1086      }
1087      self.journal_save(table_name, key);
1088    }
1089  }
1090
1091  /// Save `key`'s bindings in the live journal, unless they are already, and end the watch held ([`watch_state`]): out
1092  /// of line, a journal being open only for a rehearsal or a replay, a watch held only for a title-page reading.
1093  #[cold]
1094  #[inline(never)]
1095  fn journal_save(&mut self, table_name: TableName, key: SymStr) {
1096    note_state_change(table_name, key);
1097    // (a journal whose guard is gone is stale: nothing records into it until the next begin discards it)
1098    if !JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.get() {
1099      return;
1100    }
1101    let Some(journal) = self.journal.as_ref() else {
1102      return;
1103    };
1104    if journal.saved.contains_key(&(table_name, key)) {
1105      return;
1106    }
1107    let mut values = self.table(table_name).get(&key).cloned();
1108    // (a font's parameter array is written in place, `set_font_parameter`: saved as a copy of its own)
1109    if let Some(values) = values.as_mut().filter(|_| table_name == TableName::Value) {
1110      for value in values {
1111        if let Stored::FontDimens(params) = value {
1112          let copy = params.borrow().clone();
1113          *value = Stored::FontDimens(Rc::new(RefCell::new(copy)));
1114        }
1115      }
1116    }
1117    let counts: Vec<(u64, usize)> = self
1118      .undo
1119      .iter()
1120      .filter(|frame| journal.frames.contains(&frame.id))
1121      .filter_map(|frame| frame.table(table_name).get(&key).map(|n| (frame.id, *n)))
1122      .collect();
1123    let standing =
1124      (table_name == TableName::Meaning).then(|| self.fallback_meanings.get(&key).copied());
1125    if let Some(journal) = self.journal.as_mut() {
1126      journal.saved.insert((table_name, key), (values, counts));
1127      if let Some(standing) = standing {
1128        journal.fallback.insert(key, standing);
1129      }
1130    }
1131  }
1132
1133  /// Record `key`'s meaning in each open meaning recorder before its first assignment since that recorder began
1134  /// ([`begin_meaning_recorder`]).
1135  #[cold]
1136  fn record_meaning(&mut self, key: SymStr) {
1137    let then = self
1138      .meaning
1139      .get(&key)
1140      .and_then(|values| values.front().cloned());
1141    for recorder in &mut self.meaning_recorders {
1142      recorder.entry(key).or_insert_with(|| then.clone());
1143    }
1144  }
1145
1146  /// The `Scope::Local` binding itself: record one undo entry in the top
1147  /// frame (unless the key is already bound there — then replace) and push the
1148  /// value. Reached by every local assignment, and DIRECTLY by the save-frame
1149  /// bookkeeping ([`assign_frame_value_sym`]): tex.web §1214 lets
1150  /// `\globaldefs` (and the `\global` prefix, §1211) globalize an
1151  /// ASSIGNMENT made by `prefixed_command`, never the save stack (§274
1152  /// `new_save_level` / §282 `unsave`), so a group's own records must stay
1153  /// local whatever `\globaldefs` says. Perl (State.pm:144-151) routes its
1154  /// frame bookkeeping through the same override, and under `\globaldefs=1`
1155  /// (msc.sty:2616 `\msc@global@set`, tikzexternalshared, latex.ltx's
1156  /// `\math@fonts` block) a closed `{` group kept reporting itself as the
1157  /// current frame — the D12 "close non-boxing group" cascades.
1158  pub(crate) fn assign_local_unconditional(
1159    &mut self,
1160    table_name: TableName,
1161    key: SymStr,
1162    value: Stored,
1163  ) {
1164    self.journal_touch(table_name, key);
1165    // Again, split the logic as 1) bookkeeping in undo, then 2) operations in state_tables
1166    let mut is_replace = false;
1167    // 1. Undo mutable logic
1168    if let Some(current_frame) = self.undo.front_mut() {
1169      let current_frame_table = current_frame.table_mut(table_name);
1170
1171      is_replace = current_frame_table.get(&key).unwrap_or(&0) > &0;
1172      if is_replace { // If the value was previously assigned in this frame
1173        // we do this in 2.1, then proceed to 2.2
1174      } else {
1175        // Otherwise, push new value & set 1 to be undone
1176        current_frame_table.insert(key, 1);
1177        //  And push new binding in 2.2
1178      }
1179    }
1180    // 2. state_table mutable logic
1181    let state_table = self.table_mut(table_name);
1182    let defs = state_table.entry(key).or_default();
1183    if is_replace {
1184      // 2.1. Replace the value, i.e. remove existing one
1185      defs.pop_front();
1186    }
1187    // 2.2 Add new value
1188    defs.push_front(value);
1189  }
1190
1191  /// [`watch_meanings`]' look at an assignment of `key`'s meaning. A scope move or a copy onto itself is no change
1192  /// (`hoist_top_frame_delta`, under the lazy LaTeX.pool load); nor is a first definition: the name undefined, or, for a
1193  /// name [`WatchedName::RelaxIsUnset`], the `\relax` a `\csname` made it (`\exp_args:NNc`).
1194  #[inline]
1195  fn watch_meaning(&mut self, watched: fn(&str) -> WatchedName, key: SymStr, value: &Stored) {
1196    let kind = arena::with(key, watched);
1197    if kind != WatchedName::No
1198      && let Some(standing) = self.meaning.get(&key).and_then(|stack| stack.front())
1199      && !same_meaning(standing, value)
1200      && !(kind == WatchedName::RelaxIsUnset
1201        && self
1202          .meaning
1203          .get(&pin!("\\relax"))
1204          .and_then(|stack| stack.front())
1205          .is_some_and(|relax| same_meaning(standing, relax)))
1206    {
1207      self.meaning_watch = None;
1208      self.watched_meaning_changed = Some(key);
1209    }
1210  }
1211
1212  pub(crate) fn assign_internal(
1213    &mut self,
1214    table_name: TableName,
1215    key: SymStr,
1216    value: Stored,
1217    mut scope_opt: Option<Scope>,
1218  ) {
1219    // (a meaning a fallback class, a kernel stub or a borrowed package installs, until anything else assigns the name:
1220    // `fallback_meanings`; an open journal records the standing first)
1221    if table_name == TableName::Meaning {
1222      self.journal_touch(table_name, key);
1223      let origin = crate::definition::origin::current_origin();
1224      // (not LaTeXML's own code, which every conversion's preload runs and which assigns no such name)
1225      if let Some(watched) = self.meaning_watch
1226        && matches!(
1227          origin,
1228          crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::File
1229            | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Document
1230            | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Format
1231        )
1232        && arena::first_bytes(key).is_some_and(|[_, second, third]| watched.may_take(second, third))
1233      {
1234        self.watch_meaning(watched.select, key, &value);
1235      }
1236      if matches!(
1237        origin,
1238        crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Fallback
1239          | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Stub
1240          | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed
1241      ) {
1242        self.fallback_meanings.insert(key, origin);
1243      } else if !self.fallback_meanings.is_empty() {
1244        self.fallback_meanings.remove(&key);
1245      }
1246      if crate::definition::origin::provide_only() {
1247        self.provide_only_assigned.insert(key);
1248      }
1249      // (a macro a class's load frame defines — its binding's, its raw file's, a package's it loads — not LaTeXML's
1250      // kernel layers: `ClassLoadFrame`)
1251      if let Some(&class) = self.class_load_frames.last()
1252        && matches!(value, Stored::Expandable(_))
1253        && !matches!(
1254          origin,
1255          crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Unknown
1256            | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Plain
1257            | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::LatexDump
1258            | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Pool
1259            | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Format
1260            | crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Document
1261        )
1262        && let Stored::Expandable(defn) = &value
1263        && let Some(record) = self.class_loads.get_mut(class)
1264      {
1265        record.names.insert(key, Some(Rc::clone(defn)));
1266      }
1267      if crate::definition::origin::stand_in_loading() {
1268        self.stand_in_meanings.insert(key);
1269        if crate::definition::origin::stand_in_base_class() {
1270          self.stand_in_base_defaults.insert(key);
1271        } else if !self.stand_in_base_defaults.is_empty() {
1272          self.stand_in_base_defaults.remove(&key);
1273        }
1274        if crate::definition::origin::stand_in_own_code()
1275          && let Some(load) = self.stand_in_loads.last_mut()
1276        {
1277          load.push(key);
1278        }
1279      }
1280    } else if table_name == TableName::Value && matches!(value, Stored::Bool(true)) {
1281      // (`<file>_loaded`, `load_ltxml`: a file loaded under the provide-only guard, or by a borrow)
1282      let provide_only = crate::definition::origin::provide_only();
1283      let borrowed = crate::definition::origin::current_origin()
1284        == crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed;
1285      if provide_only || borrowed {
1286        arena::with(key, |name| {
1287          if let Some(file) = name.strip_suffix("_loaded")
1288            && !file.ends_with(".ltxml")
1289            && !file.ends_with("_raw")
1290          {
1291            for (on, list) in [
1292              (provide_only, &mut self.provide_only_loaded),
1293              (borrowed, &mut self.borrowed_loaded),
1294            ] {
1295              if on && !list.iter().any(|f| f == file) {
1296                list.push(file.to_string());
1297              }
1298            }
1299          }
1300        });
1301      }
1302    }
1303    // hotcode lookupDefinition for \globaldefs,
1304    // since this is called extremely often and should be highly standardized.
1305    // TeX semantics: positive → all assignments global, negative → \global
1306    // ignored, zero → no override. `\globaldefs` is a Number register, so the
1307    // stored variant is `Stored::Number`, NOT `Stored::Int` — Perl's `==`
1308    // coerces both, Rust must unwrap explicitly. Perl `State.pm:144-151` uses
1309    // strict `==1`/`==-1`; we slightly broaden to TeX's sign-based rule
1310    // (matches behavior for the canonical `\globaldefs=1`/`\globaldefs=-1`
1311    // uses while also handling rare `\globaldefs=2` etc).
1312    // `Scope::Named(_)` and `Scope::InPlace` are preserved per Perl's "ONLY
1313    // override global/local/undef" rule (State.pm:146 — `$scope ne 'global' &&
1314    // $scope ne 'local'` short-circuits the override): an in-place mutation of
1315    // the existing binding is NOT re-scoped by `\globaldefs`.
1316    // Without this: pgfplots' `\pgfplots@pop@next@legend`
1317    // (`\def\foo{{\globaldefs=1 \let\x=\relax}}`) silently drops the `\let`
1318    // on group exit, leaving `\pgfplots@curlegend`/`@curplotlist` undefined
1319    // and looping `\pgfplots@createlegend` at the digest wall-clock cap.
1320    let preserve = matches!(scope_opt, Some(Scope::Named(_) | Scope::InPlace));
1321    if !preserve
1322      && let Some(globaldefs) = self.value.get(&pin!("\\globaldefs"))
1323      && let Some(global_value) = globaldefs.front()
1324    {
1325      let int_value: i64 = match *global_value {
1326        Stored::Int(v) => v,
1327        Stored::Number(n) => n.0,
1328        _ => 0,
1329      };
1330      if int_value > 0 {
1331        scope_opt = Some(Scope::Global);
1332      } else if int_value < 0 {
1333        scope_opt = Some(Scope::Local);
1334      }
1335    }
1336    // TRACE: watch for cleanup:w
1337    // regular check, local scope is default, unless a global prefix is set
1338    let mut scope = match scope_opt {
1339      Some(s) => s,
1340      None => {
1341        if self.get_prefix_sym(pin!("global")) {
1342          Scope::Global
1343        } else {
1344          Scope::Local
1345        }
1346      },
1347    };
1348    // A build-time local binding at the build's BASE level is global in
1349    // effect: eager builds after digestion, when no group can pop it. The
1350    // streaming driver interleaves the build with digestion, so the same
1351    // binding would land in a digestion frame and vanish at that group's end
1352    // (`with_build_floor`).
1353    if matches!(scope, Scope::Local) && BUILD_FLOOR.get() == Some(self.undo.len()) {
1354      scope = Scope::Global;
1355    }
1356    self.journal_touch_assignment(table_name, key, matches!(scope, Scope::Global));
1357    match scope {
1358      Scope::Global => {
1359        let mut undo_count = 0;
1360
1361        // Remove bindings made in all frames down-to & including the next lower locked frame
1362        let mut last_frame = None;
1363        for frame in &mut self.undo {
1364          let is_locked = frame.locked;
1365          let frame_table = frame.table_mut(table_name);
1366          if let Some(n) = frame_table.remove(&key) {
1367            undo_count += n;
1368          }
1369          last_frame = Some(frame);
1370          if is_locked {
1371            break;
1372          }
1373        }
1374        // whatever is left -- if anything -- should be bindings below the locked frame.
1375        if let Some(frame) = last_frame {
1376          frame.table_mut(table_name).insert(key, 1); // Note that there's only one
1377          // value in the stack, now
1378        }
1379
1380        // Undo the bindings, if `key` was bound in this frame
1381        let state_table = self.table_mut(table_name);
1382        if let Some(defs) = state_table.get_mut(&key) {
1383          for _ in 1..=undo_count {
1384            defs.pop_front();
1385          }
1386        }
1387
1388        let table_entry = state_table.entry(key).or_default();
1389        table_entry.push_front(value);
1390      },
1391      Scope::Local => self.assign_local_unconditional(table_name, key, value),
1392      Scope::InPlace => {
1393        // Perl `State.pm:175`: replace the front value in the frame it was last
1394        // bound in, adding NO undo entry, so the mutation keeps the binding's
1395        // save-stack level (the `\box` / same-level semantics). If the key was
1396        // never bound, seed it at the locked base frame (Perl's "push globally"
1397        // fallback). Mirrors the Value-table `assign_value_inplace` fast path.
1398        let state_table = self.table_mut(table_name);
1399        if let Some(defs) = state_table.get_mut(&key)
1400          && let Some(front) = defs.front_mut()
1401        {
1402          *front = value;
1403        } else {
1404          state_table.entry(key).or_default().push_front(value);
1405          for frame in &mut self.undo {
1406            if frame.locked {
1407              frame.table_mut(table_name).insert(key, 1);
1408              break;
1409            }
1410          }
1411        }
1412      },
1413      Scope::Named(scope_name) => {
1414        // initialize stash if empty
1415        let needs_init = match self.stash.get(&scope_name) {
1416          None => true,
1417          Some(v) => v.is_empty(),
1418        };
1419        if needs_init {
1420          self.assign_internal(
1421            TableName::Stash,
1422            scope_name,
1423            Stored::Stash(Vec::new()),
1424            Some(Scope::Global),
1425          );
1426        }
1427        self.journal_touch(TableName::Stash, scope_name);
1428        if let Some(Stored::Stash(stash)) =
1429          self.stash.get_mut(&scope_name).as_mut().unwrap().get_mut(0)
1430        {
1431          stash.push((table_name, key, value.clone()));
1432        }
1433        let has_active = match self.stash_active.get(&scope_name) {
1434          None => false,
1435          Some(v) => !v.is_empty(),
1436        };
1437        if has_active {
1438          self.assign_internal(table_name, key, value, Some(Scope::Local));
1439        }
1440      },
1441    }
1442  }
1443
1444  /// assigns a `Stored` value at the given key and scope
1445  pub fn assign_value<T: Into<Stored>, S: Into<Option<Scope>>>(
1446    &mut self,
1447    key: &str,
1448    value: T,
1449    scope: S,
1450  ) {
1451    let value = value.into();
1452    let scope = scope.into();
1453    let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
1454    self.assign_internal(TableName::Value, key_sym, value, scope);
1455  }
1456  //======================================================================
1457  /// fetches a Stored value at the given key, from the Value table
1458  pub fn lookup_value(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&Stored> {
1459    self.lookup_value_sym(arena::pin(key))
1460  }
1461  pub fn lookup_value_sym(&self, key: SymStr) -> Option<&Stored> {
1462    match self.value.get(&key) {
1463      None => None,
1464      Some(vvec) => match vvec.front() {
1465        None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
1466        Some(other) => Some(other),
1467      },
1468    }
1469  }
1470
1471  /// mutably borrows a Stored value at the given key, from the Value table
1472  pub fn lookup_value_mut(&mut self, key: &str) -> Option<&mut Stored> {
1473    let key = arena::pin(key);
1474    self.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key);
1475    match self.value.get_mut(&key) {
1476      None => None,
1477      Some(vvec) => match vvec.front_mut() {
1478        None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
1479        Some(other) => Some(other),
1480      },
1481    }
1482  }
1483  /// like `lookup_value` but only recognizes `Stored::VecDequeStored`
1484  pub fn lookup_vecdeque(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&VecDeque<Stored>> {
1485    match self.lookup_value(key) {
1486      None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
1487      Some(v) => v.into(),
1488    }
1489  }
1490  pub fn lookup_font_info(&self, key: &Token) -> Result<Option<&Stored>> {
1491    let key_str = match lookup_definition(key)? {
1492      Some(defn) => {
1493        s!("fontinfo_{}", defn.get_cs_name())
1494      },
1495      _ => {
1496        s!("fontinfo_{key}")
1497      },
1498    };
1499    Ok(self.lookup_value(&key_str))
1500  }
1501  /// manage a (global) hash of values
1502  pub fn lookup_mapping(&self, map: &str, key: &str) -> Option<&Stored> {
1503    self.lookup_mapping_sym(arena::pin(map), key)
1504  }
1505  pub fn lookup_mapping_sym(&self, map_sym: SymStr, key: &str) -> Option<&Stored> {
1506    match self.value.get(&map_sym) {
1507      None => None,
1508      Some(map_vec) => match map_vec.front() {
1509        Some(Stored::HashStored(h)) => h.get(key),
1510        _ => None,
1511      },
1512    }
1513  }
1514
1515  pub fn lookup_mapping_keys(&self, map: &str) -> Vec<SymStr> {
1516    let map_sym = arena::pin(map);
1517    match self.value.get(&map_sym) {
1518      None => Vec::new(),
1519      Some(map_vec) => match map_vec.front() {
1520        Some(Stored::HashStored(h)) => h.keys().copied().collect(),
1521        _ => Vec::new(),
1522      },
1523    }
1524  }
1525
1526  pub fn lookup_stacked_values(&self, key: &str) -> Vec<&Stored> {
1527    let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
1528    self.lookup_stacked_values_sym(key_sym)
1529  }
1530
1531  pub fn lookup_stacked_values_sym(&self, key: SymStr) -> Vec<&Stored> {
1532    if let Some(vdq) = self.value.get(&key) {
1533      vdq.iter().collect::<Vec<&Stored>>()
1534    } else {
1535      Vec::new()
1536    }
1537  }
1538
1539  fn lookup_definition_internal(&self, key: &Token) -> Option<&VecDeque<Stored>> {
1540    let cc = key.get_catcode();
1541    let name = key.get_sym();
1542    let lookupname: Option<SymStr> = if (cc == Catcode::ACTIVE) || (cc == Catcode::CS) {
1543      // `\special_relax`-family tokens all resolve under the bare `\special_relax`
1544      // name (shared `\relax` meaning; identity lives in `noexpand_shadowed`).
1545      if name == pin!("") {
1546        None
1547      } else {
1548        Some(meaning_key(key))
1549      }
1550    } else {
1551      key.get_executable_primitive_name().map(arena::pin)
1552    };
1553
1554    if let Some(lname) = lookupname {
1555      self.meaning.get(&lname)
1556    } else {
1557      None
1558    }
1559  }
1560  pub fn ensure_tag_property(&mut self, tag: SymStr) -> &mut TagOptions {
1561    self.tag_properties.entry(tag).or_default()
1562  }
1563}
1564
1565#[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone, PartialEq)]
1566enum RotateState {
1567  Main,
1568  Std,
1569  Sty,
1570}
1571// Perf/safety: `Cell<RotateState>` instead of `static mut` — RotateState is
1572// Copy, so Cell gives us Get/Set with no unsafe, preserving the thread_local
1573// single-threaded access guarantee without requiring unsafe at each call site.
1574#[thread_local]
1575static STATE_IN_USE: std::cell::Cell<RotateState> = std::cell::Cell::new(RotateState::Main);
1576
1577pub fn use_sty_state() {
1578  if STATE_IN_USE.get() != RotateState::Sty {
1579    let mut sty_state = sty_state_mut!();
1580    let mut main_state = state_mut!();
1581    std::mem::swap(&mut *sty_state, &mut *main_state);
1582    STATE_IN_USE.set(RotateState::Sty);
1583  }
1584}
1585pub fn use_std_state() {
1586  if STATE_IN_USE.get() != RotateState::Std {
1587    let mut std_state = std_state_mut!();
1588    let mut main_state = state_mut!();
1589    std::mem::swap(&mut *std_state, &mut *main_state);
1590    STATE_IN_USE.set(RotateState::Std);
1591  }
1592}
1593pub fn use_main_state() {
1594  match STATE_IN_USE.get() {
1595    RotateState::Sty => {
1596      let mut sty_state = sty_state_mut!();
1597      let mut main_state = state_mut!();
1598      std::mem::swap(&mut *sty_state, &mut *main_state);
1599      STATE_IN_USE.set(RotateState::Main);
1600    },
1601    RotateState::Std => {
1602      let mut std_state = std_state_mut!();
1603      let mut main_state = state_mut!();
1604      std::mem::swap(&mut *std_state, &mut *main_state);
1605      STATE_IN_USE.set(RotateState::Main);
1606    },
1607    RotateState::Main => {},
1608  };
1609}
1610
1611/// Free every definition/register/box this thread accumulated, returning
1612/// all three `State` singletons (`STATE`, `STD_STATE`, `STY_STATE`) to a
1613/// fresh, empty baseline and the rotation to `Main`.
1614///
1615/// **Danger:** this invalidates all live definitions/`SymStr`-keyed data
1616/// on the thread. Sound only between fully independent conversions in a
1617/// reused process — the test harness (each test serializes to owned
1618/// `String`s, then resets before its thread exits) or a future daemon
1619/// that re-initializes afterward. The single-conversion binary never
1620/// calls this; it exits instead. Pairs with [`crate::common::arena::reset`]
1621/// — see [`crate::reset_thread_engine`] for the combined entry point and
1622/// the `#[thread_local]`-no-drop rationale.
1623pub fn reset_thread_state() {
1624  // Make sure STATE holds the main state (not swapped out with std/sty)
1625  // before we replace it, so all three slots are freed for real.
1626  use_main_state();
1627  *STATE.borrow_mut() = State::new(StateOptions {
1628    catcodes: Some(Catcodes::Standard),
1629    ..StateOptions::default()
1630  });
1631  *STD_STATE.borrow_mut() = State::new(StateOptions {
1632    catcodes: Some(Catcodes::Standard),
1633    ..StateOptions::default()
1634  });
1635  *STY_STATE.borrow_mut() = State::new(StateOptions {
1636    catcodes: Some(Catcodes::Style),
1637    ..StateOptions::default()
1638  });
1639  STATE_IN_USE.set(RotateState::Main);
1640}
1641
1642/// A refused redefinition of a locked `\<name>` that EXTENDS the meaning it read — the locked binding's replacement
1643/// text with tokens appended or prepended, as etoolbox's `\appto`/`\preto` (`\edef#1{\expandonce#1…}`) and
1644/// latex.ltx's `\g@addto@macro` (`\toks@\expandafter{#1#2}\xdef#1{\the\toks@}`) make it — extends what the
1645/// class itself defined: in TeX the class's own definition is the meaning it extends, so the record of the class's
1646/// definition (`\lx@dropped@<name>`, `dropped_key`) becomes that definition with the same tokens appended or
1647/// prepended. wiley-article.cls:420-466 `\renewcommand{\maketitle}{…\@papertype…\@paperfield…}` then :576
1648/// `\appto{\maketitle}{…}`: the record held the kernel's body and the append, and the class's title page, which
1649/// the `\maketitle` deposit replays, was gone (1911.06213). `None` (the refused definition is recorded as it is)
1650/// unless the class's record and the binding are parameterless token bodies and the refused one is the binding's body
1651/// with tokens added at one end.
1652fn extended_refused_definition(
1653  token: &Token,
1654  definition: &Stored,
1655  dropped_key: SymStr,
1656) -> Option<Stored> {
1657  let parameterless = |d: &Expandable| {
1658    d.get_parameters()
1659      .is_none_or(|p| p.get_parameters().is_empty())
1660  };
1661  let Stored::Expandable(new) = definition else {
1662    return None;
1663  };
1664  let Some(Stored::Expandable(binding)) = lookup_meaning(token) else {
1665    return None;
1666  };
1667  let Some(Stored::Expandable(prior)) =
1668    lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(arena::with(dropped_key, ToString::to_string)))
1669  else {
1670    return None;
1671  };
1672  if !parameterless(new) || !parameterless(&binding) || !parameterless(&prior) {
1673    return None;
1674  }
1675  let (
1676    Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(new_body)),
1677    Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(kernel)),
1678    Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(own)),
1679  ) = (
1680    new.get_expansion(),
1681    binding.get_expansion(),
1682    prior.get_expansion(),
1683  )
1684  else {
1685    return None;
1686  };
1687  let (new_body, kernel, own) = (new_body.unlist_ref(), kernel.unlist_ref(), own.unlist_ref());
1688  if kernel.is_empty() || new_body.len() <= kernel.len() {
1689    return None;
1690  }
1691  let extended: Vec<Token> = if new_body.starts_with(kernel) {
1692    own
1693      .iter()
1694      .chain(&new_body[kernel.len()..])
1695      .copied()
1696      .collect()
1697  } else if new_body.ends_with(kernel) {
1698    new_body[..new_body.len() - kernel.len()]
1699      .iter()
1700      .chain(own)
1701      .copied()
1702      .collect()
1703  } else {
1704    return None;
1705  };
1706  let mut kept = (*prior).clone();
1707  kept.expansion = Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(Tokens::new(extended)));
1708  Some(Stored::Expandable(Rc::new(kept)))
1709}
1710
1711/// A shorthand for installing definitions
1712pub fn install_definition<T: Into<Stored>>(definition: T, scope: Option<Scope>) {
1713  let definition = definition.into();
1714
1715  // Locked definitions!!! (or should this test be in assignMeaning?)
1716  // Ignore attempts to (re)define $cs from tex sources
1717  let token = match definition {
1718    Stored::Expandable(ref defn) => defn.get_cs(),
1719    Stored::Conditional(ref defn) => defn.get_cs(),
1720    Stored::Constructor(ref defn) => defn.get_cs(),
1721    Stored::Primitive(ref defn) => defn.get_cs(),
1722    Stored::MathPrimitive(ref defn) => defn.get_cs(),
1723    Stored::Register(ref defn) => defn.get_cs(),
1724    Stored::Token(ref token) => Cow::Borrowed(token),
1725    _ => panic!("_wrong_argument_for_install_definition"),
1726  };
1727  let cs_sym = token.get_cs_name();
1728  // A real definition retires the error-stub provenance (`is_error_stub`):
1729  // a name stubbed as `<ltx:ERROR/>` and later `\def`ed is a defined cs
1730  // again, so `\csname…\endcsname` takes tex.web's §373 path for it, not the
1731  // §370 discard. Probe-only, like the `:locked` check below.
1732  if let Some(stub_sym) = token.with_cs_name(|cs| arena::get_suffixed(cs, ":error@stub"))
1733    && lookup_bool_sym(stub_sym)
1734  {
1735    assign_value_sym(stub_sym, false, Some(Scope::Global));
1736  }
1737  // (read against this redefinition's own interface: a lock that holds only against copies, `files_lock_holds`; an
1738  // unlocked state reads no lock and allocates nothing here)
1739  let locked =
1740    !state_is_unlocked() && token.with_cs_name(|cs| redefinition_locked(cs, &definition));
1741  // (a stand-in's copy's own write to LaTeX's document hooks is document-time code, which it does not register, as it
1742  // does not its `\AtBeginDocument` code: `copy_provides_defers`. ltxgrid's `\prepdef\@begindocumenthook{\open@column@one
1743  // \@ne\set@colht…}` in qm2008_abs.cls and snp.cls, REVTeX 4 copies routed to the revtex4 binding, ran its page
1744  // grid over the binding's names at `\begin{document}`: 7 "Missing number" Warnings, 0805.2253, 0806.0481, 0805.4759)
1745  if !state_is_unlocked() && copy_provides_defers_hook_write(&token) {
1746    return;
1747  }
1748  // (a stand-in's copy's own title page, whatever becomes of it: what it prints decides its setters' defaults,
1749  // `copy_title_page_prints`)
1750  if let Stored::Expandable(ref defn) = definition
1751    && let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion()
1752  {
1753    note_copy_provides_title_page(&token, body);
1754  }
1755  if locked && copy_provides_refuses_locked(&token) {
1756    restore_cleared_binding(&token);
1757  } else if locked {
1758    restore_cleared_binding(&token);
1759    // A LOCKED BINDING OWNS ITS CALLS. The lock keeps the semantic markup of a call (Perl acb3b27e: commands "that
1760    // lose semantics when redefined"), and a use without an argument cannot produce that markup: the binding would
1761    // read the next token as its argument (a pgffor `\foreach \i/\text` loop variable: 15 errors; `\@for\text:=`:
1762    // Fatal; `\def\title{On Widgets}` then `\section*{\title}`). So a VALUE the document defines — a definition
1763    // without parameters — of a binding that takes a braced argument ([`owns_calls`]; a robust binding by its inner
1764    // `\cs␣`, the kernel `\cite`: eplain's btxmac scratch, SYNC_STATUS 67n) becomes the meaning: what
1765    // `\ifx`, `\meaning` and every use without an argument see, as in pdflatex; the binding stays the handler of
1766    // its calls, as `\lx@locked@<cs>` ([`crate::definition::expandable::SpecialCall::YieldsCalls`]). A definition
1767    // with parameters defines a command, not a value, so every use of it stays the binding's (`\renewcommand\mathbf
1768    // [1]{\boldsymbol{#1}}` with `\mathbf a`; 2006.08408's `\renewcommand{\sqrt}[2][\mkern8mu]{…}` with
1769    // `\sqrt k`). Raw class and package code stays refused, as in Perl (State.pm:502-517), and so does a binding
