1770    // without a braced interface (sectioning, `\footnote`, `\url`, `\maketitle`). The records below are written
1771    // either way, so calls behave as before. Accepted residual: a parameterless forwarder used without braces
1772    // (`\def\mathcal{\mathscr}` with `\mathcal C`; the `\mathpalette` `\sqrt` hack with `\sqrt n`, 1806.09639,
1773    // 1904.10871) runs the document's definition, as pdflatex does: author code is not analysed.
1774    // OXIDIZED_DESIGN #500; repros macro-state/m5_*; witnesses 2405.18512, 1406.2544, 1511.05179.
1775    let call_owner = match definition {
1776      Stored::Expandable(ref defn)
1777        if crate::definition::origin::current_origin()
1778          == crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Document
1779          && defines_value(&token, defn) =>
1780      {
1781        lookup_meaning(&token)
1782          .filter(|binding| owns_calls(&token, binding))
1783          .map(|binding| (Rc::clone(defn), binding))
1784      },
1785      _ => None,
1786    };
1787    // THE DROPPED SETTER'S INTERNALS EXIST, EMPTY. A raw class that
1788    // redefines a locked frontmatter command as a plain setter —
1789    // afthesis.cls:520 `\def\author#1{\def\auth@r{#1}}` — expects the
1790    // internal it would define to exist for its own readers (`\flyleaf`,
1791    // `\titlepage` :637/:688 read `\auth@r`); afthesis itself initializes its
1792    // other internals empty at load (:494-495 `\def\ti@tle{}`). Perl
1793    // (`State.pm:502-517`) drops the redefinition and nothing else, so
1794    // `\auth@r` stays undefined (usethesis). The locked binding remains the
1795    // sole semantic source (running the setter's body with the real
1796    // arguments would duplicate the author through the class layout), and
1797    // every `\def`/`\gdef`/`\edef`/`\xdef <cs>` in the dropped body whose
1798    // `<cs>` is still undefined gets an EMPTY definition — the class's own
1799    // default convention, with no name enumerated. Guard:
1800    // `perfect_kernel_batch54::refused_setter_internals_take_the_replayed_value`.
1801    if let Stored::Expandable(ref defn) = definition
1802      && let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion()
1803    {
1804      let body = body.unlist_ref();
1805      let def_names = ["\\def", "\\gdef", "\\edef", "\\xdef"];
1806      for (i, t) in body.iter().enumerate() {
1807        if t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
1808          && t.with_cs_name(|n| def_names.contains(&n))
1809          && let Some(target) = body.get(i + 1)
1810          && target.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
1811          && lookup_meaning(target).is_none()
1812          && let Ok(empty) = Expandable::new(
1813            *target,
1814            None,
1815            Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(Tokens::new(Vec::new()))),
1816            None,
1817          )
1818        {
1819          state_mut!().assign_internal(
1820            TableName::Meaning,
1821            target.get_cs_name(),
1822            Stored::Expandable(Rc::new(empty)),
1823            Some(Scope::Global),
1824          );
1825        }
1826      }
1827    }
1828    // Keep the dropped definition itself, as `\lx@dropped@<name>`: a locked
1829    // binding may run what the class wrote where its own semantics leave a gap —
1830    // `\lx@deposit@maketitle` (sect05.rs) runs a class's title-page body for the
1831    // fields the frontmatter does not carry (ryethesis.cls:282 degree/program/
1832    // university). Nothing runs it unless a binding asks. OXIDIZED_DESIGN #265.
1833    // Macros only: a later `\let<cs>\relax` (article-style classes end their
1834    // `\maketitle` with one) must not overwrite the body it follows.
1835    if matches!(definition, Stored::Expandable(_)) {
1836      let dropped_key =
1837        arena::pin(token.with_cs_name(|cs| s!("\\lx@dropped@{}", cs.trim_start_matches('\\'))));
1838      let kept = extended_refused_definition(&token, &definition, dropped_key)
1839        .unwrap_or_else(|| definition.clone());
1840      state_mut!().assign_internal(TableName::Meaning, dropped_key, kept, Some(Scope::Global));
1841    }
1842    // Record that a redefinition was dropped: a locked binding may consult
1843    // `<cs>:redefined` to yield the parts of its behaviour the class took
1844    // over (see `\lx@maketitle@cleanup`).
1845    // (a class whose load defines the name the lock kept defines it all the same, as its real file does:
1846    // `ClassLoadFrame`, `copy_base_class_defines`)
1847    note_class_load_name(meaning_key(&token));
1848    let redefined_key = arena::pin(token.with_cs_name(|cs| s!("{cs}:redefined")));
1849    state_mut!().assign_internal(
1850      TableName::Value,
1851      redefined_key,
1852      Stored::Bool(true),
1853      Some(Scope::Global),
1854    );
1855    // …and the dropped setter's SIGNATURE: an argument-taking `\def\abstract#1`
1856    // (apa7.cls:785, uwthesis.cls:805) means the class reads `{…}` as one
1857    // pre-tokenized argument stored for later, which the locked binding can
1858    // honour (`\abstract`, sect05.rs). `\newcommand` records it at its own
1859    // locked bail (sect08.rs).
1860    if let Stored::Expandable(ref defn) = definition {
1861      let nargs = defn
1862        .get_parameters()
1863        .map_or(0, |p| p.get_parameters().len());
1864      let nargs_key = arena::pin(token.with_cs_name(|cs| s!("{cs}:redefined@nargs")));
1865      state_mut!().assign_internal(
1866        TableName::Value,
1867        nargs_key,
1868        Stored::Number(Number::new(nargs as i64)),
1869        Some(Scope::Global),
1870      );
1871    }
1872    // …and, against a lock that holds only against copies, the default of the optional argument the copy takes first
1873    // (`[1][\thu@proof@name]`; a robust copy's by its inner `\cs␣`), which the binding titles its own use with
1874    // (amsthm's proof, amsthm_sty.rs).
1875    if let Some(default) = copy_default(&definition) {
1876      record_redefined_default(&token, default);
1877    }
1878    if let Some((defn, binding)) = call_owner {
1879      // The binding, kept on the first override (a later one, a loop's next iteration, overrides an override).
1880      if !matches!(binding, Stored::Expandable(ref b) if b.yields_calls()) {
1881        let owner = locked_binding_token(&token);
1882        state_mut!().assign_internal(
1883          TableName::Meaning,
1884          owner.get_cs_name(),
1885          binding,
1886          Some(Scope::Global),
1887        );
1888      }
1889      let mut overriding = (*defn).clone();
1890      overriding.special_call = expandable::SpecialCall::YieldsCalls;
1891      state_mut!().assign_internal(
1892        TableName::Meaning,
1893        cs_sym,
1894        Stored::Expandable(Rc::new(overriding)),
1895        scope,
1896      );
1897      // One report per control sequence and document (a loop overrides it on every iteration).
1898      let reported_key = arena::pin(token.with_cs_name(|cs| s!("{cs}:lock@reported")));
1899      if !lookup_bool_sym(reported_key) {
1900        assign_value_sym(reported_key, true, Some(Scope::Global));
1901        let cs_name = token.with_cs_name(ToString::to_string);
1902        Info!(
1903          "lock",
1904          cs_name,
1905          s!(
1906            "{cs_name} is locked: its calls keep the binding; its other uses take the document's definition"
1907          )
1908        );
1909      }
1910      return;
1911    }
1912    if let Some(Stored::String(s)) = state!().lookup_value("SOURCEFILE") {
1913      // report if the redefinition seems to come from document source
1914      if arena::with(*s, |txt| {
1915        txt == "Anonymous String" || TEX_OR_BIB_EXT_RE.is_match(txt) && !txt.ends_with(CODE_TEX_EXT)
1916      }) {
1917        // Perl `State.pm` L514 reports the CS itself — `Ignoring redefinition
1918        // of \cite` — not the lookup key. Reporting `lock_key` here named a
1919        // control sequence that does not exist (`\cite:locked`), so the one
1920        // diagnostic for a refused redefinition did not grep for the command
1921        // it was about.
1922        let cs_name = token.with_cs_name(ToString::to_string);
1923        Info!("ignore", cs_name, s!("Ignoring redefinition of {cs_name}"));
1924      }
1925    }
1926  } else {
1927    note_copy_provides_assignment(&token);
1928    state_mut!().assign_internal(TableName::Meaning, cs_sym, definition, scope);
1929  }
1930}
1931
1932/// Generate a stub definition for an undefined control-sequence,
1933/// along with appropriate error messge.
1934pub fn generate_error_stub(token: &Token) -> Result<Token> {
1935  let cs = token.with_cs_name(ToString::to_string);
1936  // Perl `generateErrorStub` (State.pm L532-553) has no counter branch: an undefined `\c@<ctr>` read as a
1937  // control sequence is the error TeX raises too (`! Undefined control sequence.`). The leniency Perl has is in
1938  // its counter readers, `CounterValue` (Package.pm L712; ours `counter::dialect::counter_value`): `\arabic`,
1939  // `\stepcounter`, `\addtocounter`. Witness 1910.02851 (`\ContinuedFloat` in an `algorithm` float reading
1940  // `\c@subalgorithm@save`; Perl's subfig binding reads it through `CounterValue`) is served by subfig.sty:587's own
1941  // guard in the subfig binding, which touches no sub-counter a float type lacks.
1942  // Guard `perfect_kernel_batchga1::an_undefined_counter_register_is_the_error_tex_raises`.
1943  // Gate the undefined-CS summary tally by SUPPRESS_UNDEFINED_ERRORS so it
1944  // matches the `Error!` gate at L1021 below — during expl3-code.tex raw
1945  // load with thousands of forward-references we install the ERROR stub
1946  // without polluting the user-facing summary count. See
1947  // project_kernel_dump_parity.md "iow_wrap residual" for full diagnosis.
1948  if !lookup_bool("SUPPRESS_UNDEFINED_ERRORS") {
1949    note_status(LogStatus::Undefined, Some(&cs));
1950  }
1951  // To minimize chatter, go ahead and define it...
1952  if cs.starts_with("\\if") {
1953    // Apparently an \ifsomething ???
1954    let name = cs.replace("\\if", "");
1955    // Perl `generateErrorStub` (State.pm L539-540) passes the recovery note
1956    // as a SEPARATE Error detail, so `generateMessage` renders it on its own
1957    // indented line — not merged into the primary message. Match that (and
1958    // the already-correct stomach.rs path) so the cortex `details`/log first
1959    // line is just "...is not defined." like Perl.
1960    Error!(
1961      "undefined",
1962      token,
1963      s!("The token {} is not defined.", token.stringify()),
1964      "Defining it now as with \\newif"
1965    );
1966    let setters = [
1967      Expandable::new(
1968        T_CS!(s!("\\{}true", name)),
1969        None,
1970        Some(s!("\\let{}\\iftrue", cs).into()),
1971        None,
1972      )?,
1973      Expandable::new(
1974        T_CS!(s!("\\{}false", name)),
1975        None,
1976        Some(s!("\\let{}\\iffalse", cs).into()),
1977        None,
1978      )?,
1979    ];
1980    let scope = stub_scope(&cs);
1981    past_the_lock(|| {
1982      for setter in setters {
1983        install_definition(setter, Some(Scope::Global));
1984      }
1985      let_i(token, &T_CS!("\\iffalse"), Some(scope));
1986    });
1987    crate::util::logger::note_held_stub(*token);
1988  } else {
1989    // Allow suppression of undefined errors during bulk loading (e.g., expl3-code.tex)
1990    // where forward references are later resolved by post-load fixups.
1991    if !lookup_bool("SUPPRESS_UNDEFINED_ERRORS") {
1992      Error!(
1993        "undefined",
1994        token,
1995        s!("The token {} is not defined.", token.stringify()),
1996        "Defining it now as <ltx:ERROR/>"
1997      );
1998    }
1999    let scope = stub_scope(&cs);
2000    let stub = Constructor {
2001      cs: *token,
2002      replacement: Some(Rc::new(move |document, _args, _props| {
2003        document.make_error("undefined", &cs)
2004      })),
2005      ..Constructor::default()
2006    };
2007    //TODO: sizer => "X"),
2008    past_the_lock(|| install_definition(stub, Some(scope)));
2009    // The stub's provenance travels with the NAME (tex.web §370 discards an
2010    // undefined cs inside `\csname…\endcsname`; a defined non-expandable one
2011    // takes the §373 `back_error` path): `<cs>:error@stub`, read by
2012    // `gullet::read_cs_name_inner` — not a global "last stubbed token", which
2013    // went stale across interleaved stubs.
2014    // (at the stub's own scope: a locked name's stub that ends with its group leaves the binding, which is no stub)
2015    assign_value_sym(
2016      arena::pin(token.with_cs_name(|c| s!("{c}:error@stub"))),
2017      true,
2018      Some(scope),
2019    );
2020    crate::util::logger::note_held_stub(*token);
2021  }
2022  Ok(*token)
2023}
2024
2025/// The scope [`generate_error_stub`] installs a stub at: global, as Perl's `generateErrorStub` (State.pm:553
2026/// `installDefinition(…, 'global')`), except for a name that carries a lock ([`name_carries_lock`]). Such a name is
2027/// undefined only because a `\let` cleared its binding, and that `\let` may be local: the stub is the recovery for
2028/// this clearing, so it is local too and ends where the clearing ends — `{\let\cite\undefined X\cite{a}} Y\cite{b}`
2029/// typesets the second citation, as pdflatex does, and `\begingroup\let\section\nonexist\section{A}\endgroup
2030/// \section{B}` keeps the section B. A clearing at the top level leaves the stub for the rest of the document, one
2031/// Error per name as before. Guards `perfect_kernel_batch68u::a_locked_name_cleared_in_a_group_is_back_after_it`.
2032fn stub_scope(cs: &str) -> Scope {
2033  if name_carries_lock(cs) {
2034    Scope::Local
2035  } else {
2036    Scope::Global
2037  }
2038}
2039
2040/// Run [`generate_error_stub`]'s installs with the state unlocked. The stub is the engine's recovery from an undefined
2041/// control sequence, not a source redefinition, so a lock does not refuse it: a document's `\let` of a locked binding to
2042/// an undefined name (`\let\cite\parencite` with `\parencite` undefined, 2006.07968's apa6 paper; pittetd.cls:313
2043/// `\let\maketitle\HyOrg@maketitle`, 1509.06375) clears the binding, and a refused stub left the name undefined for the
2044/// Stomach's retry (stomach.rs, Perl Stomach.pm:236-237 `invokeToken_undefined`), which raised the same Error until
2045/// TooManyErrors. Installed, the name gives one Error and its `<ltx:ERROR/>`, as pdflatex gives one "Undefined control
2046/// sequence" per use. Perl refuses its own locked names' stubs the same way (State.pm:509; `\let\section\nonexist`:
2047/// 101 Errors and Fatal, KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #841). OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #641; repro
2048/// macro-state/locked_name_let_to_undefined_stub_loop; guard
2049/// `perfect_kernel_batch68u::a_locked_name_let_to_an_undefined_one_errs_once`.
2050fn past_the_lock(install: impl FnOnce()) {
2051  let _unlocked = local_state_unlocked_guard(true);
2052  install();
2053}
2054
2055/// Undo [`generate_error_stub`]: `token` (and a stubbed `\ifX`'s `\Xtrue`/`\Xfalse`) is undefined
2056/// again. A discarded [`crate::util::logger::DiagnosticsHold`] takes back its scope's errors and the
2057/// stubs standing in for them, so the document's own later use of the command reports it.
2058pub fn remove_error_stub(token: &Token) {
2059  let cs = token.with_cs_name(ToString::to_string);
2060  // A locked name's stub was local ([`stub_scope`]): undone only while it still stands — not after the group it was
2061  // made in has ended, which gave the binding back — and undone there, so the binding of the enclosing groups stands.
2062  let scope = stub_scope(&cs);
2063  let local = matches!(scope, Scope::Local);
2064  if let Some(name) = cs.strip_prefix("\\if") {
2065    for setter in [T_CS!(s!("\\{name}true")), T_CS!(s!("\\{name}false"))] {
2066      assign_meaning(&setter, Stored::None, Some(Scope::Global));
2067    }
2068    if local && !x_equals(token, &T_CS!("\\iffalse")) {
2069      return;
2070    }
2071  } else {
2072    if local && !is_error_stub(token) {
2073      return;
2074    }
2075    assign_value_sym(arena::pin(s!("{cs}:error@stub")), false, Some(scope));
2076  }
2077  assign_meaning(token, Stored::None, Some(scope));
2078}
2079
2080/// Was `token` stubbed as `<ltx:ERROR/>` by [`generate_error_stub`]? Probe
2081/// only: the flag key is interned when the stub is made, so an unknown name
2082/// costs no arena entry (the `:locked` probe's discipline).
2083pub fn is_error_stub(token: &Token) -> bool {
2084  let key = token.with_cs_name(|c| s!("{c}:error@stub"));
2085  arena::get(&key).is_some_and(lookup_bool_sym)
2086}
2087
2088/// Install a `Constructor` for `token` whose sole effect at digestion time is to
2089/// emit `<ltx:ERROR class='undefined'>content</ltx:ERROR>` (the Rust equivalent
2090/// of Perl `Document::makeError`). It logs NOTHING — the caller is responsible
2091/// for the `Error!`/`note_status`. Mirrors the make_error constructor that
2092/// `generate_error_stub` installs for undefined *commands*, so undefined
2093/// *environments* (`\begin{undefinedenv}`) leave the same visible
2094/// `<ltx:ERROR>` marker as Perl instead of silently vanishing from the output.
2095/// Like that stub, it installs past a lock (`past_the_lock`, below).
2096pub fn install_undefined_error_constructor(token: Token, content: &str) {
2097  let content = content.to_string();
2098  let stub = Constructor {
2099    cs: token,
2100    replacement: Some(Rc::new(move |document, _args, _props| {
2101      document.make_error("undefined", &content)
2102    })),
2103    ..Constructor::default()
2104  };
2105  let scope = token.with_cs_name(stub_scope);
2106  past_the_lock(|| install_definition(stub, Some(scope)));
2107}
2108
2109/// Does a provide-only load ([`ProvideOnlyGuard`](crate::definition::origin::ProvideOnlyGuard)) decline this definition
2110/// of `token`? While the guard lives, a binding's definition (`DefinitionOrigin::Binding`, `Fallback` or `Borrowed`:
2111/// OmniBus and every binding it requires, inst_support's `\author[]{}` and epsf's `\epsfbox` among them) is declined
2112/// when the name was defined before the guard: defined now in the `\providecommand` sense (latex.ltx `\@ifundefined`: a
2113/// meaning other than `\relax`, `binding::def::dialect::is_defined_token`), not a fallback guess
2114/// (`is_fallback_meaning`), and not assigned since the guard began (`provide_only_assigned`: the load's own names,
2115/// which it may define again — OmniBus's `\keywords{}` before its `\keywords` that also opens `{keywords}`). An
2116/// environment `\begin{X}`/`\end{X}` counts as defined when `\X` is (latex.ltx `\begin` runs `\X`), and `\end{X}` also
2117/// when `\endX` is. The binding definers (`def_macro`, `def_primitive`, `def_constructor`, `def_register`,
2118/// `def_conditional`, `def_environment`, `def_math`) and `Let!` consult it before they assign anything; TeX's own
2119/// `\def`/`\let` do not, so TeX code run during the load keeps its usual effect (a hook appended to, a scratch macro),
2120/// nor does the loader's bookkeeping, made at its caller's origin. A macro marked `extends` (it wraps and delegates to
2121/// the name's current meaning) is not declined.
2122pub fn provide_only_declines(token: &Token) -> bool {
2123  use crate::{
2124    binding::def::dialect::is_defined_token,
2125    definition::origin::{DefinitionOrigin, current_origin, provide_only},
2126  };
2127  if !provide_only()
2128    || !matches!(
2129      current_origin(),
2130      DefinitionOrigin::Binding | DefinitionOrigin::Fallback | DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed
2131    )
2132  {
2133    return false;
2134  }
2135  let defined = |token: &Token| {
2136    is_defined_token(token)
2137      && !is_fallback_meaning(token)
2138      && !state!().provide_only_assigned.contains(&meaning_key(token))
2139  };
2140  if defined(token) {
2141    return true;
2142  }
2143  token.with_cs_name(|name| {
2144    let env = |prefix: &str| {
2145      name
2146        .strip_prefix(prefix)
2147        .and_then(|rest| rest.strip_suffix('}'))
2148    };
2149    if let Some(env) = env("\\begin{") {
2150      defined(&T_CS!(s!("\\{env}")))
2151    } else if let Some(env) = env("\\end{") {
2152      defined(&T_CS!(s!("\\{env}"))) || defined(&T_CS!(s!("\\end{env}")))
2153    } else {
2154      false
2155    }
2156  })
2157}
2158
2159/// "A stand-in's copy provides" (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731): note this assignment of `token`'s meaning, made
2160/// while the paper's copy of an author file is read after the binding that stands in for it
2161/// (`binding::content::read_stand_in_copy`), or while the `\AtEndOfClass`/`\AtEndOfPackage` code it registered runs
2162/// at the end of that load ([`copy_provides_wrap`]). Every assignment of a meaning goes through here — TeX's `\def`
2163/// family, `\let`, `\futurelet`, `\chardef` and the `\countdef` family, and the LaTeX declarators (`\newcommand`,
2164/// `\renewcommand`, `\DeclareRobustCommand`, `\newenvironment`, `\newcounter`, `\newif`, `\newtheorem`, `\newlength`…),
2165/// at [`install_definition`] and [`assign_meaning`]. One of a name LaTeXML's semantic layer owns
2166/// (`owned_by_the_semantic_layer`; an environment `{X}` is owned when `\begin{X}`, `\X` or `\endX` is) holds only
2167/// inside the copy's own code: the binding's meaning is kept aside and put back when the copy's read ends
2168/// ([`leave_copy_provides_frame`]), so for the document the definition acted as `\providecommand` does, while the
2169/// copy's load-time logic sees what it defined, as in TeX: aipproc.cls:559 `\ifnum\pageref>0` after its option's
2170/// `\chardef\pageref` (astro-ph0105128); epl2.cls:1317-1318's `\let\epl@org@bibitem\bibitem` before its `\def\bibitem`,
2171/// which the epl binding makes too (a kept `\let` would take the binding's wrapper for the original, and `\bibitem`
2172/// would call itself: 0906.2627, 1102.2776, 1203.4765). Every other assignment stands as TeX makes it: an undefined
2173/// name (the copy's extras), a raw kernel name, an earlier raw file's or the document's, a stub or borrow. Not while
2174/// the state is unlocked (a binding's `before`/`after` code, an `AddToMacro!` append), nor for a locked name, which
2175/// `install_definition` refuses first ([`copy_provides_refuses_locked`]). Each name kept for the binding is logged
2176/// once per copy, at Info.
2177pub fn note_copy_provides_assignment(token: &Token) {
2178  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
2179  if id == 0 || state_is_unlocked() || !matches!(token.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE)
2180  {
2181    return;
2182  }
2183  let key = meaning_key(token);
2184  let Some(at) = state!()
2185    .copy_provides_frames
2186    .iter()
2187    .rposition(|frame| frame.id == id && !frame.repeat)
2188  else {
2189    return;
2190  };
2191  if state!().copy_provides_frames[at].kept_keys.contains(&key) {
2192    return;
2193  }
2194  // (a name whose earlier assignment in the copy's code stood is the copy's: `\let\refname\relax` then
2195  // `\newcommand\refname{…}`, as LaTeX's `\newcommand` finds a `\relax` name definable)
2196  if state!().copy_provides_frames[at].assigned.contains(&key) {
2197    return;
2198  }
2199  // (a counter's representation and reset list are owned only when the stand-in binding's own load assigned them:
2200  // `counter_part`)
2201  // (an environment's names are provided or declined together: once the copy's code redefined one the binding owns —
2202  // its `\begin{X}`, kept aside — the others it defines next are kept too, though the name it redefined now carries
2203  // the copy's meaning; cimart.cls:295-315's `\newtheorem{theorem}[definition]` under OmniBus's `{theorem}` left the
2204  // binding's `\begin{theorem}` with the copy's `\end{theorem}`, a group open at the document's end: 2412.00032,
2205  // 2408.07575's `{example}`)
2206  let pair_kept = token.with_cs_name(|name| {
2207    environment_names(name).iter().any(|other| {
2208      state!().copy_provides_frames[at]
2209        .kept_keys
2210        .contains(&meaning_key(&T_CS!(other.as_str())))
2211    })
2212  });
2213  let owned = if token.with_cs_name(counter_part).is_some() {
2214    state!().stand_in_meanings.contains(&key)
2215  } else if unowned_text_default(token) || base_class_name_made_relax(token) {
2216    false
2217  } else {
2218    // (not by its macro partner alone: plain's `\endgroup` does not make a copy's `\group` LaTeXML's)
2219    let environment = |name: &str| name.starts_with("\\begin{") || name.starts_with("\\end{");
2220    pair_kept
2221      || owned_by_the_semantic_layer(token)
2222      || token.with_cs_name(|name| {
2223        environment_names(name)
2224          .iter()
2225          .filter(|other| environment(name) || environment(other))
2226          .any(|other| owned_by_the_semantic_layer(&T_CS!(other.as_str())))
2227      })
2228  };
2229  if !owned {
2230    state_mut!().copy_provides_frames[at].assigned.insert(key);
2231    return;
2232  }
2233  let binding = lookup_meaning(token).unwrap_or(Stored::None);
2234  let mut state = state_mut!();
2235  let frame = &mut state.copy_provides_frames[at];
2236  frame.kept.push((key, binding));
2237  frame.kept_keys.insert(key);
2238}
2239
2240/// Is `token` a name the stand-in binding's base class assigned (`State::stand_in_base_defaults`) that a file since
2241/// made `\relax` — a raw base class the copy loads: `\let\figurename\relax` before the copy's
2242/// `\newcommand\figurename{Fig}`? LaTeX finds a `\relax` name undefined (`\newcommand`, `\@ifundefined`), so the copy's
2243/// definition stands, as in TeX.
2244fn base_class_name_made_relax(token: &Token) -> bool {
2245  state!()
2246    .stand_in_base_defaults
2247    .contains(&meaning_key(token))
2248    && lookup_meaning(token)
2249      .is_some_and(|meaning| Some(meaning) == lookup_meaning(&crate::token::TOKEN_RELAX))
2250}
2251
2252/// Does a stand-in's copy, read or running its hook now ([`note_copy_provides_assignment`]), redefine the locked
2253/// `token`? Then the lock refuses it as for any file, but keeps no record of it — no `\lx@dropped@<name>` body for
2254/// the binding to run in a gap, no `<cs>:redefined` for it to yield to, no default or signature (the records of a raw
2255/// class taking over a locked command, OXIDIZED_DESIGN #265, #500): the binding stands in for this very file, so the
2256/// copy takes nothing over. Logged once per copy, at Info, as a redefinition of an owned name is.
2257pub fn copy_provides_refuses_locked(token: &Token) -> bool {
2258  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
2259  if id == 0 {
2260    return false;
2261  }
2262  let key = meaning_key(token);
2263  let mut state = state_mut!();
2264  if let Some(copy) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(id as usize - 1)
2265    && copy.declined.insert(key)
2266  {
2267    let file = copy.file.clone();
2268    drop(state);
2269    let name = token.with_cs_name(ToString::to_string);
2270    Info!(
2271      "provide",
2272      name,
2273      s!("{name} keeps LaTeXML's locked definition: the paper's {file} redefines it")
2274    );
2275  }
2276  true
2277}
2278
2279/// Does a stand-in's copy, read now ([`note_copy_provides_assignment`]), save `source` again into `token` — `\let
2280/// \token\source` — where `token` already holds an earlier meaning of `source` (its definition is a `\let` copy made
2281/// under `source`'s name, `definition_cs`) that the stand-in binding's load assigned, and `source` has been redefined
2282/// since — by the binding, the copy's read not having assigned `source` itself — to a meaning that reaches `token`
2283/// (`meaning_reaches`), so that the `\let` would make it call itself? Then the
2284/// `\let` is declined, in the copy's code too: `token` keeps the meaning TeX gives it, `source` before the redefinition. The binding (or a file it read) made the very save-and-wrap the copy makes — `\let\org\X`, then
2285/// `\X` redefined to call `\org` —, and saving the wrapper into `\org` makes `\X` call itself: myjmlr.cls and
2286/// jmlr_mod.cls (renamed jmlr.cls copies routed to the jmlr binding, whose jmlrutils.sty made the same wrap) run
2287/// `\let\jmlr@org@newtheorem\newtheorem` (jmlr.cls:753-754) before their own `\newtheorem{example}`, which looped to
2288/// the timeout (1506.02582, 2304.14217; OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 2, the epl2.cls `\let\epl@org@bibitem`
2289/// case met in the copy's own code). Logged once per copy, at Info.
2290pub fn copy_provides_keeps_saved_meaning(token: &Token, source: &Token, meaning: &Stored) -> bool {
2291  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
2292  if id == 0
2293    || state_is_unlocked()
2294    || !matches!(token.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE)
2295    || token == source
2296  {
2297    return false;
2298  }
2299  let key = meaning_key(token);
2300  if !state!().stand_in_meanings.contains(&key) {
2301    return false;
2302  }
2303  let Some(saved) = lookup_meaning(token) else {
2304    return false;
2305  };
2306  if definition_cs(&saved) != Some(*source) || same_meaning(&saved, meaning) {
2307    return false;
2308  }
2309  // (only while `source` still holds the binding's meaning — the copy's read has not assigned it — and only a `\let`
2310  // that would make that wrapper call itself, `source`'s meaning reaching `token`: pgf's scratch
2311  // `\let\pgfutil@reserved@c\pgfutil@reserved@b`, which `\usetikzlibrary` runs while the copy is read right after its
2312  // own `\def\pgfutil@reserved@b`, is no save-and-wrap — declining it lost 2207.07492's sn-jnl.cls:303
2313  // `\usetikzlibrary{snakes}`, and where `@b`'s loop body names `@c` it left pgf's recursion spinning to the timeout,
2314  // 2101.08181's lmcs.cls copy)
2315  let reassigned_by_the_copy = state!()
2316    .copy_provides_frames
2317    .iter()
2318    .rev()
2319    .find(|frame| frame.id == id && !frame.repeat)
2320    .is_some_and(|frame| frame.assigned.contains(&meaning_key(source)));
2321  if reassigned_by_the_copy || !meaning_reaches(meaning, key) {
2322    return false;
2323  }
2324  let mut state = state_mut!();
2325  if let Some(copy) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(id as usize - 1)
2326    && copy.declined.insert(key)
2327  {
2328    let file = copy.file.clone();
2329    drop(state);
2330    let name = token.with_cs_name(ToString::to_string);
2331    let source_name = source.with_cs_name(ToString::to_string);
2332    Info!(
2333      "provide",
2334      name,
2335      s!(
2336        "{name} keeps its saved {source_name}: the paper's {file} saves {source_name} again after its binding \
2337         redefined it"
2338      )
2339    );
2340  }
2341  true
2342}
2343
2344/// Does the macro `meaning` reach the name `key` — `key` in its body, or in the body of a macro its body names, and so
2345/// on — so that `\let\<key>` to it would make it call itself? A breadth-first walk over macro bodies, each name visited
2346/// once, up to [`MEANING_REACH_LIMIT`] names (beyond it, no: the wrapper the walk looks for calls its saved command
2347/// within a step or two — jmlrutils.sty:466-494's `\newtheorem` → `\jmlr@newtheorem` → `\jmlr@org@newtheorem`,
2348/// epl2.cls's `\bibitem` → `\epl@org@bibitem`).
2349fn meaning_reaches(meaning: &Stored, key: SymStr) -> bool {
2350  let mut seen: HashSet<SymStr> = HashSet::default();
2351  let mut pending: VecDeque<Stored> = VecDeque::from([meaning.clone()]);
2352  while let Some(current) = pending.pop_front() {
2353    let Stored::Expandable(defn) = current else {
2354      continue;
2355    };
2356    let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
2357      continue;
2358    };
2359    for t in body.unlist_ref().iter() {
2360      if !matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE) {
2361        continue;
2362      }
2363      let name = meaning_key(t);
2364      if name == key {
2365        return true;
2366      }
2367      if seen.len() >= MEANING_REACH_LIMIT {
2368        return false;
2369      }
2370      if seen.insert(name)
2371        && let Some(next) = lookup_meaning(t)
2372      {
2373        pending.push_back(next);
2374      }
2375    }
2376  }
2377  false
2378}
2379
2380/// How many names [`meaning_reaches`] visits at most.
2381const MEANING_REACH_LIMIT: usize = 256;
2382
2383/// Does a stand-in's copy, read now ([`note_copy_provides_assignment`]), (re)declare or renumber the counter `ctr` the
2384/// stand-in binding's own load declared or numbered (its `\c@<ctr>` assigned while the binding loaded,
2385/// `State::stand_in_meanings`, or the `\the<ctr>` of a counter that exists: `\newcounter`, `\numberwithin`,
2386/// `\@addtoreset`, `\counterwithin`)? Then the counter keeps the binding's numbering: its reset list is the binding's, as
2387/// its `\the<ctr>` is. Any other counter is the copy's, declared for real (`new_counter` suspends the copy's
2388/// provides-mode for it): the kernel's `equation` with the pool's article `\theequation` — as article.cls's defaults
2389/// yield to the real class's own: jheppub.sty:313-314
2390/// `\renewcommand\theequation{\thesection.\arabic{equation}}` and `\numberwithin{equation}{section}` number (2.1) as its
2391/// PDF does (2112.14648, 2607.20603) — and a counter the binding only has a `\the<ctr>` for, OmniBus's `\thechapter`
2392/// autoload stub: WileyNJDv5.cls:5728 and MRM.cls:2160 `\newcounter{chapter}[part]`, ws-rv9x6.cls:399
2393/// `\newcounter{chapter}` (2301.03442: declined, `\c@chapter` was "not a register"). Logged once per copy, at Info.
2394pub fn copy_provides_keeps_counter(ctr: &str) -> bool {
2395  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
2396  if id == 0 || state_is_unlocked() {
2397    return false;
2398  }
2399  let counter = T_CS!(s!("\\c@{ctr}"));
2400  let numbered = |token: &Token| state!().stand_in_meanings.contains(&meaning_key(token));
2401  // (a `\the<ctr>` numbers a counter that exists — the kernel's `equation` — not one it only stands for: OmniBus's
2402  // `\thechapter` autoload stub with no `\c@chapter`)
2403  let exists = lookup_meaning(&counter).is_some_and(|meaning| !matches!(meaning, Stored::None));
2404  if !numbered(&counter) && !(exists && numbered(&T_CS!(s!("\\the{ctr}")))) {
2405    return false;
2406  }
2407  let key = meaning_key(&counter);
2408  let mut state = state_mut!();
2409  if let Some(copy) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(id as usize - 1)
2410    && copy.declined.insert(key)
2411  {
2412    let file = copy.file.clone();
2413    drop(state);
2414    Info!(
2415      "provide",
2416      ctr,
2417      s!("Counter {ctr} keeps LaTeXML's numbering: the paper's {file} redeclares or renumbers it")
2418    );
2419  }
2420  true
2421}
2422
2423/// The counter whose representation or reset list `name` is — `\the<ctr>`, `\p@<ctr>`, `\cl@<ctr>` of a counter
2424/// `\c@<ctr>` — for [`note_copy_provides_assignment`] and [`copy_provides_keeps_counter`].
2425fn counter_part(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
2426  let ctr = name
2427    .strip_prefix("\\the")
2428    .or_else(|| name.strip_prefix("\\p@"))
2429    .or_else(|| name.strip_prefix("\\cl@"))?;
2430  if ctr.is_empty() {
2431    return None;
2432  }
2433  matches!(
2434    lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(s!("\\c@{ctr}"))),
2435    Some(Stored::Register(_))
2436  )
2437  .then(|| ctr.to_string())
2438}

2440/// Keep a stand-in copy's own title page (`\maketitle`, `\@maketitle`), defined while it is read
2441/// ([`install_definition`], `\newcommand`'s refusal), whether the binding's lock refuses it or the overlay holds it:
2442/// what it prints decides which of the copy's setter defaults the document shows (`frontmatter_stores::copy_title_page_prints`,
2443/// latexml_engine).
2444pub fn note_copy_provides_title_page(token: &Token, body: &Tokens) {
2445  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
2446  if id == 0 || !matches!(token.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS) {
2447    return;
2448  }
2449  if !token.with_cs_name(|name| matches!(name, "\\maketitle" | "\\@maketitle")) {
2450    return;
2451  }
2452  if let Some(copy) = state_mut!().copy_provides_copies.get_mut(id as usize - 1) {
2453    copy.title_page.push(body.clone());
2454  }
2455}

2457/// What [`watch_meanings`]' selection says of a name.
2458#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
2459pub enum WatchedName {
2460  No,
2461  Yes,
2462  /// Watched, its `\relax` being what a `\csname` leaves on a name not yet defined rather than a meaning of its own.
2463  RelaxIsUnset,
2464}
2465
2466/// A selection of names for [`watch_meanings`]. Asked of every assignment a document or a raw file makes, so the bytes after a
2467/// name's backslash that `select` can take are the only cost of any other name: 1 of 3 at the second, one at the third.
2468#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
2469pub struct MeaningWatch {
2470  pub second_byte: [u8; 3],
2471  pub third_byte:  u8,
2472  pub select:      fn(&str) -> WatchedName,
2473}
2474
2475impl MeaningWatch {
2476  #[inline]
2477  fn may_take(&self, second: u8, third: u8) -> bool {
2478    third == self.third_byte && self.second_byte.contains(&second)
2479  }
2480}
2481
2482/// Watch the meanings of the control sequences whose names `select` takes, from now on: any later assignment by a document,
2483/// a raw file or a format file that changes the standing meaning of one, however local, is seen by
2484/// [`watched_meaning_changed`] for the rest of the conversion; a new name has none to change. A native path that stands for a family of macros answers only while they are what it ports, at
2485/// the cost of a test by two bytes per such assignment and none per use (latexml_engine `l3fp_native`).
2486pub fn watch_meanings(select: MeaningWatch) {
2487  let mut state = state_mut!();
2488  state.meaning_watch = Some(select);
2489  state.watched_meaning_changed = None;
2490}
2491
2492/// Has a meaning [`watch_meanings`] watches changed? The first that did.
2493pub fn watched_meaning_changed() -> Option<SymStr> { state!().watched_meaning_changed }
2494
2495/// Would [`note_setter_call`] observe a call of `cs`? Without the observation, for a native reader that declines such a
2496/// call to whoever then makes it.
2497pub fn setter_call_is_observed(cs: &Token) -> bool {
2498  crate::definition::origin::copy_default_watch()
2499    && crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() == 0
2500    && state!().watched_setters.contains(&meaning_key(cs))
2501}
2502
2503/// Observe the calls of the setters `names` ([`State::watched_setters`]).
2504pub fn watch_setters(names: &[&str]) {
2505  let keys: Vec<SymStr> = names.iter().map(|name| meaning_key(&T_CS!(name))).collect();
2506  state_mut!().watched_setters.extend(keys);
2507}
2508
2509/// A macro `cs` is called (`Expandable::invoke`, while [`crate::definition::origin::setter_watch`]): a watched setter
2510/// called outside a stand-in copy's read is the document's (or its binding's) call, `<cs>:called`, whatever its
2511/// arguments — `\author{}` as much as `\author{Jane Doe}`, which a binding's accumulating `\author` (inst_support's)
2512/// does not queue nor clear.
2513pub fn note_setter_call(cs: &Token) {
2514  if setter_call_is_observed(cs) {
2515    assign_value(
2516      &cs.with_cs_name(|name| s!("{name}:called")),
2517      true,
2518      Some(Scope::Global),
2519    );
2520  }
2521}
2522
2523/// A call of one of LaTeXML's own macros (a binding's or the kernel's) that read its arguments from the document's own
2524/// text ([`note_document_call`]): the title-page check's second source.
2525#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
2526pub struct DocumentCall {
2527  /// the macro called
2528  pub cs:   Token,
2529  /// its arguments as read, kept once: every entry made inside the call shares them ([`claim_open_document_calls`])
2530  pub args: CallArguments,
2531  /// the input after its arguments: its expansion is read before it (open while [`gullet::input_unread_since`])
2532  mark:     gullet::InputMark,
2533  /// the entry queued inside it first, by the title-page check's count ([`claim_open_document_calls`])
2534  claimed:  Option<usize>,
2535}
2536
2537/// The arguments a [`DocumentCall`] read, shared by the entries made inside it.
2538pub type CallArguments = Rc<[DocumentArgument]>;
2539
2540/// An argument a [`DocumentCall`] read.
2541#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
2542pub struct DocumentArgument {
2543  /// its parameter's number (`#n`)
2544  pub number:   usize,
2545  /// its parameter is optional
2546  pub optional: bool,
2547  /// the call's body names it (`#n`), or its use is unknown (a closure's): it is handed on; or the macro is the kernel's,
2548  /// whose drop is TeX's own, or the binding declares it dropped by design (`Expandable::dropped_by_design`): no loss
2549  pub used:     bool,
2550  /// the tokens read
2551  pub tokens:   Tokens,
2552}
2553
2554thread_local! {
2555  /// The document calls whose expansions are still being read ([`note_document_call`]), innermost last.
2556  static DOCUMENT_CALLS: RefCell<Vec<DocumentCall>> = const { RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
2557}
2558
2559/// A macro of LaTeXML's own read its arguments `args` from the document's text (`Expandable::invoke`, while
2560/// [`crate::definition::origin::document_call_watch`]): kept while its expansion is read, the calls whose expansions
2561/// ended dropped. An argument-less call is not kept.
2562pub fn note_document_call(cs: Token, args: Vec<DocumentArgument>) {
2563  DOCUMENT_CALLS.with(|calls| {
2564    let mut calls = calls.borrow_mut();
2565    calls.retain(|call| gullet::input_unread_since(&call.mark));
2566    if args.iter().any(|arg| !arg.tokens.is_empty()) {
2567      calls.push(DocumentCall {
2568        cs,
2569        args: args.into(),
2570        mark: gullet::input_mark(),
2571        claimed: None,
2572      });
2573    }
2574  });
2575}
2576
2577/// The document calls whose expansions are being read now ([`note_document_call`]), for an entry `claimer` the
2578/// frontmatter API queues inside them (latexml_engine `title_page_check::note_frontmatter_entry`): `None` when there are
2579/// none, else the claimer of the outermost (the entry an earlier one inside the same call made, or `claimer`), those no
2580/// entry claimed before, outermost first, now `claimer`'s, and the arguments of them all — shared, not copied: an
2581/// `\author` of 3,000 names joined by `\and` makes 3,000 entries inside one call.
2582pub fn claim_open_document_calls(
2583  claimer: usize,
2584) -> Option<(usize, Vec<DocumentCall>, Vec<CallArguments>)> {
2585  DOCUMENT_CALLS.with(|calls| {
2586    let mut calls = calls.borrow_mut();
2587    calls.retain(|call| gullet::input_unread_since(&call.mark));
2588    let outermost = calls.first()?.claimed.unwrap_or(claimer);
2589    let arguments = calls.iter().map(|call| Rc::clone(&call.args)).collect();
2590    let mut claimed = Vec::new();
2591    for call in calls.iter_mut().filter(|call| call.claimed.is_none()) {
2592      call.claimed = Some(claimer);
2593      claimed.push(call.clone());
2594    }
2595    Some((outermost, claimed, arguments))
2596  })
2597}
2598
2599/// A call of a setter a raw class or a raw file of the document's own directory made ([`note_store_call`]).
2600#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
2601pub struct StoreCall {
2602  /// the setter
2603  pub cs:     Token,
2604  /// a later call of it replaces this one, the stores it writes
2605  /// ([`crate::definition::expandable::StoredParameters`])
2606  pub stored: expandable::StoredParameters,
2607  /// the arguments it writes into a store
2608  pub args:   Vec<Tokens>,
2609  /// those of them optional or in brackets: a store's key or label far more often than its text
2610  pub keys:   Vec<Tokens>,
2611}
2612
2613/// What [`take_store_calls`] gives: the setter calls kept, and what the bound left out.
2614#[derive(Debug, Default)]
2615pub struct StoreCalls {
2616  /// the calls, in order
2617  pub calls:           Vec<StoreCall>,
2618  /// how many calls [`STORE_CALLS_KEPT`] left out
2619  pub dropped:         usize,
2620  /// the setters of those, the first few, each once
2621  pub dropped_setters: Vec<Token>,
2622}
2623
2624/// The most setter calls kept ([`note_store_call`]): a document whose own style's setter is called thousands of times
2625/// before its title page keeps the first ones; what the check digests stays bounded.
2626pub const STORE_CALLS_KEPT: usize = 512;
2627
2628/// How many setters of the calls left out [`StoreCalls::dropped_setters`] names.
2629const DROPPED_SETTERS_NAMED: usize = 8;
2630
2631thread_local! {
2632  /// The setter calls the document's text made before its title page ([`note_store_call`]).
2633  static STORE_CALLS: RefCell<StoreCalls> = RefCell::new(StoreCalls::default());
2634}
2635
2636/// A setter a raw class or a raw file of the document's own directory made was called while the document's text is read
2637/// (`Expandable::invoke`, while [`crate::definition::origin::store_call_watch`]): its stored arguments `args`, and those
2638/// `keys` optional or in brackets, kept for the title-page check's third source (latexml_engine
2639/// `title_page_check::digest_raw_setter_calls`); a replacing setter's earlier call is superseded by this one, as its store is.
2640pub fn note_store_call(
2641  cs: Token,
2642  stored: &expandable::StoredParameters,
2643  args: Vec<Tokens>,
2644  keys: Vec<Tokens>,
2645) {
2646  STORE_CALLS.with(|calls| {
2647    let mut calls = calls.borrow_mut();
2648    if stored.replaces {
2649      calls.calls.retain(|call| call.cs != cs);
2650    }
2651    if args.is_empty() && keys.is_empty() {
2652      return;
2653    }
2654    if calls.calls.len() < STORE_CALLS_KEPT {
2655      calls.calls.push(StoreCall {
2656        cs,
2657        stored: stored.clone(),
2658        args,
2659        keys,
2660      });
2661    } else {
2662      calls.dropped += 1;
2663      if calls.dropped_setters.len() < DROPPED_SETTERS_NAMED && !calls.dropped_setters.contains(&cs)
2664      {
2665        calls.dropped_setters.push(cs);
2666      }
2667    }
2668  });
2669}
2670
2671/// The setter calls kept ([`note_store_call`]), taken: none are left, a new conversion's or after a title page's.
2672pub fn take_store_calls() -> StoreCalls {
2673  STORE_CALLS.with(|calls| std::mem::take(&mut *calls.borrow_mut()))
2674}
2675
2676/// Does the control sequence `cs` mean a token register (`\newtoks`, `\toks@`)?
2677pub fn is_token_register(cs: &Token) -> bool {
2678  use crate::definition::register::RegisterType;
2679  with_meaning(cs, |meaning| match meaning {
2680    Some(Stored::Register(register)) => matches!(register.register_type, RegisterType::Tokens),
2681    _ => false,
2682  })
2683}
2684
2685thread_local! {
2686  /// The arguments binding macros dropped while the watch was on ([`note_dropped_argument`]).
2687  static DROPPED_ARGUMENTS: RefCell<Vec<DroppedArgument>> = const { RefCell::new(Vec::new()) };
2688}
2689
2690/// An argument a macro dropped, which the title-page check digests itself ([`note_dropped_argument`]; latexml_engine
2691/// `title_page_check::never_digested`).
2692#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
2693pub struct DroppedArgument {
2694  /// the macro that dropped it
2695  pub cs:     Token,
2696  /// its parameter's number (`#n`)
2697  pub number: usize,
2698  /// the tokens read
2699  pub tokens: Tokens,
2700}
2701
2702/// A binding's macro `cs` dropped its required argument `argument` — its token body never names it, nor does the
2703/// binding declare it dropped by design (`Expandable::dropped_by_design`) — while a frontmatter entry the document gave
2704/// is digested (`Expandable::invoke`, while [`crate::definition::origin::dropped_argument_watch`]): kept for the
2705/// title-page check (latexml_engine `title_page_check`, jmlr's `\titletag`). A blank argument is not kept.
2706pub fn note_dropped_argument(cs: Token, argument: &DocumentArgument) {
2707  if argument.tokens.unlist_ref().iter().any(|token| {
2708    !matches!(
2709      token.get_catcode(),
2710      Catcode::SPACE | Catcode::BEGIN | Catcode::END
2711    )
2712  }) {
2713    DROPPED_ARGUMENTS.with(|dropped| {
2714      dropped.borrow_mut().push(DroppedArgument {
2715        cs,
2716        number: argument.number,
2717        tokens: argument.tokens.clone(),
2718      })
2719    });
2720  }
2721}
2722
2723/// The arguments binding macros dropped ([`note_dropped_argument`]) since the count `from`, taken; with `from` the
2724/// count before them, for a nested watch.
2725pub fn take_dropped_arguments(from: usize) -> Vec<DroppedArgument> {
2726  DROPPED_ARGUMENTS.with(|dropped| {
2727    let mut dropped = dropped.borrow_mut();
2728    let from = from.min(dropped.len());
2729    dropped.split_off(from)
2730  })
2731}
2732
2733/// How many arguments binding macros dropped so far ([`note_dropped_argument`]): a watch's start.
2734pub fn dropped_argument_count() -> usize {
2735  DROPPED_ARGUMENTS.with(|dropped| dropped.borrow().len())
2736}
2737
2738/// Forget the document calls ([`note_document_call`]): a conversion's start.
2739pub fn reset_document_calls() {
2740  DOCUMENT_CALLS.with(|calls| calls.borrow_mut().clear());
2741  DROPPED_ARGUMENTS.with(|dropped| dropped.borrow_mut().clear());
2742}
2743
2744/// Was the watched setter `cs` called outside a stand-in copy's read ([`note_setter_call`])?
2745pub fn setter_called(cs: &Token) -> bool {
2746  lookup_bool(&cs.with_cs_name(|name| s!("{name}:called")))
2747}
2748
2749/// The title pages stand-in copies defined ([`note_copy_provides_title_page`]), each with its copy's number, for
2750/// `frontmatter_stores::copy_title_page_prints` (latexml_engine).
2751pub fn copy_provides_title_pages() -> Vec<(u32, Tokens)> {
2752  state!()
2753    .copy_provides_copies
2754    .iter()
2755    .enumerate()
2756    .flat_map(|(index, copy)| {
2757      copy
2758        .title_page
2759        .iter()
2760        .map(move |body| (index as u32 + 1, body.clone()))
2761    })
2762    .collect()
2763}
2764
2765/// Copy `id`'s own version of the name `token` the binding owns, kept in its overlay while the binding's meaning is in
2766/// force ([`note_copy_provides_assignment`]).
2767pub fn copy_provides_overlaid_meaning(id: u32, token: &Token) -> Option<Stored> {
2768  state!()
2769    .copy_provides_copies
2770    .get((id as usize).checked_sub(1)?)
2771    .and_then(|copy| copy.overlay.get(&meaning_key(token)).cloned())
2772}
2773
2774/// Begin a frame of copy `id`'s provides-mode ([`crate::definition::origin::enter_copy_provides`]; `0` suspends the mode for
2775/// a file the copy loads, a repeat of the mode in force adds nothing): the copy's own definitions of names the binding
2776/// owns, kept from its earlier read or hooks, are in force again for its code, the meanings they replace kept aside.
2777pub fn enter_copy_provides_frame(id: u32, previous: u32) {
2778  let mut kept = Vec::new();
2779  let mut kept_keys = rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default();
2780  if id != 0 && id != previous {
2781    let overlay: Vec<(SymStr, Stored)> = state!()
2782      .copy_provides_copies
2783      .get(id as usize - 1)
2784      .map(|copy| copy.overlay.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.clone())).collect())
2785      .unwrap_or_default();
2786    for (key, meaning) in overlay {
2787      let current = meaning_by_key(key);
2788      kept.push((key, current));
2789      kept_keys.insert(key);
2790      state_mut!().assign_internal(TableName::Meaning, key, meaning, Some(Scope::Global));
2791    }
2792  }
2793  state_mut!().copy_provides_frames.push(CopyProvidesFrame {
2794    id,
2795    repeat: id == previous,
2796    kept,
2797    kept_keys,
2798    assigned: rustc_hash::FxHashSet::default(),
2799  });
2800}
2801
2802/// End the frame [`enter_copy_provides_frame`] began: each name the copy's code redefined, and the binding owns, takes
2803/// back the meaning kept aside, and the copy's own is kept for its next frame (a hook it registered).
2804pub fn leave_copy_provides_frame() {
2805  let Some(frame) = state_mut!().copy_provides_frames.pop() else {
2806    return;
2807  };
2808  if frame.id == 0 || frame.repeat {
2809    return;
2810  }
2811  let mut frame = frame;
2812  copy_text_names_stand(&mut frame);
2813  // A name the copy `\let` to its own version of a name the binding owns takes the binding's back with it:
2814  // emulateapj5.sty:386 defines `\keywords`, :394 `\let\subjectheadings=\keywords`, and the document's
2815  // `\subjectheadings` made the class's "Subject headings:" paragraph where the binding makes `<keywords>`
2816  // (astro-ph0011001). Kept in the copy's overlay with the rest, for its hooks. Only a definition the copy made under
2817  // the kept name itself: a kept flag the copy `\let` to `\iftrue` shares that object with every other true flag
2818  // (elife.cls:336-337's `\ifstre@msuppinfoopen`, newfile.sty:91: 2303.08822).
2819  let replaced: Vec<(Stored, Stored)> = frame
2820    .kept
2821    .iter()
2822    .map(|(key, kept)| (*key, meaning_by_key(*key), kept.clone()))
2823    .filter(|(key, copys, kept)| {
2824      !same_meaning(copys, kept) && definition_cs(copys).is_some_and(|cs| meaning_key(&cs) == *key)
2825    })
2826    .map(|(_, copys, kept)| (copys, kept))
2827    .collect();
2828  if !replaced.is_empty() {
2829    for key in &frame.assigned {
2830      let current = meaning_by_key(*key);
2831      if let Some((_, kept)) = replaced
2832        .iter()
2833        .find(|(copys, _)| same_meaning(copys, &current))
2834      {
2835        let mut state = state_mut!();
2836        if let Some(copy) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(frame.id as usize - 1) {
2837          copy.overlay.insert(*key, current);
2838        }
2839        state.assign_internal(TableName::Meaning, *key, kept.clone(), Some(Scope::Global));
2840      }
2841    }
2842  }
2843  for (key, kept) in frame.kept.into_iter().rev() {
2844    let copys = meaning_by_key(key);
2845    // (a name the copy's code left as it found it — the kernel's `\protected@edef` lets `\protect` aside and back —
2846    // was not redefined: nothing to log)
2847    let redefined = !same_meaning(&copys, &kept);
2848    let mut state = state_mut!();
2849    let mut logged = None;
2850    if let Some(copy) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(frame.id as usize - 1) {
2851      if redefined && copy.declined.insert(key) {
2852        logged = Some(copy.file.clone());
2853      }
2854      if redefined {
2855        copy.restored.insert(key, kept.clone());
2856      }
2857      copy.overlay.insert(key, copys);
2858    }
2859    state.assign_internal(TableName::Meaning, key, kept, Some(Scope::Global));
2860    drop(state);
2861    if let Some(file) = logged {
2862      let name = arena::to_string(key);
2863      Info!(
2864        "provide",
2865        name,
2866        s!(
2867          "{name} keeps LaTeXML's definition: the paper's {file} redefines it for its own code only"
2868        )
2869      );
2870    }
2871  }
2872}
2873
2874/// Are `a` and `b` the same meaning: the same definition object (a `\let` copies the object), or equal otherwise?
2875fn same_meaning(a: &Stored, b: &Stored) -> bool {
2876  match (a, b) {
2877    (Stored::Expandable(x), Stored::Expandable(y)) => Rc::ptr_eq(x, y),
2878    (Stored::Constructor(x), Stored::Constructor(y)) => Rc::ptr_eq(x, y),
2879    (Stored::Primitive(x), Stored::Primitive(y)) => Rc::ptr_eq(x, y),
2880    (Stored::MathPrimitive(x), Stored::MathPrimitive(y)) => Rc::ptr_eq(x, y),
2881    (Stored::Conditional(x), Stored::Conditional(y)) => Rc::ptr_eq(x, y),
2882    (Stored::Register(x), Stored::Register(y)) => Rc::ptr_eq(x, y),
2883    (Stored::Token(x), Stored::Token(y)) => x == y,
2884    (Stored::None, Stored::None) => true,
2885    _ => false,
2886  }
2887}
2888
2889/// The meaning bound to `key` (a [`meaning_key`]), `Stored::None` when there is none.
2890fn meaning_by_key(key: SymStr) -> Stored {
2891  state!()
2892    .meaning
2893    .get(&key)
2894    .and_then(|entry| entry.front().cloned())
2895    .unwrap_or(Stored::None)
2896}
2897
2898/// A routed copy's definitions of the declared text names (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 14,
2899/// [`copy_text_name_declared`]) that read as text ([`copy_text_refusal`]) stand for the document when its read ends,
2900/// as the real class's: they leave `frame`'s kept names, which the binding's meanings replace. Decided on the copy's
2901/// own meanings, before any is put back, so `\let\refname\relax` then `\newcommand\refname{…}` is the copy's last.
2902/// Logged once per name, at Info.
2903fn copy_text_names_stand(frame: &mut CopyProvidesFrame) {
2904  let file = {
2905    let state = state!();
2906    match state.copy_provides_copies.get(frame.id as usize - 1) {
2907      Some(copy) if copy.routed => copy.file.clone(),
2908      _ => return,
2909    }
2910  };
2911  let mut standing = Vec::new();
2912  for (key, kept) in &frame.kept {
2913    let name = arena::to_string(*key);
2914    let copys = meaning_by_key(*key);
2915    if same_meaning(&copys, kept) || !copy_text_name_declared_as(&name) {
2916      continue;
2917    }
2918    match copy_text_refusal(&copys) {
2919      None => {
2920        standing.push(*key);
2921        Info!(
2922          "provide",
2923          name,
2924          s!("{name} takes the paper's {file} definition: a text name its binding declares")
2925        );
2926      },
2927      Some(refused) => {
2928        // (one Info per name, when it is first declined — the check runs again at each frame the copy's read leaves
2929        // — and not the general one of `leave_copy_provides_frame`)
2930        let first = state_mut!()
2931          .copy_provides_copies
2932          .get_mut(frame.id as usize - 1)
2933          .is_some_and(|copy| copy.declined.insert(*key));
2934        if first {
2935          Info!(
2936            "provide",
2937            name,
2938            s!(
2939              "{name} keeps LaTeXML's definition: the paper's {file} defines this text name as more than text \
2940               ({refused})"
2941            )
2942          );
2943        }
2944      },
2945    }
2946  }
2947  frame.kept.retain(|(key, _)| !standing.contains(key));
2948  for key in standing {
2949    frame.kept_keys.remove(&key);
2950  }
2951}
2952
2953/// Number a new stand-in copy of `file` for [`note_copy_provides_assignment`] (1-based,
2954/// [`crate::definition::origin::copy_provides`]); `routed`: its `\Provides` line routed it to `binding`
2955/// (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 12).
2956pub fn new_copy_provides_record(file: &str, binding: &str, routed: bool) -> u32 {
2957  let mut state = state_mut!();
2958  state.copy_provides_copies.push(CopyProvidesRecord {
2959    file: file.to_string(),
2960    binding: binding.to_string(),
2961    routed,
2962    ..CopyProvidesRecord::default()
2963  });
2964  state.copy_provides_copies.len() as u32
2965}
2966
2967/// The other names of the environment a name `\X`, `\endX`, `\begin{X}` or `\end{X}` belongs to (latex.ltx `\begin{X}`
2968/// runs `\X`, `\end{X}` runs `\endX`): for `\X` the binding's `\begin{X}`, `\end{X}` and the macro `\endX`, for `\endX`
2969/// `\begin{X}`, `\end{X}` and `\X`; for `\begin{X}` the macros `\X`, `\endX` and `\end{X}`, for `\end{X}` `\X`, `\endX`
2970/// and `\begin{X}`.
2971fn environment_names(name: &str) -> Vec<String> {
2972  if let Some(env) = name
2973    .strip_prefix("\\begin{")
2974    .and_then(|rest| rest.strip_suffix('}'))
2975  {
2976    return vec![s!("\\{env}"), s!("\\end{env}"), s!("\\end{{{env}}}")];
2977  }
2978  if let Some(env) = name
2979    .strip_prefix("\\end{")
2980    .and_then(|rest| rest.strip_suffix('}'))
2981  {
2982    return vec![s!("\\{env}"), s!("\\end{env}"), s!("\\begin{{{env}}}")];
2983  }
2984  match name.strip_prefix('\\') {
2985    Some(macro_name) if !macro_name.is_empty() => {
2986      match macro_name.strip_prefix("end").filter(|env| !env.is_empty()) {
2987        Some(env) => vec![s!("\\begin{{{env}}}"), s!("\\end{{{env}}}"), s!("\\{env}")],
2988        None => vec![
2989          s!("\\begin{{{macro_name}}}"),
2990          s!("\\end{{{macro_name}}}"),
2991          s!("\\end{macro_name}"),
2992        ],
2993      }
2994    },
2995    _ => Vec::new(),
2996  }
2997}
2998
2999/// Is `token`'s meaning a text default: a public name (letters only) whose meaning is a macro without parameters
3000/// expanding to text (characters, at least one letter: `\refname`'s "References")?
3001fn text_default(token: &Token) -> bool {
3002  let public = token.with_cs_name(|name| {
3003    name.strip_prefix('\\').is_some_and(|letters| {
3004      !letters.is_empty() && letters.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic())
3005    })
3006  });
3007  if !public {
3008    return false;
3009  }
3010  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(token) else {
3011    return false;
3012  };
3013  if defn
3014    .paramlist
3015    .as_ref()
3016    .is_some_and(|params| !params.get_parameters().is_empty())
3017  {
3018    return false;
3019  }
3020  let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = &defn.expansion else {
3021    return false;
3022  };
3023  body.unlist_ref().iter().all(|t| {
3024    matches!(
3025      t.get_catcode(),
3026      Catcode::LETTER | Catcode::OTHER | Catcode::SPACE
3027    )
3028  }) && body
3029    .unlist_ref()
3030    .iter()
3031    .any(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::LETTER)
3032}
3033
3034/// Is `token`'s meaning a text default ([`text_default`]) the stand-in binding does not own, for
3035/// [`note_copy_provides_assignment`]: assigned last by the base class the binding loaded
3036/// (`State::stand_in_base_defaults`: article, report or book, whose bindings assign their classes' names in their own
3037/// load, the OmniBus guess) or by LaTeXML's kernel before it? Then the copy's definition of it stands, as the real
3038/// class's does over article.cls's: MRM.cls:3191 `\def\refname{REFERENCES}` (2506.14995's PDF), ws-rv9x6.cls:91
3039/// `\newcommand\bibname{References}` (2301.03442). One the binding's own code assigned, or a binding it required
3040/// (natbib's), stays the binding's.
3041fn unowned_text_default(token: &Token) -> bool {
3042  use crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin;
3043  if !text_default(token) {
3044    return false;
3045  }
3046  let key = meaning_key(token);
3047  if state!().stand_in_meanings.contains(&key) {
3048    return state!().stand_in_base_defaults.contains(&key);
3049  }
3050  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(token) else {
3051    return false;
3052  };
3053  matches!(
3054    defn.origin,
3055    DefinitionOrigin::Unknown | DefinitionOrigin::Pool
3056  ) || class_layer_definition(token)
3057}
3058
3059/// Does a stand-in's copy, read now, `\newcommand` a text default its binding does not own (`unowned_text_default`),
3060/// in a real class that lacks the name? Then the declaration defines it, as it does in TeX: LaTeXML's kernel or the
3061/// binding's base class predefined it, and no base class the copy loads defines it — a standalone class (ws-rv9x6.cls:91
3062/// `\newcommand\bibname{References}`, 2301.03442), or a name its base class lacks (article.cls has no `\bibname`). A
3063/// name the base class the copy `\LoadClass`es defines is the real class's already, so its `\newcommand` is TeX's
3064/// "already defined" ([`copy_provides_text_default_clashes`]).
3065pub fn copy_provides_text_default_yields(token: &Token) -> bool {
3066  crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() != 0
3067    && !state_is_unlocked()
3068    && unowned_text_default(token)
3069    && !copy_base_class_defines(token)
3070}
3071
3072/// Does a stand-in's copy, read now, `\newcommand` a text default its binding does not own that a base class it
3073/// `\LoadClass`ed defines (`copy_base_class_defines`: article.cls:616 `\newcommand\refname`, :618 `\figurename`, which
3074/// the article binding assigns in its own load; a raw base class's own definition)? The real class has the name, so
3075/// LaTeX stops with "Command … already defined" and keeps the first definition: an Error here as there (sect08.rs
3076/// `\newcommand`; the reviewer's probes newcmd_base and nc_chain, pdflatex 2 errors, "References"/"Figure 1";
3077/// nc_namedef 1). A name the base class made `\relax` is definable, as LaTeX's `\newcommand` finds it.
3078/// `\def`/`\renewcommand` redefine as before.
3079pub fn copy_provides_text_default_clashes(token: &Token) -> bool {
3080  crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() != 0
3081    && !state_is_unlocked()
3082    && text_default(token)
3083    && !binding_owns(token)
3084    && copy_base_class_defines(token)
3085}
3086
3087/// The stand-in binding the copy read now (`copy_provides`) was read after, or the empty string when no copy is read.
3088pub fn copy_provides_binding() -> String {
3089  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
3090  if id == 0 {
3091    return String::new();
3092  }
3093  state!()
3094    .copy_provides_copies
3095    .get(id as usize - 1)
3096    .map(|copy| copy.binding.clone())
3097    .unwrap_or_default()
3098}

3100/// The text names a stand-in's copy may supply over its binding, the kernel's default (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES
3101/// #731 rules 13 and 14; user ruling 2026-10-21: "the bindings should always win in terms of semantic markup … the
3102/// slight typographic variations in the raw .cls or .sty file … if we can preserve and reuse them that should be via
3103/// a generic mechanism that is set in the binding with a reasonable default"): the standard classes' and LaTeX's
3104/// `\…name` macros, the word a heading or a label prints — aip-cp.cls:875 `\newcommand\refname{REFERENCES}`
3105/// (1506.04050, 1604.08112), the AASTeX 6+ copies' `\def\abstractname{ABSTRACT}` (2107.03400), proto.cls:798
3106/// `\def\abstractname{~}` (1402.7103). A binding extends it with its own ([`declare_copy_text_names`]). Everything else
3107/// a binding defines is its markup of the class, and the copy's version of it is declined: caption formats
3108/// (`\fnum@<type>`, whose punctuation met the binding's caption separator, "Fig. 1.—.— " in 1106.2565), the caption
3109/// separator, `\@thmcountersep` (1803.06644's "Theorem 51"), `\the<ctr>`, `\p@<ctr>`, `\the<ctr>@ID`, list labels
3110/// (`\labelitemi`), `\today`, `\baselinestretch`, `\CurrentOption`, every `\lx@` name, structure.
3111pub const COPY_TEXT_NAMES: [&str; 13] = [
3112  "\\abstractname",
3113  "\\appendixname",
3114  "\\bibname",
3115  "\\chaptername",
3116  "\\contentsname",
3117  "\\figurename",
3118  "\\indexname",
3119  "\\listfigurename",
3120  "\\listtablename",
3121  "\\partname",
3122  "\\proofname",
3123  "\\refname",
3124  "\\tablename",
3125];
3126
3127/// Declare `names` (`\apj`) text names of the binding loading now, besides the kernel's [`COPY_TEXT_NAMES`]: a copy
3128/// routed to it or a derivative of its class may supply them (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rules 13 and 14). The
3129/// one declaration point of the mechanism, a value `"<name>:copy@text"` as a lock is `"<name>:locked"`; a binding
3130/// declares the names it defines, where it defines them (aas_macros_sty.rs: its journal abbreviations, which
3131/// 1402.7103's proto.cls :1729-1790 spells out, "Astrophys. J.").
3132pub fn declare_copy_text_names(names: &[&str]) {
3133  for name in names {
3134    assign_value(&s!("{name}:copy@text"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3135  }
3136}
3137
3138/// Is `token` a declared text name ([`COPY_TEXT_NAMES`], [`declare_copy_text_names`])? Decided by its name; a routed
3139/// copy's definition of it must read as text besides ([`copy_text_refusal`]).
3140fn copy_text_name_declared(token: &Token) -> bool { token.with_cs_name(copy_text_name_declared_as) }
3141
3142/// [`copy_text_name_declared`] for the name `name` (`\refname`).
3143fn copy_text_name_declared_as(name: &str) -> bool {
3144  COPY_TEXT_NAMES.contains(&name) || arena::get(s!("{name}:copy@text")).is_some_and(lookup_bool_sym)
3145}
