3147/// Is `token` a text name the standard class's binding assigned in its own load, which a derivative of that class read
3148/// now defines itself (`binding::content::standard_class_binding_assigned`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 13)?
3149/// In TeX the derivative is read in the class's place, so its `\newcommand` of the name is the first: aip-cp.cls:875
3150/// `\newcommand\refname{REFERENCES}` (1506.04050, 1604.08112). Only a declared text name yields
3151/// (`copy_text_name_declared`: `\refname`, `\figurename`, `\abstractname`); every other name the binding defined
3152/// stays its markup of the class — `\appendix` (`<ltx:appendix>`, article_cls.rs, book_cls.rs), book's
3153/// `\frontmatter`/`\mainmatter`/`\backmatter` (unnumbered chapters), whose `\newcommand`s in a full class copy
3154/// (hep-ex0405054's book_modified.cls:531, math0301023's khbook.cls:285; 0705.2210, hep-ph0008011, cond-mat0402505,
3155/// quant-ph0409078) lost the appendices and numbered the preface; the caption formats (`\fnum@table`), `\@ptsize`,
3156/// `\@pnumwidth`. Names a stand-in the derivative loads assigned are not the class's (guard
3157/// `perfect_kernel_batch77w::standard_class_derivative_defines_only_its_class_names`).
3158pub fn standard_class_binding_name(token: &Token) -> bool {
3159  copy_text_name_declared(token)
3160    && crate::binding::content::standard_class_binding_assigned(meaning_key(token))
3161}
3162
3163/// Is a copy its `\Provides` line routed to its binding (OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 12) read now, its own
3164/// code locked as a raw read's is?
3165fn reading_a_routed_copy() -> bool {
3166  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
3167  id != 0
3168    && !state_is_unlocked()
3169    && state!()
3170      .copy_provides_copies
3171      .get(id as usize - 1)
3172      .is_some_and(|copy| copy.routed)
3173}
3174
3175/// Does a routed copy, read now, `\newcommand` (or `\providecommand`) a declared text name (`copy_text_name_declared`)
3176/// that LaTeXML's code defined (owned as rule 1 tells, `owned_by_the_semantic_layer`) and no base class the copy
3177/// `\LoadClass`es defines? Then the declaration defines it, as the real class's first definition (proto.cls:798's
3178/// `\abstractname`, :1731 `\newcommand\apj{\ref@jnl{\it Astrophys.\ J.}}` over the aas_macros binding's "ApJ",
3179/// 1402.7103; OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 14); a name the copy's base class has is LaTeX's "already
3180/// defined", as before. Whether its definition stands for the document is decided when the copy's read ends
3181/// (`leave_copy_provides_frame`, `copy_text_refusal`).
3182pub fn routed_copy_text_name_yields(token: &Token) -> bool {
3183  copy_text_name_declared(token)
3184    && reading_a_routed_copy()
3185    && owned_by_the_semantic_layer(token)
3186    && !copy_base_class_defines(token)
3187}
3188
3189/// Why does the routed copy's meaning `meaning` of a declared text name not stand for the document, when the copy's
3190/// read ends (`leave_copy_provides_frame`; OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #731 rule 14)? `None`: it stands — a macro
3191/// without parameters whose body reads as text ([`text_body_refusal`]). The safety check reads the copy's own
3192/// definition and fails closed: `\let\refname\relax`, a macro reading arguments, one whose expansion is LaTeXML's code
3193/// or a `\refname` that sets a heading keeps the binding's; the reason names the first refused token.
3194fn copy_text_refusal(meaning: &Stored) -> Option<String> {
3195  let Stored::Expandable(defn) = meaning else {
3196    return Some(s!("not a macro"));
3197  };
3198  if defn
3199    .paramlist
3200    .as_ref()
3201    .is_some_and(|params| !params.get_parameters().is_empty())
3202  {
3203    return Some(s!("parameters"));
3204  }
3205  match &defn.expansion {
3206    Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) => {
3207      text_body_refusal(body.unlist_ref(), &mut Vec::new()).map(|refused| refused.to_string())
3208    },
3209    Some(ExpansionBody::Closure(_)) => Some(defn.cs.to_string()),
3210    None => None,
3211  }
3212}
3213
3214/// Does `meaning` read arguments — a macro or primitive with parameters?
3215fn reads_arguments(meaning: &Stored) -> bool {
3216  let params = match meaning {
3217    Stored::Expandable(defn) => defn.paramlist.as_ref(),
3218    Stored::Primitive(defn) => defn.paramlist.as_ref(),
3219    _ => None,
3220  };
3221  params.is_some_and(|params| !params.get_parameters().is_empty())
3222}
3223
3224/// The most macros a copy's text name may go through ([`text_body_refusal`]): a journal name's `\ref@jnl`, a font
3225/// switch's, a counter's representation; sectioning and title pages go through LaTeX's machinery of many more.
3226const TEXT_NAME_MACROS: usize = 32;
3227
3228/// The primitives of LaTeXML's code a text name may call besides those printing a character or selecting a font by
3229/// their definition ([`printing_primitive`]): the spaces a name prints, `\ ` (1402.7103's proto.cls:1731 `Astrophys.\
3230/// J.`), `~`'s `\lx@NBSP` (proto.cls:798 `\def\abstractname{~}`, :1737 `Appl.~Opt.`) and `\nobreakspace`'s
3231/// `\lx@nobreakspace`.
3232const TEXT_NAME_SPACES: &[&str] = &["\\ ", "\\lx@NBSP", "\\lx@nobreakspace"];
3233
3234/// LaTeX's font selection interface (NFSS, latex.ltx's `\DeclareRobustCommand\fontseries` …), which a font switch's
3235/// robust command reaches and LaTeXML defines (latex_constructs sect08.rs, sect13.rs): a text name may call them,
3236/// unfollowed — `\fontfamily` reads its argument under a lock, `\not@math@alphabet@@` reads one to warn in math. The
3237/// AASTeX 6+ and emulateapj copies' journal names reach them (`\let\jnl@style=\rm`, `\rm` → `\rmfamily` →
3238/// `\fontfamily\rmdefault\selectfont`: 2007.00749, 2107.03400, 1905.12710), the PoS copies' `\bfseries\scshape`
3239/// (1506.03731, 2504.20273).
3240const TEXT_NAME_FONT_SELECTION: &[&str] = &[
3241  "\\fontencoding",
3242  "\\fontfamily",
3243  "\\fontseries",
3244  "\\fontshape",
3245  "\\fontsize",
3246  "\\selectfont",
3247  "\\usefont",
3248  "\\not@math@alphabet",
3249  "\\not@math@alphabet@@",
3250  "\\lx@normalfont@base",
3251];
3252
3253/// Does the primitive `defn` print or select a font: a character (`\ss`), a font switch (`\it`, a `\font` selector)
3254/// or one of [`TEXT_NAME_SPACES`]?
3255fn printing_primitive(defn: &crate::definition::primitive::Primitive) -> bool {
3256  defn.font.is_some()
3257    || defn.font_id.is_some()
3258    || matches!(
3259      defn.replacement,
3260      Some(crate::definition::PrimitiveBody::String(_))
3261    )
3262    || defn
3263      .cs
3264      .with_cs_name(|name| TEXT_NAME_SPACES.contains(&name))
3265}
3266
3267/// The first token of `body`, a copy's definition of a declared text name, that does not read as text — the safety
3268/// check, which fails closed. Every macro it calls is followed, and those theirs call (at most [`TEXT_NAME_MACROS`],
3269/// `seen`); a robust command's `\protect\X` is followed through `\X` and LaTeX's inner `\X ` (`\DeclareRobustCommand`).
3270/// Characters, a `\let` or `\chardef` character (`\bgroup`, `\&`), a conditional (both branches are read), a font, a
3271/// primitive that prints or selects a font ([`printing_primitive`]), LaTeX's font selection commands
3272/// ([`TEXT_NAME_FONT_SELECTION`], not followed) and macros composing them read as text, so does an empty body (the
3273/// class prints no name). Refused: a constructor (an element), `\par` (a paragraph), `\csname`/`\endcsname` (a name
3274/// made at run time), an undefined name (an error), a macro whose expansion is LaTeXML's code (`\begin`), any other
3275/// primitive (`\noindent`, `\vskip`), a register, a locked command reading arguments (LaTeXML's markup interface:
3276/// `\@startsection`, `\@lbibitem`; a binding owns its calls, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #500).
3277fn text_body_refusal(body: &[Token], seen: &mut Vec<SymStr>) -> Option<Token> {
3278  let protect = T_CS!("\\protect");
3279  for t in body {
3280    if !matches!(t.get_catcode(), Catcode::CS | Catcode::ACTIVE) || *t == protect {
3281      continue;
3282    }
3283    if t.with_cs_name(|name| matches!(name, "\\par" | "\\csname" | "\\endcsname")) {
3284      return Some(*t);
3285    }
3286    let key = meaning_key(t);
3287    if seen.contains(&key) {
3288      continue;
3289    }
3290    let meaning = lookup_meaning(t);
3291    if meaning.is_some() && t.with_cs_name(|name| TEXT_NAME_FONT_SELECTION.contains(&name)) {
3292      continue;
3293    }
3294    if t.with_cs_name(name_carries_lock) && meaning.as_ref().is_some_and(reads_arguments) {
3295      return Some(*t);
3296    }
3297    let reads_as_text = match meaning {
3298      Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) => {
3299        seen.push(key);
3300        if seen.len() > TEXT_NAME_MACROS {
3301          return Some(*t);
3302        }
3303        match &defn.expansion {
3304          Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(inner)) => {
3305            if let Some(refused) = text_body_refusal(inner.unlist_ref(), seen) {
3306              return Some(refused);
3307            }
3308            true
3309          },
3310          Some(ExpansionBody::Closure(_)) => false,
3311          None => true,
3312        }
3313      },
3314      Some(Stored::Primitive(defn)) => {
3315        printing_primitive(&defn)
3316          && defn
3317            .paramlist
3318            .as_ref()
3319            .is_none_or(|params| params.get_parameters().is_empty())
3320      },
3321      // (a `\chardef` character prints it: `\&`, latex.ltx's chardef, in the AASTeX copies' `A\&A`)
3322      Some(Stored::Register(register)) => {
3323        matches!(
3324          register.register_type,
3325          crate::definition::register::RegisterType::CharDef
3326        )
3327      },
3328      Some(
3329        Stored::Token(_) | Stored::Conditional(_) | Stored::Font(_) | Stored::FontDirective(_),
3330      ) => true,
3331      _ => false,
3332    };
3333    if !reads_as_text {
3334      return Some(*t);
3335    }
3336  }
3337  None
3338}
3339
3340/// Did the stand-in binding's own load assign `token` last (`State::stand_in_meanings`, not its base class's
3341/// `State::stand_in_base_defaults`)? Then the copy's definition only provides it ([`note_copy_provides_assignment`]).
3342fn binding_owns(token: &Token) -> bool {
3343  let key = meaning_key(token);
3344  let state = state!();
3345  state.stand_in_meanings.contains(&key) && !state.stand_in_base_defaults.contains(&key)
3346}
3347
3348/// The class file name a load request names (`article`, `dir/mybase.cls` → `article.cls`, `mybase.cls`).
3349pub fn class_file_name(file: &str) -> String {
3350  let name = file.trim();
3351  let name = name.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next().unwrap_or(name);
3352  if name.ends_with(".cls") {
3353    name.to_string()
3354  } else {
3355    s!("{name}.cls")
3356  }
3357}
3358
3359/// RAII frame of one class load (`binding::content::input_definitions`, a `.cls` with its options handled: the
3360/// `\documentclass`, a `\LoadClass`, a binding's `LoadClass!`). While it is open, the macros assigned outside LaTeXML's
3361/// kernel layers — the class binding's own (article.cls's names, article_cls.rs), the raw file's, a package's it
3362/// loads — and the names a lock kept from a definition it made are recorded as the class's (`State::class_loads`),
3363/// as its real file defines them; a repeat load records nothing new. A load a stand-in's copy requests (`copy`: its own
3364/// `\LoadClass`, or a class loaded while that one is open) also names the class among the copy's base classes
3365/// (`CopyProvidesRecord::loads_classes`), whose names its `\newcommand` finds defined
3366/// ([`copy_provides_text_default_clashes`]): chained `\LoadClass{mybase}` over article (the reviewer's probe nc_chain).
3367pub struct ClassLoadFrame {
3368  copy: bool,
3369}
3370
3371impl ClassLoadFrame {
3372  /// Open the frame of loading the class `file`, requested by the stand-in copy `copy` if any.
3373  pub fn enter(file: &str, copy: Option<u32>) -> Self {
3374    let name = class_file_name(file);
3375    let mut state = state_mut!();
3376    if let Some(id) = copy
3377      && let Some(record) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(id as usize - 1)
3378      && !record.loads_classes.contains(&name)
3379    {
3380      record.loads_classes.push(name.clone());
3381    }
3382    // (a class loaded inside the frames open now is loaded inside each of them: the binding's `LoadClass!` of its
3383    // base class, ceurart_cls.rs → OmniBus → article, is a class the binding's load loaded)
3384    let state = &mut *state;
3385    // (the classes the load open now had loaded before this one: for a copy's own load, its binding's base classes)
3386    let outer_classes = copy
3387      .and_then(|_| state.class_load_frames.last())
3388      .and_then(|&open| state.class_loads.get(open))
3389      .map(|record| record.classes.clone())
3390      .unwrap_or_default();
3391    for &open in &state.class_load_frames {
3392      if let Some(outer) = state.class_loads.get_mut(open)
3393        && outer.file != name
3394        && !outer.classes.contains(&name)
3395      {
3396        outer.classes.push(name.clone());
3397      }
3398    }
3399    let index = match state
3400      .class_loads
3401      .iter()
3402      .position(|record| record.file == name)
3403    {
3404      Some(index) => index,
3405      None => {
3406        state.class_loads.push(ClassLoadRecord {
3407          file: name,
3408          ..ClassLoadRecord::default()
3409        });
3410        state.class_loads.len() - 1
3411      },
3412    };
3413    state.class_load_frames.push(index);
3414    if let Some(id) = copy {
3415      state.copy_class_loads.push((id, outer_classes));
3416    }
3417    ClassLoadFrame { copy: copy.is_some() }
3418  }
3419}
3420
3421impl Drop for ClassLoadFrame {
3422  fn drop(&mut self) {
3423    let mut state = state_mut!();
3424    state.class_load_frames.pop();
3425    if self.copy {
3426      state.copy_class_loads.pop();
3427    }
3428  }
3429}
3430
3431/// The stand-in copy whose own `\LoadClass` is open now, if any ([`ClassLoadFrame`]).
3432pub fn open_copy_class_load() -> Option<u32> { state!().copy_class_loads.last().map(|(id, _)| *id) }
3433
3434/// The classes the stand-in binding's load loaded — its base class — for the copy read now
3435/// ([`ClassLoadRecord::classes`]). While the copy's own code runs, the classes of the class load open now; inside a
3436/// class the copy's own `\LoadClass` loaded (a shipped mybase.cls between the copy and a TeX Live class), the classes
3437/// loaded when the copy's first class load began ([`State::copy_class_loads`]), so a class mybase.cls `\LoadClass`es
3438/// meets the binding's base class too, not the empty class load of mybase.cls (72a review r7 NIT 8; guard
3439/// `perfect_kernel_batchint23::a_shipped_intermediate_class_meets_the_class_slot`).
3440pub fn open_class_load_classes() -> Vec<String> {
3441  let state = state!();
3442  if crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() != 0 {
3443    return state
3444      .class_load_frames
3445      .last()
3446      .and_then(|&class| state.class_loads.get(class))
3447      .map(|record| record.classes.clone())
3448      .unwrap_or_default();
3449  }
3450  let Some((id, _)) = state.copy_class_loads.last() else {
3451    return Vec::new();
3452  };
3453  state
3454    .copy_class_loads
3455    .iter()
3456    .find(|(copy, _)| copy == id)
3457    .map(|(_, classes)| classes.clone())
3458    .unwrap_or_default()
3459}
3460
3461/// Name `classes` among the base classes of the stand-in copy `id` (`CopyProvidesRecord::loads_classes`): the classes
3462/// its binding loaded, which stand for the class the copy's own `\LoadClass` named (`binding::content`
3463/// `binding_base_class_stands_for`).
3464pub fn note_copy_base_classes(id: u32, classes: &[String]) {
3465  let mut state = state_mut!();
3466  if let Some(record) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(id as usize - 1) {
3467    for class in classes {
3468      if !record.loads_classes.contains(class) {
3469        record.loads_classes.push(class.clone());
3470      }
3471    }
3472  }
3473}
3474
3475/// Record `key` as a name the class load open now defines ([`ClassLoadFrame`]).
3476fn note_class_load_name(key: SymStr) {
3477  let mut state = state_mut!();
3478  if let Some(&class) = state.class_load_frames.last()
3479    && let Some(record) = state.class_loads.get_mut(class)
3480  {
3481    record.names.entry(key).or_insert(None);
3482  }
3483}
3484
3485/// Does a base class of the stand-in copy read now — a class its own `\LoadClass` requested, or one loaded while that
3486/// was open ([`ClassLoadFrame`]) — define `token`: did that class's load frame assign it (article.cls:613-622's names,
3487/// which article_cls.rs assigns in its own load; a raw base class's `\expandafter\def\csname bibname\endcsname`, the
3488/// reviewer's probe nc_namedef) or a lock keep its definition? Its document-time code
3489/// (`\AtBeginDocument{\def\bibname…}`, nc_cond) has not run, so defines nothing yet, as in TeX.
3490fn copy_base_class_defines(token: &Token) -> bool {
3491  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
3492  if id == 0 {
3493    return false;
3494  }
3495  let key = meaning_key(token);
3496  let state = state!();
3497  let Some(copy) = state.copy_provides_copies.get(id as usize - 1) else {
3498    return false;
3499  };
3500  copy.loads_classes.iter().any(|class| {
3501    state
3502      .class_loads
3503      .iter()
3504      .any(|record| record.file == *class && record.names.contains_key(&key))
3505  })
3506}
3507
3508/// Is `token`'s meaning the macro a class's load frame assigned it ([`ClassLoadFrame`]): the class layer's —
3509/// article.cls's names, which its binding assigns, a raw class's own —, not LaTeXML's semantic layer? A text default of
3510/// the class layer is the class's, as LaTeXML's kernel predefinitions of the same names are ([`unowned_text_default`]).
3511fn class_layer_definition(token: &Token) -> bool {
3512  let Some(Stored::Expandable(defn)) = lookup_meaning(token) else {
3513    return false;
3514  };
3515  let key = meaning_key(token);
3516  state!().class_loads.iter().any(
3517    |record| matches!(record.names.get(&key), Some(Some(assigned)) if Rc::ptr_eq(assigned, &defn)),
3518  )
3519}
3520
3521/// The one "has chapters" test (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #179): does the class have chapters, as the real class's
3522/// `\@ifundefined{chapter}` sees it? natbib's forks head the bibliography by it (NJDnatbib.sty:1050, ws-rv-har.sty:676),
3523/// appendix.sty:38 and titlesec.sty:1526 number by it, and the kernel's `\appendix` numbers within chapters only then
3524/// (`begin_appendices`). The answer the "has chapters" step stored after the document's class loaded
3525/// (`settle_chapter_after_class_load`, latexml_engine sect01.rs, [`CLASS_HAS_CHAPTERS`]); while a class loads, the
3526/// test made now ([`class_has_chapters_now`]), as a class's own `\@ifundefined{chapter}` is answered by what it has
3527/// defined so far. Not a `chapter` counter alone: WileyNJDv5.cls:5728 and MRM.cls:2160 `\newcounter{chapter}[part]` with
3528/// no `\chapter` (2609.08425, 2506.14995: "REFERENCES"; 2211.16474: "Appendix A"). A LaTeX 2.09 `\documentstyle`
3529/// document never settles (only a `\documentclass` retracts and settles, `\chapter:settles`); its class's end stores
3530/// the answer (`\chapter:stored`, `store_chapter_after_latex_ix_class_load`, latexml_engine sect01.rs), so
3531/// `\documentstyle[appendix]{report}` numbers its appendix chapters "Appendix A" and `\documentstyle{article}` with the
3532/// document's own `\newcounter{chapter}` + `\def\chapter` letters its appendix "A", as pdflatex. Before any class load
3533/// (plain TeX), the test made now.
3534pub fn class_defines_chapter() -> bool {
3535  if crate::binding::content::class_loading()
3536    || !(lookup_bool("\\chapter:settles") || lookup_bool("\\chapter:stored"))
3537  {
3538    class_has_chapters_now()
3539  } else {
3540    lookup_bool(CLASS_HAS_CHAPTERS)
3541  }
3542}
3543
3544/// The value [`class_defines_chapter`] reads once the document's class has loaded.
3545pub const CLASS_HAS_CHAPTERS: &str = "\\chapter:class@has@chapters";
3546
3547/// The "has chapters" test made now (OXIDIZED_DESIGN #179), which `settle_chapter_after_class_load` stores after each
3548/// class load: the class has chapters when
3549/// - (a) its own code assigned `\chapter`: a meaning other than the kernel's (a raw class's `\providecommand\chapter`, a
3550///   binding's own `\chapter`: ws-rv9x6, nic-series, jmlr, kluwer's `chapter` option; a `\let\chapter\artchapter`), a
3551///   redefinition the kernel's lock refused (`\chapter:redefined`, state.rs `install_definition`, sect08.rs), or a
3552///   stand-in copy's definition its binding's owned name kept aside or declined (pasj00.cls's `\def\chapter`;
3553///   ws-rv9x6.cls:499 `\newcommand\chapter`, 2301.03442). A meaning that is undefined, `\relax`
3554///   (`\let\chapter\@undefined`; `\csname chapter\endcsname`) or the undefined-error stub of a call is no definition;
3555/// - (b) a compiled binding made the chapter counter (`\c@chapter` of LaTeXML's own origin: report, book, memoir…;
3556///   Perl's own probe, latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:690). A raw or copy `\newcounter{chapter}` alone is not;
3557/// - (c) the class is unknown and OmniBus guesses it (`OmniBus:fallback`: the class loader's fallback, and subfiles,
3558///   whose subfiles.cls:38-40 loads the main file's class), as an unknown class may well have chapters: OmniBus
3559///   autoloads book.cls on `\thechapter` (arXiv:2602.10407).
3560pub fn class_has_chapters_now() -> bool {
3561  let (assigned, kernel_kept) = class_chapter_sources();
3562  assigned || kernel_kept
3563}
3564
3565/// The two halves of [`class_has_chapters_now`]: whether the class's own code assigned `\chapter` (a), and whether the
3566/// class keeps LaTeXML's kernel `\chapter` as a binding's chapters (b) or OmniBus's guess (c).
3567pub fn class_chapter_sources() -> (bool, bool) {
3568  use crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin;
3569  let chapter = T_CS!("\\chapter");
3570  let relax = lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\relax"));
3571  let meaningful =
3572    |meaning: &Stored| !matches!(meaning, Stored::None) && relax.as_ref() != Some(meaning);
3573  let key = meaning_key(&chapter);
3574  let by_copy = state!()
3575    .copy_provides_copies
3576    .iter()
3577    .any(|copy| copy.declined.contains(&key) || copy.overlay.get(&key).is_some_and(meaningful));
3578  let meaning = lookup_meaning(&chapter);
3579  let defined = meaning.as_ref().is_some_and(meaningful) && !lookup_bool("\\chapter:error@stub");
3580  // the kernel's own meaning (the pool's, or `\lx@kernel@chapter` / `\lx@kernel@chapter@counted` put back): a class's
3581  // same-bodied `\newcommand\chapter{\@startsection{chapter}…}` is the class's
3582  let kernels = defined
3583    && (x_equals(&chapter, &T_CS!("\\lx@kernel@chapter"))
3584      || x_equals(&chapter, &T_CS!("\\lx@kernel@chapter@counted")))
3585    && meaning
3586      .as_ref()
3587      .and_then(definition_origin)
3588      .is_some_and(|origin| matches!(origin, DefinitionOrigin::Pool | DefinitionOrigin::Unknown));
3589  let assigned = by_copy || lookup_bool("\\chapter:redefined") || (defined && !kernels);
3590  let binding_counter = matches!(
3591    lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\c@chapter")),
3592    Some(Stored::Register(ref register)) if register.origin.is_latexml_owned()
3593  );
3594  (assigned, binding_counter || lookup_bool("OmniBus:fallback"))
3595}
3596
3597/// The meaning the latest stand-in copy of `file` (a file name, `NJDnatbib.sty`) gave `token` in its own code: its
3598/// overlaid version of a name its binding owns, if the copy redefined it ([`note_copy_provides_assignment`]). For a
3599/// binding's code that runs after its copy (`\AtEndOfPackage`), to tell the copy's version by what it defined.
3600pub fn stand_in_copy_meaning(file: &str, token: &Token) -> Option<Stored> {
3601  let key = meaning_key(token);
3602  state!()
3603    .copy_provides_copies
3604    .iter()
3605    .rev()
3606    .find(|copy| copy.file.rsplit(['/', '\\']).next() == Some(file))
3607    .and_then(|copy| copy.overlay.get(&key).cloned())
3608}
3609
3610/// The meaning the latest stand-in copy that redefined `token` for its own code only gave it (its overlay, the copy's
3611/// `declined` name: "keeps LaTeXML's definition", [`note_copy_provides_assignment`]), while the meaning in force is still
3612/// the binding's the copy's frame gave back ([`CopyProvidesRecord::restored`]; a later file's redefinition is no stale
3613/// copy's to read, review revPB S4): what the paper's copy runs where the binding's version runs here. For the frontmatter's reading of a class's title code (latexml_engine `title_code_author_definitions`:
3614/// neurips_2022.sty's `\@maketitle`, which defines `\NewAnd`, where the neurips binding keeps its own; 2205.14141,
3615/// 2205.14539).
3616pub fn copy_kept_meaning(token: &Token) -> Option<Stored> {
3617  let key = meaning_key(token);
3618  if !owned_by_the_semantic_layer(token) {
3619    return None;
3620  }
3621  let current = meaning_by_key(key);
3622  state!()
3623    .copy_provides_copies
3624    .iter()
3625    .rev()
3626    .find(|copy| copy.declined.contains(&key))
3627    .filter(|copy| {
3628      copy
3629        .restored
3630        .get(&key)
3631        .is_some_and(|kept| same_meaning(kept, &current))
3632    })
3633    .and_then(|copy| copy.overlay.get(&key).cloned())
3634}
3635
3636/// Is `token`'s current meaning owned by LaTeXML's semantic layer, for [`note_copy_provides_assignment`]? Either the
3637/// name was assigned while a stand-in binding loaded ([`crate::definition::origin::StandInLoadGuard`]: its own definitions,
3638/// `\let`s and `RawTeX`, and the bindings it required), at an origin of LaTeXML's own, or its meaning is a definition
3639/// made under this very name (not a `\let` copy of another's, whose object carries the other name) at such an origin —
3640/// `Unknown`, `Pool` or `Binding` (definition/origin.rs): a pool's (`\section`, `\maketitle`) or another binding's
3641/// (natbib's `\citep`, xkeyval's `\usevalue`); a name the stand-in binding's load assigned is owned at `Fallback` too,
3642/// the OmniBus base a class binding loads (`\received`, `\corres`). The format's own definitions (`Plain`,
3643/// `LatexDump`, `Format`), the document's world (`File`, `Document`) and the other guesses that yield (`Fallback`,
3644/// `Stub`, `Borrowed`) are not owned.
3645/// A name a package the binding loaded defined stays owned: the narrower rule (only the binding's own code, so that a
3646/// copy's redefinition of a package's macro stands as in TeX) gave the copy the class bindings' frontmatter and layout
3647/// macros (`\title`, `\author`, `\thanks`, `\@makecaption`, `\the<counter>`, the NFSS size commands; aastex7's three
3648/// Fatals again, 2504.00922, 2605.15371, 2607.03848); the case it was for, 2411.02780's equations.sty, is another file
3649/// of that name (`binding::content` `copy_is_the_stood_in_file`).
3650fn owned_by_the_semantic_layer(token: &Token) -> bool {
3651  use crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin;
3652  let ours = |origin: DefinitionOrigin| {
3653    matches!(
3654      origin,
3655      DefinitionOrigin::Unknown | DefinitionOrigin::Pool | DefinitionOrigin::Binding
3656    )
3657  };
3658  lookup_meaning(token).is_some_and(|meaning| {
3659    definition_origin(&meaning).is_some_and(|origin| {
3660      let key = meaning_key(token);
3661      if state!().binding_lets.contains(&key) && ours(origin) {
3662        // (a `\let` LaTeXML's own code made: the object carries the source's name, `State::binding_lets`)
3663        true
3664      } else if state!().stand_in_meanings.contains(&key) {
3665        // (the stand-in binding's guesses too: the OmniBus base a class binding loads, `Fallback` so that the
3666        // document's own definition wins, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #408 — the copy is not the document: WileyNJDv5's
3667        // `\received` keeps OmniBus's dated frontmatter, 2609.19994)
3668        ours(origin) || origin == DefinitionOrigin::Fallback
3669      } else {
3670        // (or a `\let` the pool made to one of the semantic layer's own internals, `\let\label\lx@label`
3671        // (sect11.rs): no file defines an `\lx@` name, so the object is LaTeXML's construct — a copy's `\def\label`
3672        // writing the `.aux` (aastex7.cls:635, aastex701.cls, siamltex.cls, emulateapj.cls) left every label of the
3673        // document unrecorded: 2508.00291, 2510.05388, 2604.27071)
3674        ours(origin)
3675          && definition_cs(&meaning)
3676            .is_some_and(|cs| cs == *token || cs.with_cs_name(|name| name.starts_with("\\lx@")))
3677      }
3678    })
3679  })
3680}
3681
3682/// The name a definition was made under (a `\let` copy keeps its source's).
3683fn definition_cs(meaning: &Stored) -> Option<Token> {
3684  match meaning {
3685    Stored::Expandable(defn) => Some(defn.cs),
3686    Stored::Constructor(defn) => Some(defn.cs),
3687    Stored::Primitive(defn) => Some(defn.cs),
3688    Stored::MathPrimitive(defn) => Some(defn.cs),
3689    Stored::Conditional(defn) => Some(defn.cs),
3690    Stored::Register(defn) => Some(defn.cs),
3691    _ => None,
3692  }
3693}
3694
3695/// Who made the definition `meaning` (`None`: not a definition — a `\let` to a character, an undefined name).
3696fn definition_origin(meaning: &Stored) -> Option<crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin> {
3697  match meaning {
3698    Stored::Expandable(defn) => Some(defn.origin),
3699    Stored::Constructor(defn) => Some(defn.origin),
3700    Stored::Primitive(defn) => Some(defn.origin),
3701    Stored::MathPrimitive(defn) => Some(defn.origin),
3702    Stored::Conditional(defn) => Some(defn.origin),
3703    Stored::Register(defn) => Some(defn.origin),
3704    _ => None,
3705  }
3706}
3707
3708/// The code a stand-in's copy registers with `\AtEndOfClass`/`\AtEndOfPackage`, which runs at the end of its own
3709/// load: wrapped in the copy's scope markers `\lx@copy@provides@begin{N}` … `\lx@copy@provides@end`, so it runs under
3710/// the copy's provides-mode, with the copy's own definitions in force ([`enter_copy_provides_frame`]; aa.cls:71's
3711/// `\AtEndOfClass` redefinition of `{abstract}` keeps the binding's). `tokens` unchanged when no copy is read or it is
3712/// wrapped already.
3713pub fn copy_provides_wrap(tokens: Tokens) -> Tokens {
3714  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
3715  let begin = T_CS!("\\lx@copy@provides@begin");
3716  if id == 0 || tokens.unlist_ref().first() == Some(&begin) {
3717    return tokens;
3718  }
3719  let mut wrapped = vec![begin, T_BEGIN!()];
3720  wrapped.extend(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(id.to_string())).unlist());
3721  wrapped.push(T_END!());
3722  wrapped.extend(tokens.unlist());
3723  wrapped.push(T_CS!("\\lx@copy@provides@end"));
3724  Tokens::new(wrapped)
3725}
3726
3727/// Does a stand-in's copy, read now, defer code to the document through `seam` (`\AtBeginDocument`,
3728/// `\AtEndDocument`, etoolbox's `\AtEndPreamble`/`\AfterEndPreamble`/`\AfterEndDocument`, lthooks' `\AddToHook`)? Then
3729/// the code is not registered: it is the class's or style's document-time machinery, which the binding has taken over
3730/// — layout checks against the class's page dimensions (icml2024.sty's "The page layout violates the ICML style",
3731/// 2407.02750), validations of the class's own setters the document called as the binding's (PoS.cls "\abstract{...}
3732/// should appear before \begin{document}", 1001.3214; ieeeaccess.cls's `\EOD`, 2007.03025; aipproc.cls's
3733/// `\layoutstyle`, astro-ph0105128), patches of the kernel's `\enddocument` (aastex7.cls:459, whose
3734/// `\rvtx@enddocument@patch` reads up to `\@checkend` and ran off the file: 2504.00922, 2605.15371, 2607.03848). On the
3735/// 2026-10-17 census sample no Error was fixed by such code. Logged once per copy and seam, at Info.
3736pub fn copy_provides_defers(seam: &str) -> bool {
3737  let id = crate::definition::origin::copy_provides();
3738  if id == 0 {
3739    return false;
3740  }
3741  let key = arena::pin(seam);
3742  let mut state = state_mut!();
3743  if let Some(copy) = state.copy_provides_copies.get_mut(id as usize - 1)
3744    && copy.deferred.insert(key)
3745  {
3746    let file = copy.file.clone();
3747    drop(state);
3748    Info!(
3749      "provide",
3750      seam,
3751      s!(
3752        "The paper's {file} defers code to the document ({seam}); not registered: its binding stands in for that code"
3753      )
3754    );
3755  }
3756  true
3757}
3758
3759/// Is `token` one of LaTeX's document hooks (`\@begindocumenthook`, `\@enddocumenthook`), which a stand-in's copy read
3760/// now writes — `\g@addto@macro`, ltxgrid's `\prepdef`, a `\def` —, so that the write is document-time code, which the
3761/// copy does not register, as it does not its `\AtBeginDocument` code ([`copy_provides_defers`], Info)? ltxgrid's
3762/// `\prepdef\@begindocumenthook{\open@column@one\@ne\set@colht…}` (qm2008_abs.cls:3233, snp.cls; REVTeX 4 copies
3763/// routed to the revtex4 binding) ran its page grid over the binding's names at `\begin{document}`: 7 "Missing number"
3764/// Warnings in 0805.2253, 0806.0481, 0805.4759.
3765pub fn copy_provides_defers_hook_write(token: &Token) -> bool {
3766  crate::definition::origin::copy_provides() != 0
3767    && token.with_cs_name(|cs| matches!(cs, "\\@begindocumenthook" | "\\@enddocumenthook"))
3768    && token.with_cs_name(copy_provides_defers)
3769}
3770
3771/// Put lthooks' code registration (`\AddToHook code`, the inner macro latex.ltx's `\AddToHook` hands the hook name,
3772/// label and code: `\hook_gput_code:nnn{#1}{#2}{#3}`) under `\lx@copy@provides@AddToHook`, which registers nothing
3773/// ([`copy_provides_defers`]), while `on` — a stand-in's copy is read — and back after. The kernel's
3774/// own meaning is kept as `\lx@copy@provides@saved@AddToHook`. No-op where the format has no hooks or no seam is
3775/// defined.
3776pub fn swap_copy_provides_hook_seam(on: bool) {
3777  let seam = T_CS!("\\AddToHook code");
3778  let saved = T_CS!("\\lx@copy@provides@saved@AddToHook");
3779  let wrapper = T_CS!("\\lx@copy@provides@AddToHook");
3780  let set = |token: &Token, meaning: Stored| {
3781    let key = token.pin_cs_name();
3782    state_mut!().assign_internal(TableName::Meaning, key, meaning, Some(Scope::Global));
3783  };
3784  if on {
3785    if let (Some(kernel), Some(wrap)) = (lookup_meaning(&seam), lookup_meaning(&wrapper)) {
3786      set(&saved, kernel);
3787      set(&seam, wrap);
3788    }
3789  } else if let Some(kernel) = lookup_meaning(&saved) {
3790    set(&seam, kernel);
3791    set(&saved, Stored::None);
3792  }
3793}
3794
3795/// Begin a stand-in binding's load ([`crate::definition::origin::StandInLoadGuard`]): no name of its own assigned yet
3796/// ([`State::stand_in_loads`]); a stand-in it loads records its own apart.
3797pub fn begin_stand_in_load() {
3798  let mut state = state_mut!();
3799  state.stand_in_loads.push(Vec::new());
3800  state.stand_in_load_files.push(Vec::new());
3801}
3802
3803/// A stand-in binding's own code loads `file` ([`State::stand_in_load_files`]).
3804pub fn note_stand_in_file(file: &str) {
3805  if let Some(files) = state_mut!().stand_in_load_files.last_mut() {
3806    files.push(file.to_string());
3807  }
3808}
