3810/// The files the stand-in binding loaded last loaded from its own code ([`State::stand_in_last_files`]).
3811pub fn stand_in_built_on() -> Vec<String> { state!().stand_in_last_files.clone() }
3812
3813/// End the stand-in binding's load [`begin_stand_in_load`] began: its names are the last load's
3814/// ([`State::stand_in_last_load`]), and, when the stand-in binding loading it requested it in its own code
3815/// (`into_outer`, `StandInLoadGuard::loaded_by`), that binding's too.
3816pub fn end_stand_in_load(into_outer: bool) {
3817  let mut state = state_mut!();
3818  let files = state.stand_in_load_files.pop().unwrap_or_default();
3819  if let Some(load) = state.stand_in_loads.pop() {
3820    // (a stand-in its own code loads is part of its command set: the diagrams binding's `diagrams.tex`, whose names
3821    // are Paul Taylor's package's, hep-ph9609379; not one a file it loads requests — OmniBus's aas_macros under
3822    // asme2ej, 2309.12017, or nature-pre, 2102.01782, 2111.09589, swelled their sets from 22 and 3 names to 87 and 68)
3823    if into_outer && let Some(outer) = state.stand_in_loads.last_mut() {
3824      outer.extend(load.iter().copied());
3825    }
3826    state.stand_in_last_load = load;
3827    state.stand_in_last_files = files;
3828  }
3829}
3830
3831/// Every name the latest stand-in binding's own code assigned ([`State::stand_in_last_load`]).
3832pub fn stand_in_last_load_names() -> rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr> {
3833  state!().stand_in_last_load.iter().copied().collect()
3834}
3835
3836/// The public names (letters only: no `@`) the latest stand-in binding's own code assigned ([`State::stand_in_last_load`]).
3837pub fn stand_in_public_names() -> rustc_hash::FxHashSet<String> {
3838  state!()
3839    .stand_in_last_load
3840    .iter()
3841    .map(|key| arena::to_string(*key))
3842    .filter(|name| {
3843      name.strip_prefix('\\').is_some_and(|letters| {
3844        !letters.is_empty() && letters.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic())
3845      })
3846    })
3847    .collect()
3848}
3849
3850/// Begin a provide-only load: no name assigned in it yet.
3851pub fn reset_provide_only_assigned() {
3852  let mut state = state_mut!();
3853  state.provide_only_assigned.clear();
3854  state.provide_only_loaded.clear();
3855}
3856
3857/// End a provide-only load: the files first loaded during it (but the fallback class itself) loaded only what they
3858/// could provide, so they are marked unloaded again — `<file>_loaded`, its `.ltxml_loaded` twin, `_load_attempted` and
3859/// the kernel's `\ver@<file>` — and a later `\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage` of one loads it whole
3860/// (`\documentstyle[graphics,myjunkx]{article}\usepackage{graphicx}`: graphicx's keyval `\includegraphics`).
3861pub fn end_provide_only() {
3862  let files = std::mem::take(&mut state_mut!().provide_only_loaded);
3863  for file in files {
3864    if file == "OmniBus.cls" {
3865      continue;
3866    }
3867    mark_unloaded(&file);
3868  }
3869}
3870
3871/// Mark `file` (`amsthm.sty`) as not loaded, globally: its `<file>_loaded`, the `.ltxml_loaded` twin, the raw load's
3872/// `_raw_loaded` and `_load_attempted` are cleared and the kernel's `\ver@<file>` and `\opt@<file>` are undefined, so
3873/// `\@ifpackageloaded` is false, no option clash is checked, and a later `\usepackage`/`\RequirePackage` loads it
3874/// again — the binding and the raw file it reads. The definitions it made stay in force. Used when a load provided
3875/// only part of a file ([`end_provide_only`]) and for a binding's borrowed package and the files it loaded
3876/// (`binding::content::require_package_borrowed`).
3877pub fn mark_unloaded(file: &str) {
3878  for flag in ["_loaded", ".ltxml_loaded", "_raw_loaded", "_load_attempted"] {
3879    if lookup_bool(&s!("{file}{flag}")) {
3880      assign_value(&s!("{file}{flag}"), false, Some(Scope::Global));
3881    }
3882  }
3883  for kernel in ["ver", "opt"] {
3884    assign_meaning(
3885      &T_CS!(s!("\\{kernel}@{file}")),
3886      Stored::None,
3887      Some(Scope::Global),
3888    );
3889  }
3890}
3891
3892/// Begin a borrow (`binding::content::require_package_borrowed`): no file loaded by it yet. Returns the list of an
3893/// enclosing borrow, which [`end_borrow`] puts back.
3894pub fn begin_borrow() -> Vec<String> { std::mem::take(&mut state_mut!().borrowed_loaded) }
3895
3896/// End a borrow: the files first marked loaded during it (`borrowed_loaded`) are marked unloaded ([`mark_unloaded`]),
3897/// and the enclosing borrow's list is put back.
3898pub fn end_borrow(outer: Vec<String>) {
3899  let files = std::mem::replace(&mut state_mut!().borrowed_loaded, outer);
3900  for file in files {
3901    mark_unloaded(&file);
3902  }
3903}
3904
3905/// Has the document unloaded `file` (`amsthm.sty`), which a class binding marked loaded on its class's behalf, by
3906/// letting its `\ver@<file>` to `\relax` or `\undefined`? LaTeX's loaded test is that macro (latex.ltx `\@ifl@aded`:
3907/// `\expandafter\ifx\csname ver@#2.#1\endcsname\relax`, true for both), which a binding load defines (content.rs, Perl
3908/// Package.pm:2326). The rule is narrow, as floatrow's marks are (user ruling 2026-10-14: no general loader `\ver@`
3909/// rule; floatrow_sty.rs): only a file a binding recorded as `<file>:class_marked` — ams_support_sty.rs for amsart's
3910/// `\@namedef{ver@amsthm.sty}` (amsart.cls:41-42; amsproc, amsbook alike) — is so taken back; every other file keeps
3911/// the loader's own record. The amsart idiom is `\expandafter\let\csname ver@amsthm.sty\endcsname\relax` before
3912/// `\usepackage{amsthm}` (1510.08833, 1704.05671, 1707.00258, 1611.00843, 2106.07418, 2304.10644). Perl's loader reads
3913/// only `<file>_loaded` (Package.pm:2327-2329), KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #720.
3914pub fn unmarked_by_document(file: &str) -> bool {
3915  if !lookup_bool(&s!("{file}:class_marked")) || !lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_loaded")) {
3916    return false;
3917  }
3918  match lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{file}"))) {
3919    None => true,
3920    Some(ver) => lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\relax")).is_some_and(|relax| ver == relax),
3921  }
3922}
3923
3924/// Is `file` (`amsmath.sty`, `memoir.cls`) loaded, as LaTeX asks it (`\@ifpackageloaded`, `\@ifclassloaded`: latex.ltx
3925/// `\@ifl@aded`, `\ver@<file>` defined and not `\relax`)? One answer on every load path: a binding's load and a raw
3926/// file's define the macro (Package.pm:2343, :2393), and so do a class's or a document's own marks (floatrow.sty:34,
3927/// `\@namedef{ver@…}`) and their removals (fontenc.sty's tail, a document's `\let\ver@amsthm.sty\relax`). Perl's
3928/// `\@ifl@aded` reads the loader's records (latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:946-953), which a binding may clear so that the
3929/// next request runs it again ([`load_recorded`]; aa.cls.ltxml:57): OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #1015.
3930pub fn file_loaded(file: &str) -> bool {
3931  lookup_meaning(&T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{file}"))).is_some_and(|ver| {
3932    !matches!(ver, Stored::None)
3933      && Some(&ver) != lookup_meaning(&crate::token::TOKEN_RELAX).as_ref()
3934  })
3935}
3936
3937/// `\@ifpackageloaded{name}` ([`file_loaded`]).
3938pub fn package_loaded(name: &str) -> bool { file_loaded(&s!("{name}.sty")) }
3939
3940/// `\@ifclassloaded{name}` ([`file_loaded`]).
3941pub fn class_loaded(name: &str) -> bool { file_loaded(&s!("{name}.cls")) }
3942
3943/// Has the loader recorded a load of `file`: its binding's or a stand-in's (`<file>_loaded`) or its raw TeX's
3944/// ([`raw_loaded`])? The loader's own question (Package.pm:2328-2330, :2363; `DefAutoload`, :1096), not LaTeX's
3945/// ([`file_loaded`]): it says whether the definitions were read here, and a binding clears it to be read again.
3946pub fn load_recorded(file: &str) -> bool { lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_loaded")) || raw_loaded(file) }
3947
3948/// Did the binding of this very name load `file` (`<file>.ltxml_loaded`, Package.pm:2332), not a stand-in's?
3949pub fn binding_loaded(file: &str) -> bool { lookup_bool(&s!("{file}.ltxml_loaded")) }
3950
3951/// Was `file`'s raw TeX read (`<file>_raw_loaded`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN #23)?
3952pub fn raw_loaded(file: &str) -> bool { lookup_bool(&s!("{file}_raw_loaded")) }
3953
3954/// Record `file` loaded for a binding that stands in for it (thmtools for thm-patch.sty, aipproc for aipxfm.sty), as
3955/// the original's own load does: the loader's record, so a later request is a no-op, and `\ver@<file>` (`\fmtversion`,
3956/// Package.pm:2343) unless the file is marked already.
3957pub fn mark_loaded(file: &str) {
3958  assign_value(&s!("{file}_loaded"), true, Some(Scope::Global));
3959  if !file_loaded(file)
3960    && let Some(fmtversion) = lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\fmtversion"))
3961  {
3962    assign_meaning(&T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{file}")), fmtversion, Some(Scope::Global));
3963  }
3964}
3965
3966/// Take back a binding's load of `file` that its original does not make (revtex4_support's hyperref, which revtex4-2.cls
3967/// never loads): its record, so the document's own request runs the binding again, and `\ver@<file>`, so
3968/// `\@ifpackageloaded` is false until then. The definitions stay; the binding's other records too ([`mark_unloaded`]
3969/// clears them all).
3970pub fn unmark_loaded(file: &str) {
3971  assign_value(&s!("{file}_loaded"), Stored::None, Some(Scope::Global));
3972  assign_meaning(
3973    &T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{file}")),
3974    Stored::None,
3975    Some(Scope::Global),
3976  );
3977}
3978
3979/// Run `read` with `file` marked loaded in the current group, its record and `\ver@<file>`, where it is not loaded
3980/// and not recorded (a recorded load the document unmarked keeps its record): cleveref's binding reads cleveref.sty as
3981/// if amsmath were loaded (Perl cleveref.sty.ltxml:23-27).
3982pub fn with_pretended_load<R>(file: &str, read: impl FnOnce() -> R) -> R {
3983  if file_loaded(file) || load_recorded(file) {
3984    return read();
3985  }
3986  let (record, ver) = (s!("{file}_loaded"), T_CS!(s!("\\ver@{file}")));
3987  let was = lookup_meaning(&ver);
3988  assign_value(&record, true, Some(Scope::Local));
3989  if let Some(fmtversion) = lookup_meaning(&T_CS!("\\fmtversion")) {
3990    assign_meaning(&ver, fmtversion, Some(Scope::Local));
3991  }
3992  let result = read();
3993  assign_value(&record, Stored::None, Some(Scope::Local));
3994  assign_meaning(&ver, was.unwrap_or(Stored::None), Some(Scope::Local));
3995  result
3996}
3997
3998/// Run `read`, a read of `file` that must not use it up (colordvi's of dvipsnam.def), and put `file`'s load records
3999/// back as they were.
4000pub fn keeping_load_records<R>(file: &str, read: impl FnOnce() -> R) -> R {
4001  let flags = ["_raw_loaded", "_loaded", "_load_attempted"].map(|flag| s!("{file}{flag}"));
4002  let was = flags.clone().map(|flag| lookup_bool(&flag));
4003  let result = read();
4004  for (flag, was) in flags.iter().zip(was) {
4005    assign_value(flag, was, Some(Scope::Global));
4006  }
4007  result
4008}
4009
4010/// Is `token`'s current meaning a fallback class's guess (`DefinitionOrigin::Fallback`), a kernel stub
4011/// (`DefinitionOrigin::Stub`) or a borrowed package's (`DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed`), which a document's own
4012/// `\newcommand` may replace?
4013pub fn is_fallback_meaning(token: &Token) -> bool {
4014  let state = state!();
4015  !state.fallback_meanings.is_empty() && state.fallback_meanings.contains_key(&meaning_key(token))
4016}
4017
4018/// The origin of `token`'s current meaning when it is a fallback guess, a kernel stub or a borrowed package's
4019/// ([`is_fallback_meaning`]): `Fallback`, `Stub` or `Borrowed`, whatever kind of definition it is (only macros record
4020/// their own origin, `Definition::get_origin`; amsthm's proof is a constructor).
4021pub fn fallback_meaning_origin(
4022  token: &Token,
4023) -> Option<crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin> {
4024  let state = state!();
4025  if state.fallback_meanings.is_empty() {
4026    return None;
4027  }
4028  state.fallback_meanings.get(&meaning_key(token)).copied()
4029}
4030
4031/// A borrowing binding's claim on the borrowed names its real package defines too: their meanings no longer carry the
4032/// borrowed mark (`DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed`, [`fallback_meaning_origin`]), so they are the binding's own, as if it
4033/// had defined them again at its own origin, and a later file's `\newcommand`/`\newenvironment` of them is refused
4034/// as LaTeX refuses it ("Command … already defined"), not honoured. ntheorem.sty's `[amsthm]` option defines
4035/// `\proofname` and `{proof}` (ntheorem.sty:668-711), which its binding takes from the borrowed amsthm; theorem.sty's
4036/// `\newskip\theorempreskipamount` (theorem.sty:122-123) likewise. A name whose meaning is not borrowed is left
4037/// alone.
4038pub fn claim_borrowed_meanings(names: &[&str]) {
4039  use crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin;
4040  if state!().fallback_meanings.is_empty() {
4041    return;
4042  }
4043  let keys: Vec<SymStr> = names
4044    .iter()
4045    .map(|name| meaning_key(&crate::T_CS!(*name)))
4046    .collect();
4047  let mut state = state_mut!();
4048  for key in keys {
4049    if state.fallback_meanings.get(&key) == Some(&DefinitionOrigin::Borrowed) {
4050      state.fallback_meanings.remove(&key);
4051    }
4052  }
4053}
4054
4055// SAFETY
4056// any method which does not return a borrowed piece of data should be package-level
4057// so that the global singleton State can get locked+unlocked during the same call
4058// thus entirely AVOIDING possible runtime panics due to RefCell lock races.
4059// TODO: Should this be a prelude?
4060
4061/// assigns a `Stored` value at the given key and scope
4062/// Direct mirror of Perl's free-function form
4063/// `LaTeXML::Core::State::assign_internal($STATE, $table, $key, $value, $scope)`
4064/// (Core/State.pm L140). Bypasses every dialect / lock / let-chase / admission
4065/// layer Rust has accreted on top of the table mutation; used by the dump
4066/// loader (Core/Dumper.pm `V/Cc/Mc/Sc/Lc/Uc/Dc/Im/I/Lt`) so the dump replay
4067/// matches Perl exactly: one record == one `assign_internal` call.
4068pub fn assign_internal<T: Into<Stored>>(
4069  table_name: TableName,
4070  key: SymStr,
4071  value: T,
4072  scope: Option<Scope>,
4073) {
4074  state_mut!().assign_internal(table_name, key, value.into(), scope);
4075}
4076
4077/// Bind `key` to `value` in the value table — Perl's `AssignValue`.
4078///
4079/// [`Scope`] decides how long the binding lasts: [`Scope::Local`] expires with
4080/// the enclosing TeX group, [`Scope::Global`] does not, [`Scope::Named`] applies
4081/// only while that scope is activated, and [`Scope::InPlace`] rebinds at the
4082/// frame the value was last bound in. Passing `None` takes the state's current
4083/// default. Read back with [`lookup_value`], or one of the typed
4084/// [`lookup_string`] / [`lookup_number`] / [`lookup_bool`] accessors.
4085pub fn assign_value<T: Into<Stored>, S: Into<Option<Scope>>>(key: &str, value: T, scope: S) {
4086  state_mut!().assign_value(key, value, scope)
4087}
4088
4089/// Assigns `value` to `key` globally and past the open assignment journal's rollback (Rust-only), as TeX never takes back
4090/// what it wrote to a file: no speculative run's undoing (a frontmatter rehearsal, a label's ink run) takes it back. Only
4091/// for a record TeX writes to a file and reads back on a later run — an aux-file label, imsart.sty's `\ead` `\safelabel`
4092/// that its `\printead` reads —, never for a value a rehearsal's own reading decides: that one must roll back with it.
4093/// OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #986.
4094pub fn assign_value_past_rollback(key: &str, value: impl Into<Stored>) {
4095  let mut state = state_mut!();
4096  state.assign_value(key, value, Some(Scope::Global));
4097  let key = arena::pin(key);
4098  if let Some(journal) = state.journal.as_mut() {
4099    journal.saved.remove(&(TableName::Value, key));
4100  }
4101}
4102
4103/// assigns a `Stored` value 'inplace': replaces the front value in whatever frame
4104/// it was originally assigned in, without recording an undo entry.
4105/// This matches Perl's `assignValue(key, value, 'inplace')`.
4106/// Used for MODE changes in enter_horizontal (switches mode without creating a new binding).
4107pub fn assign_value_inplace(key: &str, value: impl Into<Stored>) {
4108  assign_value_inplace_sym(arena::pin(key), value)
4109}
4110/// Sym-keyed variant of `assign_value_inplace` — skip the per-call
4111/// `arena::pin(key)` for hot callers with a pre-pinned SymStr.
4112pub fn assign_value_inplace_sym(key_sym: SymStr, value: impl Into<Stored>) {
4113  let value = value.into();
4114  let state = &mut *state_mut!();
4115  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
4116  let table = &mut state.value;
4117  if let Some(vvec) = table.get_mut(&key_sym)
4118    && let Some(front) = vvec.front_mut()
4119  {
4120    *front = value;
4121    return;
4122  }
4123  // If the value was never assigned, push globally (matching Perl behavior)
4124  let vvec = table.entry(key_sym).or_default();
4125  vvec.push_front(value);
4126  // Find the locked frame and record the undo there
4127  for frame in &mut state.undo {
4128    if frame.locked {
4129      frame.table_mut(TableName::Value).insert(key_sym, 1);
4130      break;
4131    }
4132  }
4133}
4134
4135/// A save-frame bookkeeping binding (`groupInitiator`, `BOUND_MODE`, the
4136/// frame id, an environment's group code…): always local to the current
4137/// frame, exempt from `\globaldefs` and the `\global` prefix — tex.web §1214
4138/// vs §274. See `State::assign_local_unconditional`.
4139pub fn assign_frame_value_sym<T: Into<Stored>>(key: SymStr, value: T) {
4140  state_mut!().assign_local_unconditional(TableName::Value, key, value.into());
4141}
4142
4143/// [`assign_frame_value_sym`] with a string key.
4144pub fn assign_frame_value<T: Into<Stored>>(key: &str, value: T) {
4145  assign_frame_value_sym(arena::pin(key), value);
4146}
4147
4148/// assigns a `Stored` value at the given (arena ticket!) key and scope
4149pub fn assign_value_sym<T: Into<Stored>, S: Into<Option<Scope>>>(key: SymStr, value: T, scope: S) {
4150  let value = value.into();
4151  let scope = scope.into();
4152  state_mut!().assign_internal(TableName::Value, key, value, scope);
4153}
4154
4155/// inline lookup_value after which globally assign an empty Tokens() to undo
4156pub fn remove_value(key: &str) -> Option<Stored> { remove_value_sym(arena::pin(key)) }
4157
4158/// `remove_value` variant for hot call sites with a pre-pinned SymStr (see
4159/// `crate::pin!`) — added for `after_assignment`, which fires on every
4160/// `\def`/`\let`/register assignment.
4161pub fn remove_value_sym(key_sym: SymStr) -> Option<Stored> {
4162  let mut state = state_mut!();
4163  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
4164  match state.value.get_mut(&key_sym) {
4165    None => None,
4166    Some(vvec) => match vvec.front_mut() {
4167      None | Some(&mut Stored::None) => None,
4168      Some(found) => Some(std::mem::take(found)),
4169    },
4170  }
4171}
4172/// Replaces the value in question with `Stored::None` (see `checkin_value` for returning it)
4173pub fn checkout_value(key: &str) -> Option<Stored> {
4174  let key = arena::pin(key);
4175  let mut state = state_mut!();
4176  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key);
4177  match state.value.get_mut(&key) {
4178    None => None,
4179    Some(vvec) => vvec.front_mut().map(std::mem::take),
4180  }
4181}
4182/// Returns a value into its `Stored::None` placeholder (see `checkout_value` for taking it)
4183pub fn checkin_value(key: &str, value: Stored) {
4184  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
4185  let mut state = state_mut!();
4186  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
4187  match state.value.get_mut(&key_sym) {
4188    None => {
4189      // Key was never assigned — silently ignore the checkin
4190      emit_warn(
4191        "internal",
4192        "state",
4193        &format!("checkin_value called for unknown key '{key}'"),
4194      );
4195    },
4196    Some(vvec) => match vvec.front_mut() {
4197      None => {
4198        emit_warn(
4199          "internal",
4200          "state",
4201          &format!("checkin_value called with empty value stack for key '{key}'"),
4202        );
4203      },
4204      Some(found) => {
4205        match found {
4206          Stored::None => std::mem::replace(found, value),
4207          _ => panic!("checkin_value should only be called after checkout_value"),
4208        };
4209      },
4210    },
4211  }
4212}
4213/// manage a (global) list of values
4214pub fn push_value<T: Into<Stored>>(key: &str, value: T) -> Result<()> {
4215  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
4216  let value = value.into();
4217  // Capture any BUG-path message, but raise the Error! *after* the state_mut!()
4218  // borrow is dropped — Error! reads MAX_ERRORS, and a live mutable borrow there
4219  // panics "RefCell already mutably borrowed" (tikz-cd 2001.08973).
4220  let bug: Option<String> = {
4221    let mut state = state_mut!();
4222    state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
4223    if !state.value.contains_key(&key_sym) {
4224      state.assign_internal(
4225        TableName::Value,
4226        key_sym,
4227        Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::new()),
4228        Some(Scope::Global),
4229      );
4230    }
4231    match state.value.get_mut(&key_sym).unwrap().front_mut() {
4232      Some(&mut Stored::VecDequeStored(ref mut front)) => {
4233        front.push_back(value);
4234        None
4235      },
4236      // auto-vivify, if None
4237      Some(ref mut field) if matches!(field, Stored::None) => {
4238        let mut new_vdq = VecDeque::new();
4239        new_vdq.push_back(value);
4240        **field = Stored::VecDequeStored(new_vdq);
4241        None
4242      },
4243      // Convert Strings (immutable array) to VecDequeStored for push — matches Perl auto-vivification
4244      Some(ref mut field) if matches!(field, Stored::Strings(_)) => {
4245        let existing: VecDeque<Stored> = if let Stored::Strings(strings) = &**field {
4246          strings.iter().map(|s| Stored::String(*s)).collect()
4247        } else {
4248          VecDeque::new()
4249        };
4250        let mut new_vdq = existing;
4251        new_vdq.push_back(value);
4252        **field = Stored::VecDequeStored(new_vdq);
4253        None
4254      },
4255      other => Some(s!(
4256        "BUG: Tried to push_value into an unsupported Stored field! Field was: {other:?}"
4257      )),
4258    }
4259  };
4260  if let Some(message) = bug {
4261    // Lowercase category for consistency with engine convention.
4262    Error!("state", "Stored", message);
4263  }
4264  Ok(())
4265}
4266/// pops the last value in a named `Stored::VecDequeStored` queue, if any
4267pub fn pop_value(key: &str) -> Result<Option<Stored>> {
4268  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
4269  // Compute the pop result under the borrow, then raise the BUG Error! *after*
4270  // dropping it — Error! reads MAX_ERRORS, which panics under a live mutable
4271  // borrow (mirrors push_value; tikz-cd 2001.08973).
4272  let popped: std::result::Result<Option<Stored>, ()> = {
4273    let mut state = state_mut!();
4274    state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
4275    if !state.value.contains_key(&key_sym) {
4276      state.assign_internal(
4277        TableName::Value,
4278        key_sym,
4279        Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::new()),
4280        Some(Scope::Global),
4281      );
4282    }
4283    if let Some(&mut Stored::VecDequeStored(ref mut front)) =
4284      state.value.get_mut(&key_sym).unwrap().front_mut()
4285    {
4286      Ok(front.pop_back())
4287    } else {
4288      Err(())
4289    }
4290  };
4291  match popped {
4292    Ok(v) => Ok(v),
4293    Err(()) => {
4294      Error!(
4295        "State",
4296        "Stored",
4297        "BUG: Tried to pop_value from a non-vecdeque value key!"
4298      );
4299      Ok(None)
4300    },
4301  }
4302}
4303/// Check if the Value table contains a given key
4304pub fn has_value(key: &str) -> bool { has_value_sym(arena::pin(key)) }
4305/// Sym-keyed variant of `has_value` — avoids the per-call `arena::pin(key)`.
4306pub fn has_value_sym(key_sym: SymStr) -> bool {
4307  match state!().value.get(&key_sym) {
4308    None => false,
4309    Some(list) => match list.front() {
4310      None => false,
4311      Some(v) => !matches!(v, &Stored::None),
4312    },
4313  }
4314}
4315/// Pushes Tokens into a `Stored::Tokens` value when defined,
4316/// or assigns when new.
4317pub fn push_tokens(key: &str, value: Tokens) {
4318  let mut state = state_mut!();
4319  match state.lookup_value_mut(key) {
4320    Some(Stored::Tokens(tks)) => tks.unlist_mut().extend(value.unlist()),
4321    // Perl `pushValue` (State.pm:218-223) creates a missing list globally: a `\vadjust` inside a group
4322    // (`Alpha {\vadjust{Bravo}} Charlie.`) kept its material past the group's end, where a local list was lost
4323    // (guard `box_primitives::vadjust`).
4324    None | Some(Stored::None) => {
4325      state.assign_value(key, Stored::Tokens(value), Some(Scope::Global))
4326    },
4327    Some(other) => panic!("Can only push_tokens into a Stored::Tokens, but got {other:?}"),
4328  }
4329}
4330
4331/// Appends a digested box to the `Stored::VecDigested` list under `key`, creating the list globally when missing (as
4332/// [`push_tokens`], Perl `pushValue`, State.pm:218-223).
4333pub fn push_digested(key: &str, value: Digested) {
4334  let mut state = state_mut!();
4335  match state.lookup_value_mut(key) {
4336    Some(Stored::VecDigested(list)) => list.push(value),
4337    None | Some(Stored::None) => {
4338      state.assign_value(key, Stored::VecDigested(vec![value]), Some(Scope::Global))
4339    },
4340    Some(other) => panic!("Can only push_digested into a Stored::VecDigested, but got {other:?}"),
4341  }
4342}
4343
4344/// Take the list [`push_tokens`] or [`push_digested`] has built under `key`, leaving it empty in place; `None` when
4345/// there is none.
4346pub fn take_pushed(key: &str) -> Option<Stored> {
4347  match state_mut!().lookup_value_mut(key) {
4348    Some(Stored::Tokens(tks)) => Some(Stored::Tokens(std::mem::take(tks))),
4349    Some(Stored::VecDigested(list)) => Some(Stored::VecDigested(std::mem::take(list))),
4350    _ => None,
4351  }
4352}
4353
4354/// Make `value` the list under `key` again, in place: what [`take_pushed`] took, the list built since dropped.
4355pub fn restore_pushed(key: &str, value: Option<Stored>) {
4356  let mut state = state_mut!();
4357  match (state.lookup_value_mut(key), value) {
4358    (Some(slot @ (Stored::Tokens(_) | Stored::VecDigested(_))), Some(value)) => *slot = value,
4359    (Some(slot @ (Stored::Tokens(_) | Stored::VecDigested(_))), None) => *slot = Stored::None,
4360    (None | Some(Stored::None), Some(value)) => state.assign_value(key, value, Some(Scope::Global)),
4361    _ => {},
4362  }
4363}
4364
4365/// The value bound to `key`, or `None` when nothing is bound — Perl's
4366/// `LookupValue`.
4367///
4368/// Returns whatever [`Stored`] variant was assigned, so a caller that knows the
4369/// type usually wants [`lookup_string`] / [`lookup_number`] / [`lookup_bool`]
4370/// instead. Clones the value; [`with_value`] lends it when inspecting is enough.
4371pub fn lookup_value(key: &str) -> Option<Stored> { state!().lookup_value(key).cloned() }
4372pub fn with_value<R, FnR>(key: &str, caller: FnR) -> R
4373where FnR: FnOnce(Option<&Stored>) -> R {
4374  caller(state!().lookup_value(key))
4375}
4376/// Sym-keyed variant of `with_value` — avoids the per-call `arena::pin(key)`.
4377pub fn with_value_sym<R, FnR>(key: SymStr, caller: FnR) -> R
4378where FnR: FnOnce(Option<&Stored>) -> R {
4379  caller(state!().lookup_value_sym(key))
4380}
4381pub fn with_value_mut<R, FnR>(key: &str, caller: FnR) -> R
4382where FnR: FnOnce(Option<&mut Stored>) -> R {
4383  caller(state_mut!().lookup_value_mut(key))
4384}
4385/// `\fontdimen<p>` of the font whose parameter array is stored under `key`,
4386/// in sp (Perl's `$$fontinfo{data}`, TeX_Fonts.pool.ltxml:131-137); `None`
4387/// when the font has no array yet or `p` lies past its end.
4388pub fn font_parameter(key: &str, p: usize) -> Option<i64> {
4389  with_value(key, |value| match value {
4390    Some(Stored::FontDimens(params)) => p
4391      .checked_sub(1)
4392      .and_then(|index| params.borrow().get(index).copied()),
4393    _ => None,
4394  })
4395}
4396
4397/// Give the font whose array is stored under `key` its parameters, in sp:
4398/// Perl's `\font` filling a new font's `$$fontinfo{data}` from its metric
4399/// (TeX_Fonts.pool.ltxml:108-117). Unbound by any group, as a font is.
4400pub fn assign_font_parameters(key: &str, params: Vec<i64>) {
4401  assign_value_inplace(key, Stored::FontDimens(Rc::new(RefCell::new(params))));
4402}
4403
4404/// Set `\fontdimen<p>` of the font whose array is stored under `key`; a
4405/// parameter past its end grows it with zeros (TeX_Fonts.pool.ltxml:138-146,
4406/// tex.web §580), and a font without one gets an array of its own — as TeX's
4407/// `\nullfont` has parameters, where Perl's setter does nothing for a font
4408/// without data (:141). Written in
4409/// place and so global, as every font parameter assignment is (tex.web §1253):
4410/// the array is created unbound by any group, whatever `\globaldefs`. Returns
4411/// false, assigning nothing, for parameter 0 or one past TeX's font memory
4412/// ([`font::FONT_MEM_SIZE`]).
4413pub fn set_font_parameter(key: &str, p: usize, value: i64) -> bool {
4414  let Some(index) = p
4415    .checked_sub(1)
4416    .filter(|&index| index < font::FONT_MEM_SIZE)
4417  else {
4418    return false;
4419  };
4420  let set = |params: &mut Vec<i64>| {
4421    if params.len() <= index {
4422      params.resize(index + 1, 0);
4423    }
4424    params[index] = value;
4425  };
4426  let existing = with_value(key, |stored| match stored {
4427    Some(Stored::FontDimens(params)) => Some(Rc::clone(params)),
4428    _ => None,
4429  });
4430  match existing {
4431    Some(params) => {
4432      // (a font's parameter is written in loops: the key's pinned only for a journal or a watch)
4433      if WRITES_WATCHED.get() {
4434        state_mut!().journal_touch(TableName::Value, arena::pin(key));
4435      }
4436      set(&mut params.borrow_mut())
4437    },
4438    None => {
4439      let mut params = Vec::new();
4440      set(&mut params);
4441      assign_font_parameters(key, params);
4442    },
4443  }
4444  true
4445}
4446
4447/// A binding's document-wide state, one value of the Value table: bound globally, as the `\global` macros and registers
4448/// of the package it stands for are, so a rollback (`begin_assignment_journal`: a rehearsal, a title-page check) puts it
4449/// back, where a thread-local would keep what the discarded run did (`FRONTMATTER_SINGLE_ENGINE_PLAN.md` "What a rollback
4450/// leaves"). It changes in place, unless an open journal holds it: the first change under a journal works on a copy, so a
4451/// list a binding grows costs no more than a thread-local's outside one.
4452pub struct DocumentState<T> {
4453  key:   &'static str,
4454  state: std::marker::PhantomData<fn() -> T>,
4455}
4456
4457impl<T: Clone + Default + 'static> DocumentState<T> {
4458  pub const fn new(key: &'static str) -> Self {
4459    Self {
4460      key,
4461      state: std::marker::PhantomData,
4462    }
4463  }
4464
4465  /// Read the state; the default where nothing was changed yet.
4466  pub fn read<R>(&self, read: impl FnOnce(&T) -> R) -> R {
4467    let held = with_value(self.key, |stored| match stored {
4468      Some(Stored::Opaque(held)) => Some(Rc::clone(held)),
4469      _ => None,
4470    });
4471    match held.as_ref().and_then(|held| held.downcast_ref::<T>()) {
4472      Some(state) => read(state),
4473      None => read(&T::default()),
4474    }
4475  }
4476
4477  /// Change the state; `change` must not reach this same state again.
4478  pub fn update<R>(&self, change: impl FnOnce(&mut T) -> R) -> R {
4479    let key = arena::pin(self.key);
4480    let held = {
4481      let state = &mut *state_mut!();
4482      state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key);
4483      state
4484        .value
4485        .get_mut(&key)
4486        .and_then(VecDeque::front_mut)
4487        .map(std::mem::take)
4488    };
4489    let bound = held.is_some();
4490    let mut own: Rc<dyn std::any::Any> = match held {
4491      Some(Stored::Opaque(held)) if held.is::<T>() => held,
4492      _ => Rc::new(T::default()),
4493    };
4494    if Rc::get_mut(&mut own).is_none() {
4495      let copy = own.downcast_ref::<T>().cloned().unwrap_or_default();
4496      own = Rc::new(copy);
4497    }
4498    let result = Rc::get_mut(&mut own)
4499      .and_then(|state| state.downcast_mut::<T>())
4500      .map(change)
4501      .expect("a state held once, of its type");
4502    if bound {
4503      assign_value_inplace_sym(key, Stored::Opaque(own));
4504    } else {
4505      assign_value_sym(key, Stored::Opaque(own), Some(Scope::Global));
4506    }
4507    result
4508  }
4509}
4510
4511/// Note for an open assignment journal that the box in the register `key` is about to have its dimension `property` set
4512/// (`\\wd`, `\\ht`, `\\dp`): a property of the box itself, which no binding's value holds. Also ends the watch held
4513/// ([`watch_state`]).
4514pub fn journal_box_dimension(key: &str, property: &'static str) {
4515  if !WRITES_WATCHED.get() {
4516    return;
4517  }
4518  set_watch(0);
4519  if !JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.get() {
4520    return;
4521  }
4522  let a_box = with_value(key, |stored| match stored {
4523    Some(Stored::Digested(a_box)) => Some(a_box.clone()),
4524    _ => None,
4525  });
4526  if let Some(a_box) = a_box
4527    && let Some(journal) = state_mut!().journal.as_mut()
4528  {
4529    let value = crate::BoxOps::get_property(&a_box, property)
4530      .as_deref()
4531      .cloned();
4532    journal.box_dimensions.push((a_box, property, value));
4533  }
4534}
4535
4536/// The input stream stored under `key` (`\\openin`'s), to read it or close it: a mouth changes in place, so under an open
4537/// assignment journal the first one to change it works on a copy of its own, in the stream's place, and a rollback gives the
4538/// document's back where it stood. A `\\read` in a rehearsal (a frontmatter field, a title page) took the document's next line.
4539pub fn input_stream_to_change(key: &str) -> Option<Rc<RefCell<mouth::Mouth>>> {
4540  let mouth = with_value(key, |stored| match stored {
4541    Some(Stored::Mouth(mouth)) => Some(Rc::clone(mouth)),
4542    _ => None,
4543  })?;
4544  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
4545  let copying = {
4546    let state = state!();
4547    JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.get()
4548      && state
4549        .journal
4550        .as_ref()
4551        .is_some_and(|journal| !journal.saved.contains_key(&(TableName::Value, key_sym)))
4552  };
4553  if !copying {
4554    return Some(mouth);
4555  }
4556  let copy = Rc::new(RefCell::new(mouth.borrow_mut().copy_for_a_rollback()));
4557  assign_value_inplace_sym(key_sym, Stored::Mouth(Rc::clone(&copy)));
4558  Some(copy)
4559}
4560
4561/// Undo-stack depth (open TeX groups) — pass-1 streaming telemetry.
4562pub fn undo_depth() -> usize { state!().undo.len() }
4563
4564/// The save-stack depth at which an interleaved build step began (`None` =
4565/// not building, or building after digestion the eager way).
4566#[thread_local]
4567static BUILD_FLOOR: std::cell::Cell<Option<usize>> = std::cell::Cell::new(None);
4568
4569/// Run a build step (a fragment's `Document::absorb`) that the streaming
4570/// driver interleaves with digestion. Eager Rust and Perl build AFTER
4571/// digestion, outside every group, so a constructor's local `assign_value`
4572/// (the aligning context's `ALIGNING_NODE`, a frontmatter store, a
4573/// counter) never pops; interleaved, the same binding lands in whichever
4574/// digestion group is open at the seam and is popped at that group's end —
4575/// tikz-network under tufte-book streamed with a stale root-level aligning
4576/// context re-exposed by `\end{fullwidth}`, stamping `ltx_align_left` on
4577/// chapters 4-5 and the appendices. Bindings made at the depth the step
4578/// began at are therefore global for the step; the frames the build itself
4579/// pushes above that depth (collected environment frames) keep their local
4580/// bindings and pop as in eager.
4581pub fn with_build_floor<R>(f: impl FnOnce() -> R) -> R {
4582  let previous = BUILD_FLOOR.replace(Some(undo_depth()));
4583  let result = f();
4584  BUILD_FLOOR.set(previous);
4585  result
4586}
4587
4588/// A bit of Perl "existence as truth" semantics mixed in with proper boolean lookup
4589pub fn lookup_bool(key: &str) -> bool { lookup_bool_sym(arena::pin(key)) }
4590
4591/// `lookup_bool` variant for hot call sites with a pre-pinned SymStr
4592/// (see `crate::pin!`). Skips the per-call `arena::pin(key)` hash
4593/// lookup — significant on every-expansion hot paths. `SymStr` is a
4594/// `u32` wrapper (Copy), so it passes by value — no borrow overhead.
4595pub fn lookup_bool_sym(key: SymStr) -> bool {
4596  let state = state!();
4597  match state.lookup_value_sym(key) {
4598    None => false,
4599    Some(v) => v.into(),
4600  }
4601}
4602
4603/// `lookup_string` variant using a pre-pinned SymStr key.
4604pub fn lookup_string_from_sym(key: SymStr) -> String {
4605  let state = state!();
4606  match state.lookup_value_sym(key) {
4607    None => String::new(),
4608    Some(v) => v.into(),
4609  }
4610}
4611/// like `lookup_value`, but casts the entry into a SymStr from the string interner
4612///  (`pin!("")` if None)
4613pub fn lookup_string_sym(key: &str) -> SymStr {
4614  let state = state!();
4615  match state.lookup_value(key) {
4616    None => pin!(""),
4617    Some(Stored::String(v)) => *v,
4618    Some(other) => arena::pin(other.to_string()),
4619  }
4620}
4621/// like `lookup_value`, but casts the entry into a String (empty if None)
4622pub fn lookup_string(key: &str) -> String {
4623  let state = state!();
4624  match state.lookup_value(key) {
4625    None => String::new(),
4626    // A list value has no scalar string form; return "" rather than leaking the
4627    // internal `VecDequeStored[…]`/`Strings` Debug repr (#315). Structural
4628    // access to list values is via the Rhai `LookupValue` binding, which
4629    // returns an array (mirroring Perl's `LookupValue` → arrayref).
4630    Some(v) if v.is_list() => String::new(),
4631    Some(v) => v.into(),
4632  }
4633}
4634/// like `lookup_value` but only recognizes Int, Bool and Number variants of Stored (default: 0)
4635pub fn lookup_int(key: &str) -> i64 { lookup_int_sym(arena::pin(key)) }
4636
4637/// Perl `Conditional.pm:61-62`: bump the global conditional counter and
4638/// return the new id for the `IfFrame` (see [`State::if_count`]).
4639pub fn next_if_id() -> i64 {
4640  let mut state = state_mut!();
4641  state.if_count += 1;
4642  state.if_count
4643}
4644/// Perl `unshiftValue('if_stack', …)` (Conditional.pm:63): open a conditional (see
4645/// [`State::if_stack`]).
4646pub fn push_if_frame(frame: Rc<RefCell<crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame>>) {
4647  state_mut!().if_stack.push(frame);
4648}
4649/// The innermost active conditional, Perl `LookupValue('if_stack')->[0]`.
4650pub fn top_if_frame() -> Option<Rc<RefCell<crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame>>> {
4651  state!().if_stack.last().cloned()
4652}
4653/// Perl `shiftValue('if_stack')`: close the innermost active conditional.
4654pub fn pop_if_frame() -> Option<Rc<RefCell<crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame>>> {
4655  state_mut!().if_stack.pop()
4656}
4657/// The number of active conditionals.
4658pub fn if_stack_depth() -> usize { state!().if_stack.len() }
4659/// The active conditionals, each with its state as it is now, for [`restore_if_stack`] to put back.
4660pub fn if_stack_snapshot() -> Vec<(
4661  Rc<RefCell<crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame>>,
4662  crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame,
4663)> {
4664  state!()
4665    .if_stack
4666    .iter()
4667    .map(|frame| (Rc::clone(frame), frame.borrow().clone()))
4668    .collect()
4669}
4670/// Put the active conditionals back as [`if_stack_snapshot`] took them: the frames it held, their state as it was —
4671/// those a run opened dropped, those it closed (a `\fi` of its own matching one opened before it) open again.
4672pub fn restore_if_stack(
4673  snapshot: Vec<(
4674    Rc<RefCell<crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame>>,
4675    crate::definition::conditional::IfFrame,
4676  )>,
4677) {
4678  let frames = snapshot
4679    .into_iter()
4680    .map(|(frame, saved)| {
4681      *frame.borrow_mut() = saved;
4682      frame
4683    })
4684    .collect();
4685  state_mut!().if_stack = frames;
4686}
4687/// Perl `$LaTeXML::IF_LIMIT`: the runaway ceiling on conditional ids
4688/// (`0` = unlimited), set by `latexml.sty`'s `iflimit` option.
4689pub fn if_limit() -> i64 { state!().if_limit }
4690/// Set [`if_limit`] (Perl `latexml.sty.ltxml:101`).
4691pub fn set_if_limit(limit: i64) { state_mut!().if_limit = limit; }
4692/// The conditionals counted so far ([`State::if_count`]).
4693pub fn if_count() -> i64 { state!().if_count }
4694/// Put [`if_count`] back: a discarded run the read budget ended counts against no limit (latexml_engine sect05.rs
4695/// `speculative_run_read`).
4696pub fn set_if_count(count: i64) { state_mut!().if_count = count; }
4697/// Perl `Object.pm:163-168`: bump the global absorb counter and return it
4698/// (see [`State::absorb_count`]).
4699pub fn next_absorb_id() -> i64 {
4700  let mut state = state_mut!();
4701  state.absorb_count += 1;
4702  state.absorb_count
4703}
4704/// Perl `$LaTeXML::ABSORB_LIMIT`: the runaway ceiling on absorbed boxes
4705/// (`0` = unlimited), set by `latexml.sty`'s `absorblimit` option.
4706pub fn absorb_limit() -> i64 { state!().absorb_limit }
4707/// Set [`absorb_limit`] (Perl `latexml.sty.ltxml:104`).
4708pub fn set_absorb_limit(limit: i64) { state_mut!().absorb_limit = limit; }
4709/// `lookup_int` variant for hot call sites with a pre-pinned SymStr (see
4710/// `crate::pin!`). Skips the per-call `arena::pin(key)` hash lookup — the
4711/// sibling of [`lookup_bool_sym`].
4712pub fn lookup_int_sym(key: SymStr) -> i64 {
4713  let state = state!();
4714  match state.lookup_value_sym(key) {
4715    Some(Stored::Int(i)) => *i,
4716    Some(Stored::Bool(true)) => 1, // this is Perl's boolean -> integer semantics
4717    Some(Stored::Number(n)) => n.value_of(),
4718    _ => 0,
4719  }
4720}
4721/// `lookup_int` variant that never panics on a live mutable borrow.
4722///
4723/// Returns `None` when STATE is currently mutably borrowed (contention),
4724/// `Some(0)` when the key is absent/non-integer (matching `lookup_int`'s
4725/// default), else `Some(value)`.
4726///
4727/// This exists for the `Error!`/`Warn!` reporting path: an error can legitimately
4728/// be raised from inside a `state_mut()` scope (e.g. `push_value`'s BUG branch,
4729/// or any constructor `after_digest` holding the borrow). A plain `borrow()` there
4730/// panics "RefCell already mutably borrowed", aborting the whole conversion
4731/// (FATAL_panic; crashed tikz-cd 2001.08973 via `push_value("QED@stack", …)`).
4732/// The error reporter must be re-entrancy-safe regardless of what borrows are
4733/// held — degrade to "unknown" on contention rather than crash.
4734pub fn try_lookup_int(key: &str) -> Option<i64> {
4735  let state = (*STATE).try_borrow().ok()?;
4736  Some(match state.lookup_value(key) {
4737    Some(Stored::Int(i)) => *i,
4738    Some(Stored::Bool(true)) => 1,
4739    Some(Stored::Number(n)) => n.value_of(),
4740    _ => 0,
4741  })
4742}
4743
4744/// [`try_lookup_int`]'s string counterpart: the string at `key`, `None` when it holds none or the state is borrowed.
4745pub fn try_lookup_string(key: &str) -> Option<String> {
4746  let state = (*STATE).try_borrow().ok()?;
4747  match state.lookup_value(key) {
4748    Some(Stored::String(s)) => Some(arena::with(*s, str::to_owned)),
4749    _ => None,
4750  }
4751}
4752
4753/// [`assign_value`] globally on the reporting path, where the state may be borrowed (as [`try_lookup_int`]): whether
4754/// it was assigned.
4755pub fn try_assign_value_global(key: &str, value: Stored) -> bool {
4756  let Ok(mut state) = (*STATE).try_borrow_mut() else {
4757    return false;
4758  };
4759  state.assign_value(key, value, Some(Scope::Global));
4760  true
4761}
4762
4763pub fn remove_vecdeque(key: &str) -> Option<VecDeque<Stored>> {
4764  match remove_value(key) {
4765    Some(Stored::VecDequeStored(v)) => Some(v),
4766    _ => None,
4767  }
4768}
4769/// convenience method to lookup the current value at the "font" key
4770pub fn lookup_font() -> Option<Rc<Font>> {
4771  // try_borrow, not state!()'s borrow(): this accessor is reachable from a
4772  // Whatsit's Display/revert path (e.g. tex_glue::revert_skip → lookup_font)
4773  // which can run *while STATE is already mutably borrowed* — e.g. formatting a
4774  // whatsit into a log/error message inside a state_mut() scope. A plain
4775  // borrow() then panics "RefCell already mutably borrowed", aborting the worker
4776  // (FATAL_101; crashed hep-th9908053, a \documentstyle[12pt]{article} 2.09
4777  // paper). Degrade to None on contention instead of crashing.
4778  //
4779  // CAUTION for future callers (PR #249 review P3-18): None-on-contention is
4780  // only correct for Display/revert/log-formatting consumers (where a
4781  // defaulted font is cosmetic). Several digestion-path callers `.unwrap()`
4782  // the result (tbox.rs, whatsit.rs, stomach.rs) — they would panic loudly on
4783  // contention, which is the desired behavior there: a DIGESTION-path
4784  // re-entrant lookup is a real bug, and silently defaulting the font would
4785  // turn it into invisible wrong-font drift in the XML. If you add a caller,
4786  // pick deliberately: `.unwrap()` on digestion paths, graceful None only
4787  // where the font is presentational.
4788  let Ok(st) = (*STATE).try_borrow() else {
4789    return None;
4790  };
4791  match st.lookup_value_sym(pin!("font")) {
4792    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4793    Some(f) => f.into(),
4794  }
4795}
4796/// convenience method to lookup the current value at the "mathfont" key
4797pub fn lookup_mathfont() -> Option<Rc<Font>> {
4798  // Route through `lookup_value_sym` with a cached SymStr (via
4799  // `pin!`) to skip the per-call `arena::pin("mathfont")` probe on
4800  // this hot path (math-env entry/exit, per-formula checks).
4801  match state!().lookup_value_sym(pin!("mathfont")) {
4802    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4803    Some(v) => v.into(),
4804  }
4805}
4806
4807/// a convenience method to globally asign a `Font` to the "font" key
4808pub fn assign_font(font: Rc<Font>, scope: Option<Scope>) {
4809  assign_value_sym(pin!("font"), Stored::Font(font), scope);
4810}
4811
4812/// a variant of `lookup_value` that casts the value into `Number`
4813pub fn lookup_number(key: &str) -> Option<Number> {
4814  match state!().lookup_value(key) {
4815    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4816    Some(v) => v.into(),
4817  }
4818}
4819/// a variant of `lookup_value` that casts the value into `Float`
4820///
4821/// The float counterpart of [`lookup_number`]. `Float` isn't a TeX register
4822/// type (see `common::float`), but binding authors need a fractional read/write
4823/// pair — e.g. `NOMINAL_FONT_SIZE` at the `11pt` class option is `10.95`, which
4824/// [`lookup_number`]/[`lookup_int`] would truncate to `10` (issue #542).
4825pub fn lookup_float(key: &str) -> Option<Float> {
4826  match state!().lookup_value(key) {
4827    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4828    Some(v) => v.into(),
4829  }
4830}
4831/// a variant of `lookup_value` that casts the value into `Dimension`
4832pub fn lookup_dimension(key: &str) -> Option<Dimension> {
4833  let key = register_value_key(key);
4834  match state!().lookup_value(&key) {
4835    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4836    Some(v) => v.into(),
4837  }
4838}
4839/// Whether `address` is a slot of a primitive register bank — `\count<n>`,
4840/// `\dimen<n>`, `\skip<n>`, `\muskip<n>`, `\toks<n>`, `\box<n>` — i.e. what
4841/// `\newdimen` & co. allocate (tex.web §1224), as opposed to a register named
4842/// after its own (or another) control sequence.
4843pub fn is_allocated_register_address(address: &str) -> bool {
4844  [
4845    "\\count", "\\dimen", "\\skip", "\\muskip", "\\toks", "\\box",
4846  ]
4847  .iter()
4848  .any(|bank| {
4849    address
4850      .strip_prefix(bank)
4851      .is_some_and(|n| !n.is_empty() && n.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
4852  })
4853}
4854
4855/// Where a register's value lives: its address, which is not its CS name once the
4856/// format allocated it (latex.ltx's `\newdimen\columnsep` is `\dimen124`; the
4857/// dump records it and `def_register` keeps it). Name-keyed readers of a kernel
4858/// length (`lookup_dimension("\\textwidth")`) go through the address.
4859fn register_value_key(key: &str) -> Cow<'_, str> {
4860  if key.starts_with('\\')
4861    && let Some(register) = lookup_register_definition(&T_CS!(key))
4862    && is_allocated_register_address(&register.address)
4863  {
4864    return Cow::Owned(register.address.clone());
4865  }
4866  Cow::Borrowed(key)
4867}
4868/// a variant of `lookup_value` that only recognizes a `Stored::Glue`
4869pub fn lookup_glue(key: &str) -> Option<Glue> {
4870  let key = register_value_key(key);
4871  match state!().lookup_value(&key) {
4872    Some(Stored::Glue(v)) => Some(*v),
4873    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4874    Some(other) => panic!("State lookup expected Glue, found: {other:?}"),
4875  }
4876}
4877/// a variant of `lookup_value` that only recognizes a `Stored::Glue`
4878pub fn lookup_muglue(key: &str) -> Option<MuGlue> {
4879  match state!().lookup_value(key) {
4880    Some(Stored::MuGlue(v)) => Some(*v),
4881    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4882    Some(other) => panic!("State lookup expected MuGlue, found: {other:?}"),
4883  }
4884}
4885/// a variant of `lookup_value` that casts the response into `Tokens`
4886pub fn lookup_tokens(key: &str) -> Option<Tokens> {
4887  let state = state!();
4888  match state.lookup_value(key) {
4889    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
4890    Some(Stored::Tokens(v)) => Some(v.clone()),
4891    Some(Stored::Token(v)) => Some(Tokens::new(vec![*v])),
4892    Some(Stored::String(sym)) => {
4893      // Release the state borrow first, then read the interned string through
4894      // the re-entrant arena. The copy is unavoidable: an arena `&str` is not
4895      // `'static` (it dangles across a realloc — see WISDOM), and `TeXString`
4896      // borrows only `'static`, so the value has to be owned to cross into the
4897      // tokenizer. `Mouth::new` copies its input anyway.
4898      let sym = *sym;
4899      drop(state);
4900      arena::with(sym, |astr| {
4901        Some(mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(
4902          astr.to_string(),
4903        )))
4904      })
4905    },
4906    Some(Stored::VecDequeStored(v)) => {
4907      // Reverting the queue to Tokens routes each String item through
4908      // `mouth::tokenize_internal`, which takes a *mutable* STATE borrow — so
4909      // clone the queue and drop the immutable `state` borrow first (mirrors
4910      // the `Stored::String` branch above). Without this, LookupTokens on a
4911      // VecDequeStored key (e.g. "class_options") panics "RefCell already
4912      // borrowed" (#314).
4913      let vdq = v.clone();
4914      drop(state);
4915      Stored::VecDequeStored(vdq).into()
4916    },
4917    _ => None,
4918  }
4919}
4920/// a variant of `lookup_value` that only recognizes a `Stored::Token`
4921pub fn lookup_token(key: &str) -> Option<Token> {
4922  match state!().lookup_value(key) {
4923    Some(Stored::Token(t)) => Some(*t),
4924    _ => None,
4925  }
4926}
4927
4928/// a variant of `lookup_token` taking an already-pinned SymStr key —
4929/// avoids the per-call `arena::pin(key)` hash lookup.
4930pub fn lookup_token_sym(key: SymStr) -> Option<Token> {
4931  match state!().lookup_value_sym(key) {
4932    Some(Stored::Token(t)) => Some(*t),
4933    _ => None,
4934  }
4935}
4936
4937pub fn lookup_alignment() -> Option<Digested> {
4938  // Can only be a token or definition; we want defns!
4939  // is this the right logic here? don't expand unless digesting?
4940  state!().lookup_value_sym(pin!("Alignment")).and_then(|v| {
4941    if let Stored::Digested(d) = v {
4942      if matches!(d.data(), DigestedData::Alignment(_)) {
4943        // for now clone the Digested object (approx. an Rc<_> clone)
4944        // instead of returning &Digested, to simplify lifetime checks
4945        Some(d.clone())
4946      } else {
4947        None
4948      }
4949    } else {
4950      None
4951    }
4952  })
4953}
4954pub fn assign_alignment(alignment: Alignment, scope: Option<Scope>) {
4955  assign_value("Alignment", alignment, scope);
4956}
4957
4958pub fn assign_register(
4959  cs: &str,
4960  value: RegisterValue,
4961  scope: Option<Scope>,
4962  parameters: Vec<ArgWrap>,
4963) -> Result<()> {
4964  assign_register_token(&T_CS!(cs), value, scope, parameters)
4965}
4966/// `assign_register` variant taking a pre-built Token — lets hot
4967/// callers skip the `T_CS!(&str)` pin when they already have the CS
4968/// cached (e.g. via `T_CS!("\\c@…")` literal which routes through
4969/// `pin!`).
4970pub fn assign_register_token(
4971  cs: &Token,
4972  value: RegisterValue,
4973  scope: Option<Scope>,
4974  parameters: Vec<ArgWrap>,
4975) -> Result<()> {
4976  let defn_opt = lookup_definition(cs)?;
4977  if let Some(defn) = defn_opt
4978    && defn.is_register()
4979  {
4980    defn.set_value(value, scope, parameters);
4981    return Ok(());
4982  }
4983  Warn!(
4984    "expected",
4985    "register",
4986    format!("The control sequence '{cs}' is not a register")
4987  );
4988  Ok(())
4989}
4990pub fn lookup_register(cs: &str, parameters: Vec<ArgWrap>) -> Result<Option<RegisterValue>> {
4991  lookup_register_token(&T_CS!(cs), parameters)
4992}
4993/// Token-keyed variant of `lookup_register` — saves the per-call
4994/// `T_CS!(&str)` pin for hot callers with a cached CS token.
4995pub fn lookup_register_token(
4996  cs: &Token,
4997  parameters: Vec<ArgWrap>,
4998) -> Result<Option<RegisterValue>> {
4999  Ok(match lookup_definition(cs)? {
5000    Some(defn) => {
5001      if defn.is_register() {
5002        defn.value_of(parameters)
5003      } else {
5004        let message = s!("The control sequence '{}' is not a register", cs);
5005        Warn!("expected", "register", message);
5006        None
5007      }
5008    },
5009    _ => None,
5010  })
5011}
5012
5013/// Quiet sibling of [`lookup_register`] for call sites that mirror Perl's
5014/// explicit `lookupDefinition(cs) && $defn->isRegister ? $defn->valueOf : <default>`
5015/// guard — e.g. TeX_Tables `\lx@text@intercol`/`\lx@math@intercol`
5016/// (`TeX_Tables.pool.ltxml` L639/L646), where a document may legitimately
5017/// `\renewcommand` a length register (`\tabcolsep`/`\arraycolsep`) into a plain
5018/// macro. In that case the register-ness is genuinely gone in Perl too, and Perl
5019/// silently falls back to its default (`Dimension(0)`) with **no warning**.
5020/// Returns `None` (no warning) when the CS is undefined or is not a register,
5021/// so the caller can apply its own faithful default.
5022pub fn lookup_register_quiet(cs: &str) -> Option<RegisterValue> {
5023  let defn = lookup_definition(&T_CS!(cs)).ok().flatten()?;
5024  if defn.is_register() {
5025    defn.value_of(Vec::new())
5026  } else {
5027    None
5028  }
5029}
5030
5031/// Faithful port of Perl `LookupDimension` (`Package.pm` L1371-1393, as
5032/// widened by upstream PR #2829): try to turn the argument into a Dimension,
5033/// recognizing strings, registers, ….
5034///
5035/// * a string that looks like an obvious dimension (`/^[0-9+-.]\w\w+$/`,
5036///   e.g. `"3pt"` — but NOT `"0.4pt"`, whose `.` fails `\w`) parses directly;
5037/// * otherwise the string is tokenized: a single token that resolves to a
5038///   register returns its value ("easy and proper case");
5039/// * a multi-token sequence is read as a dimension from a fresh mouth;
5040/// * anything else warns (`expected:register`) unless `noerror`, and yields
5041///   `None` (Perl returns undef).
5042///
5043/// NOTE the #2829 semantics change carried over faithfully: a single token
5044/// whose definition is a MACRO (e.g. a document that `\def`s `\jot`) no
5045/// longer reads its body as a dimension — it now falls through to the warn
5046/// branch. (Perl's digested-Box coercion branch has no Rust equivalent here:
5047/// all our callers pass strings.)
5048pub fn lookup_dimension_cs(cs: &str, noerror: bool) -> Option<Dimension> {
5049  use std::str::FromStr;
5050  // Obvious dimension string? (Perl: /^[0-9\+\-\.]\w\w+$/)
5051  let mut chars = cs.chars();
5052  let leading_sign_or_digit =
5053    matches!(chars.next(), Some(c) if c.is_ascii_digit() || matches!(c, '+' | '-' | '.'));
5054  let obvious = leading_sign_or_digit
5055    && cs.chars().count() >= 3
5056    && chars.all(|c| c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '_');
5057  if obvious && let Ok(d) = Dimension::from_str(cs) {
5058    return Some(d);
5059  }
5060  let tokens = mouth::tokenize_internal(TeXString::assembled(cs.to_string()));
5061  let toks = tokens.unlist();
5062  if toks.len() == 1 {
5063    match lookup_definition(&toks[0]) {
5064      Ok(Some(defn)) if defn.is_register() => {
5065        // Easy (and proper) case.
5066        return defn.value_of(Vec::new()).map(|rv| Dimension::from(&rv));
5067      },
5068      // Defined but not a register (a `\def`-ized length): fall through and
5069      // read its body as a dimension. NB this is a deliberate DIVERGENCE
5070      // from post-#2829 Perl, which unintentionally LOST this path in the
5071      // rewrite (a single macro token falls to the warn branch upstream) —
5072      // see KNOWN_PERL_ERRORS #41. Real arXiv papers `\def\arraycolsep{...}`
5073      // (cluster regressions cover this); pre-#2829 Perl read the body.
5074      Ok(Some(_)) => {},
5075      // Undefined single token: warn like Perl and yield nothing.
5076      _ => {
5077        if !noerror {
5078          let message = s!("The control sequence '{}' is not a register", cs);
5079          Warn!("expected", "register", message);
5080        }
5081        return None;
5082      },
5083    }
5084  }
5085  // Read the token sequence (a defined single CS expands here, exactly like
5086  // Perl's readingFromMouth) as a dimension from a fresh mouth; an
5087  // unreadable sequence warns Missing-number inside read_dimension and
5088  // yields Dimension(0), matching Perl.
5089  gullet::reading_from_mouth(mouth::Mouth::default(), move || {
5090    gullet::unread(Tokens::new(toks));
5091    gullet::read_dimension()
5092  })
5093  .ok()
5094}
5095
5096pub fn lookup_expandable(
5097  token: &Token,
5098  toplevel_opt: Option<bool>,
5099) -> Result<Option<Rc<dyn Definition>>> {
5100  let toplevel = toplevel_opt.unwrap_or(true); // Default, for full expansion, same as read_x_token
5101  // Can only be a token or definition; we want defns!
5102  // is this the right logic here? don't expand unless digesting?
5103  Ok(
5104    lookup_definition(token)?
5105      .filter(|defn| (*defn).is_expandable() && (toplevel || !(*defn).is_protected())),
5106  )
5107}
5108
5109/// Whether token is affected by \noexpand
5110pub fn is_dont_expandable(token: &Token) -> bool {
5111  // Basically: a CS or Active token that is either not defined, or is expandable
5112  // (but not \let to a token)
5113  if token.get_catcode().is_active_or_cs() {
5114    let lookupname = meaning_key(token);
5115    if lookupname != pin!("") {
5116      match state!().meaning.get(&lookupname) {
5117        Some(entry) => {
5118          if let Some(def) = entry.front() {
5119            // the expandable variants are allowed
5120            matches!(
5121              def,
5122              Stored::Expandable(_) | Stored::Conditional(_) | Stored::None
5123            )
5124          } else {
5125            // undefined is allowed too (this is *really* subtle -- took some debugging of
5126            // etoolbox) both an empty VDQ, a VDQ with an entry present but matching
5127            // Stored::Noney, OR a completely missing VDQ are allowed "undefined" cases, each of
5128            // which flagging as "true"
5129            true
5130          }
5131        },
5132        None => true,
5133      }
5134    } else {
5135      true
5136    }
5137  } else {
5138    false
5139  }
5140}
5141
5142pub fn lookup_conditional(token: &Token) -> Option<ConditionalType> {
5143  // `get_executable_name` previously built a fresh `String` + `arena::pin`
5144  // probe per call; `pin_cs_name` already returns a cached `SymStr`
5145  // (primitive → `Catcode::name_sym`, otherwise `self.text`). Saves a
5146  // RefCell mut-borrow on the interner + a hashmap probe per token in
5147  // the gullet's conditional dispatch.
5148  if !token.code.is_executable() {
5149    return None;
5150  }
5151  let lookup_sym = token.pin_cs_name();
5152  state!().meaning.get(&lookup_sym).and_then(|entry| {
5153    if let Some(Stored::Conditional(defn)) = entry.front() {
5154      Some(defn.conditional_type)
5155    } else {
5156      None
5157    }
5158  })
5159}
5160
5161pub fn unshift_value<T: Into<Stored>>(key: &str, values: Vec<T>) {
5162  let values_iter = values.into_iter().map(Into::into);
5163  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
5164  let mut state = state_mut!();
5165  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
5166  if !state.value.contains_key(&key_sym) {
5167    state.assign_internal(
5168      TableName::Value,
5169      key_sym,
5170      Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::new()),
5171      Some(Scope::Global),
5172    )
5173  }
5174  let receiver = state.value.get_mut(&key_sym).unwrap().front_mut();
5175  if let Some(&mut Stored::VecDequeStored(ref mut front)) = receiver {
5176    for value in values_iter.rev() {
5177      // preserving order unshift, as Perl's
5178      front.push_front(value)
5179    }
5180  } else if receiver.is_none() || matches!(receiver, Some(Stored::None)) {
5181    // Key doesn't exist yet — create a new VecDequeStored via the existing borrow
5182    let mut vd = VecDeque::new();
5183    for value in values_iter {
5184      vd.push_back(value);
5185    }
5186    state.assign_internal(
5187      TableName::Value,
5188      key_sym,
5189      Stored::VecDequeStored(vd),
5190      Some(Scope::Global),
5191    );
5192  } else {
5193    // Wrong type — warn but don't panic
5194    Warn!(
5195      "unexpected",
5196      "unshift_value",
5197      s!(
5198        "unshift_value expects VecDequeStored receiver for key {:?}, got: {:?}",
5199        key,
5200        receiver.map(|r| std::mem::discriminant(r))
5201      )
5202    );
5203  }
5204}
5205
5206pub fn shift_value(key: &str) -> Result<Option<Stored>> {
5207  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
5208  let mut state = state_mut!();
5209  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
5210  if !state.value.contains_key(&key_sym) {
5211    state.assign_internal(
5212      TableName::Value,
5213      key_sym,
5214      Stored::VecDequeStored(VecDeque::new()),
5215      Some(Scope::Global),
5216    )
5217  }
5218  Ok(
5219    if let Some(&mut Stored::VecDequeStored(ref mut front)) =
5220      state.value.get_mut(&key_sym).unwrap().front_mut()
5221    {
5222      front.pop_front()
5223    } else {
5224      Error!(
5225        "State",
5226        "Stored",
5227        "BUG: Tried to shift_value from a non-vecdeque value key!"
5228      );
5229      None
5230    },
5231  )
5232}
5233
5234/// Bind `key` to `value` inside the named mapping — Perl's `AssignMapping`.
5235///
5236/// A mapping is a named hash living in the value table (`TAG_PROPERTIES`,
5237/// `counter_for_type`, …). The mapping itself is created **globally** on first
5238/// use, so that entries assigned inside a group are still found from outside
5239/// it; passing `None` for `value` removes the key. Read entries back with
5240/// [`with_mapping`].
5241pub fn assign_mapping<T: Into<Stored>>(map: &str, key: &str, value: Option<T>) {
5242  let map_sym = arena::pin(map);
5243  let mut state = state_mut!();
5244  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, map_sym);
5245  if !state.value.contains_key(&map_sym) || state.value[&map_sym].is_empty() {
5246    state.assign_internal(
5247      TableName::Value,
5248      map_sym,
5249      Stored::HashStored(SymHashMap::default()),
5250      Some(Scope::Global),
5251    );
5252  }
5253  let map_store = state.value.get_mut(&map_sym).unwrap();
5254  // TODO: What is the right abstraction here? this is hacky
5255  let mut stub_hash = SymHashMap::default();
5256  let mapping = match *map_store.front_mut().unwrap() {
5257    Stored::HashStored(ref mut mapping) => mapping,
5258    _ => &mut stub_hash,
5259  };
5260  match value {
5261    None => mapping.remove(key),
5262    Some(v) => mapping.insert(key, v.into()),
5263  };
5264}
5265
5266pub fn lookup_mapping(map: &str, key: &str) -> Option<Stored> {
5267  state!().lookup_mapping(map, key).cloned()
5268}
5269/// Sym-keyed variant — skip the per-call `arena::pin(map)` for hot
5270/// callers with a pre-pinned map key (e.g. via `pin!("siunitx_macros")`).
5271pub fn lookup_mapping_sym(map_sym: SymStr, key: &str) -> Option<Stored> {
5272  state!().lookup_mapping_sym(map_sym, key).cloned()
5273}

5275//======================================================================
5276/// Was `name` bound?  If  `frame` is given, check only whether it is bound in
5277/// that frame (0 is the topmost).
5278pub fn is_value_bound(key: &str, frame_opt: Option<usize>) -> bool {
5279  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
5280  match frame_opt {
5281    Some(frame) => state!()
5282      .undo
5283      .get(frame)
5284      .as_ref()
5285      .unwrap()
5286      .table(TableName::Value)
5287      .contains_key(&key_sym),
5288    None => !state!()
5289      .value
5290      .get(&key_sym)
5291      .unwrap_or(&VecDeque::new())
5292      .is_empty(),
5293  }
5294}
5295
5296/// The value `key` has in stack frame `frame` (0 = innermost): Perl State.pm:280-286 `valueInFrame`, which skips
5297/// the bindings the frames above it made.
5298pub fn value_in_frame(key: &str, frame: usize) -> Option<Stored> {
5299  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
5300  let state = state!();
5301  let p: usize = state
5302    .undo
5303    .iter()
5304    .take(frame)
5305    .map(|f| {
5306      f.table(TableName::Value)
5307        .get(&key_sym)
5308        .copied()
5309        .unwrap_or(0)
5310    })
5311    .sum();
5312  state.value.get(&key_sym).and_then(|v| v.get(p)).cloned()
5313}
5314
5315/// The position in `key`'s value stack of the binding stack frame `frame` (0 = innermost) made, and how many it made
5316/// there (0 when it made none).
5317fn frame_binding_span(state: &State, key_sym: SymStr, frame: usize) -> (usize, usize) {
5318  let count = |f: &UndoFrame| {
5319    f.table(TableName::Value)
5320      .get(&key_sym)
5321      .copied()
5322      .unwrap_or(0)
5323  };
5324  let offset = state.undo.iter().take(frame).map(count).sum();
5325  (offset, state.undo.get(frame).map_or(0, count))
5326}
5327
5328/// Undoes the bindings of `key` that stack frame `frame` (0 = innermost) made — what popping that frame would undo,
5329/// for this key alone — and leaves the frames above it as they are.
5330pub fn unbind_value_in_frame(key: &str, frame: usize) {
5331  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
5332  let mut state = state_mut!();
5333  let (offset, count) = frame_binding_span(&state, key_sym, frame);
5334  if count == 0 {
5335    return;
5336  }
5337  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
5338  if let Some(f) = state.undo.get_mut(frame) {
5339    f.table_mut(TableName::Value).remove(&key_sym);
5340  }
5341  if let Some(values) = state.value.get_mut(&key_sym) {
5342    let end = (offset + count).min(values.len());
5343    values.drain(offset.min(end)..end);
5344  }
5345}
5346
5347/// Replaces the value `key` has in stack frame `frame` (0 = innermost), which must have bound it; returns the value
5348/// it had there, `None` when the frame made no binding of `key` (and nothing is changed).
5349pub fn replace_value_in_frame(key: &str, frame: usize, value: Stored) -> Option<Stored> {
5350  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
5351  let mut state = state_mut!();
5352  let (offset, count) = frame_binding_span(&state, key_sym, frame);
5353  if count == 0 {
5354    return None;
5355  }
5356  state.journal_touch(TableName::Value, key_sym);
5357  let slot = state.value.get_mut(&key_sym)?.get_mut(offset)?;
5358  Some(std::mem::replace(slot, value))
5359}
5360
5361/// The stack frame (0 = innermost) that made the `n`-th newest binding of `key` (0 = the binding in force), for
5362/// [`value_in_frame`]: which group a value was set in.
5363pub fn frame_of_binding(key: &str, n: usize) -> Option<usize> {
5364  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
5365  let state = state!();
5366  let mut seen = 0;
5367  for (frame, f) in state.undo.iter().enumerate() {
5368    let bound = f
5369      .table(TableName::Value)
5370      .get(&key_sym)
5371      .copied()
5372      .unwrap_or(0);
5373    if bound > 0 {
5374      if seen + bound > n {
5375        return Some(frame);
5376      }
5377      seen += bound;
5378    }
5379  }
5380  None
5381}
5382
5383//======================================================================
5384/// Lookup & assign a character's Catcode
5385pub fn lookup_catcode(c: char) -> Option<Catcode> {
5386  // speedup over variant with allocation
5387  // i.e. "let s = c.to_string();"
5388  let s = arena::pin_char(c);
5389  match state!().catcode.get(&s) {
5390    None => unicode_letter_catcode_default(c),
5391    Some(cvec) => match cvec.front() {
5392      Some(Stored::Catcode(cc)) => Some(*cc),
5393      Some(_) => None, // non-catcode value in catcode table — treat as undefined
5394      _ => unicode_letter_catcode_default(c),
5395    },
5396  }
5397}
5398
5399/// Whether the document's engine persona is a Unicode engine: the `luatex` or
5400/// `xetex` profile of latexml.sty. Both formats load load-unicode-data
5401/// (latex.ltx:22071-22076), take `\char` code points above 255 natively and
5402/// have no utf8.def byte model; the engine-identity probes stay per-profile.
5403pub fn unicode_engine_profile() -> bool {
5404  lookup_bool("LUATEX_PROFILE") || lookup_bool("XETEX_PROFILE")
5405}
5406
5407/// The format-time Unicode letter catcodes of the LuaTeX/XeTeX kernels, as a
5408/// lazy default for code points the catcode table does not pin.
5409///
5410/// latex.ltx:22071-22076 inputs `load-unicode-data` only under LuaTeX/XeTeX, and
5411/// load-unicode-data.tex:98-99,134-135 assigns `\catcode 11` to every code point
5412/// of General_Category L or M. pdfTeX never does (utf8.def makes the bytes
5413/// active), and Perl `State.pm:113-115` letters only ASCII, so outside the
5414/// LuaTeX profile a non-ASCII char without a table entry stays OTHER
5415/// (`Mouth::get_next_char`). The profile check runs only for non-ASCII chars,
5416/// so the ASCII tokenizer path is untouched. U+0080–U+00FF are pinned OTHER by
5417/// the format dump; the `luatex` option re-letters them eagerly
5418/// (`latexml_sty/mod.rs`). Witnesses: circledtext (102→0), jnuexam, tikz-bagua;
5419/// guards `perfect_kernel_batch56::{non_ascii_letters_stay_other_under_pdftex,
5420/// non_ascii_letters_are_letters_under_luatex}`.
5421fn unicode_letter_catcode_default(c: char) -> Option<Catcode> {
5422  if c > '\x7f'
5423    && ((unicode_engine_profile() && is_unicode_letter_or_mark(c))
5424      || (lookup_bool("PTEX_PROFILE") && is_ptex_kanji_letter(c)))
5425  {
5426    Some(Catcode::LETTER)
5427  } else {
5428    None
5429  }
5430}
5431
5432/// K12 — pTeX's control-word letters. pTeX/upTeX give every non-ASCII code
5433/// point a `kcatcode` (upTeX manual §kcatcode: 16 kanji, 17 kana, 18 other
5434/// kchar, 19 hangul, 20 modifier; ASCII is always Latin), and a control word
5435/// after `\` extends over kanji, kana (and, upTeX, hangul/modifier) characters
5436/// — `\if西暦` is one control sequence (jsarticle.cls:1927 `\newif\if西暦`,
5437/// ptex-manual `\黄マーカー`); kcatcode 18 (symbols, fullwidth punctuation)
5438/// ends it, and Latin-1/Greek/Cyrillic stay inputenc bytes (kcatcode 15), so
5439/// this is the block set of the kanji/kana/hangul kcatcodes, not the Unicode
5440/// letter class. Under `PTEX_PROFILE` (set by `\NeedsTeXFormat{pLaTeX2e}` /
5441/// `{upLaTeX2e}`) these default to LETTER, as the LuaTeX profile letters the
5442/// L/M class; under pdfTeX they stay OTHER, where both LaTeXML engines let
5443/// `\newif\if西暦`'s bare `\if` to `\iffalse` and every `\if` after it fails.
5444pub fn is_ptex_kanji_letter(c: char) -> bool {
5445  matches!(c as u32,
5446    0x3040..=0x30FF   // Hiragana, Katakana (kana, 17)
5447    | 0x31F0..=0x31FF // Katakana phonetic extensions (17)
5448    | 0x1AFF0..=0x1B16F // Kana Supplement / Extended-A/B, Small Kana (17)
5449    | 0xFF66..=0xFF6F // halfwidth katakana (17; U+FF70 ー is 18)
5450    | 0xFF71..=0xFF9D
5451    | 0xFF10..=0xFF19 // fullwidth digits and Latin letters (17)
5452    | 0xFF21..=0xFF3A
5453    | 0xFF41..=0xFF5A
5454    | 0x2E80..=0x2FDF // CJK radicals, Kangxi radicals (16)
5455    | 0x3105..=0x312F // Bopomofo (16)
5456    | 0x3190..=0x31EF // Kanbun, Bopomofo extended, CJK strokes (16)
5457    | 0x3400..=0x4DBF // CJK Unified Ideographs Extension A (16)
5458    | 0x4E00..=0x9FFF // CJK Unified Ideographs (16)
5459    | 0xF900..=0xFAFF // CJK Compatibility Ideographs (16)
5460    | 0x20000..=0x3FFFF // CJK Extensions B-J (16)
5461    | 0x1100..=0x11FF // Hangul Jamo (19)
5462    | 0x3130..=0x318F // Hangul compatibility Jamo (19)
5463    | 0xA960..=0xA97F // Hangul Jamo Extended-A (19)
5464    | 0xAC00..=0xD7FF // Hangul syllables and Jamo Extended-B (19)
5465  )
5466}
5467
5468/// load-unicode-data.tex:134-135 for U+0080–U+00FF: the LuaTeX/XeTeX format
5469/// letters every L/M code point, but the latex dump pins that range OTHER
5470/// (pdfTeX's utf8.def model), so the lazy default in `lookup_catcode` never
5471/// reaches it. Called at the format seam (`latexml_engine::latex`) and by the
5472/// `luatex`/`xetex` options of latexml.sty; 65 global assignments.
5473pub fn assign_luatex_latin1_letters() {
5474  for n in 0x80u32..=0xFF {
5475    if let Some(ch) = char::from_u32(n)
5476      && is_unicode_letter_or_mark(ch)
5477    {
5478      assign_catcode(ch, Catcode::LETTER, Some(Scope::Global));
5479    }
5480  }
5481}
5482
5483/// General_Category L ∪ M, the set load-unicode-data.tex letters. `is_alphabetic`
5484/// also admits Nl (Roman numerals), which the kernel leaves OTHER; combining marks
5485/// (M) are added explicitly.
5486pub fn is_unicode_letter_or_mark(c: char) -> bool {
5487  (c.is_alphabetic() && !c.is_numeric()) || unicode_normalization::char::is_combining_mark(c)
5488}
5489

5492  let s = arena::pin_char(key);
5493  state_mut!().assign_internal(TableName::Catcode, s, Stored::Catcode(value), scope);
5494}
