5600/// The key under which a token's meaning is stored. **All** `\special_relax`-family
5601/// tokens (`\noexpand`'d forms — the bare `\special_relax` and every
5602/// `\special_relax\x01<shadowed>`) resolve under the bare `\special_relax` name:
5603/// they share its `\relax` meaning, faithful to TeX where a `\noexpand`'d token
5604/// has relax meaning regardless of which token it shadows. The shadowed identity
5605/// is recovered separately via [`Token::noexpand_shadowed`] (delimited matching
5606/// only). Use this anywhere a token's *name* keys a meaning lookup or a
5607/// "same control sequence?" comparison. Cheap on the common path: non-CS tokens
5608/// short-circuit before any string access.
5609#[inline]
5610pub fn meaning_key(token: &Token) -> SymStr {
5611  if token.is_noexpand_family() {
5612    pin!("\\special_relax")
5613  } else {
5614    token.text
5615  }
5616}
5617
5618/// Get the "Meaning" of a token.
5619///
5620/// For active control sequences this may give the definition object (if
5621/// defined) or another token (if `\let`) or `None`. Any other token is returned
5622/// as is — which is what makes a `\let`-style comparison between a control
5623/// sequence and a character token work.
5624///
5625/// Clones the stored meaning; use [`with_meaning`] when inspecting it is enough.
5626pub fn lookup_meaning(token: &Token) -> Option<Stored> {
5627  if token.get_catcode().is_active_or_cs() && token.text != pin!("") {
5628    match state!().meaning.get(&meaning_key(token)) {
5629      Some(entry) => match entry.front() {
5630        None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
5631        Some(other) => Some(other.clone()),
5632      },
5633      None => None,
5634    }
5635  } else {
5636    Some(Stored::Token(*token))
5637  }
5638}
5639
5640/// Whether the current meaning of `token` was made inside a group open now, so it is gone once that group closes —
5641/// whether or not a meaning stands outside it (a global assignment clears the counts of the open groups' frames). A
5642/// binding that queues what it reads to digest later (the frontmatter API) loses such a meaning: aistats's
5643/// `\aistatsaddress` prints in place inside `\twocolumn[…]`, where 1302.5348 defines `\newcommand{\UMDAddress}{…}`
5644/// just before it, and where a paper may renew a macro it defined globally (77o review probe `g_globlocal`: pdflatex
5645/// prints the local meaning).
5646pub fn meaning_is_group_local(token: &Token) -> bool {
5647  if !token.get_catcode().is_active_or_cs() {
5648    return false;
5649  }
5650  let key = meaning_key(token);
5651  let state = state!();
5652  let Some(values) = state.meaning.get(&key) else {
5653    return false;
5654  };
5655  if values
5656    .front()
5657    .is_none_or(|value| matches!(value, Stored::None))
5658  {
5659    return false;
5660  }
5661  // (the groups above the locked base frame, which holds the global binding: a global assignment is counted there,
5662  // and clears the counts above it, state.rs `assign_internal`)
5663  state
5664    .undo
5665    .iter()
5666    .take_while(|frame| !frame.locked)
5667    .any(|frame| frame.meaning.get(&key).is_some_and(|count| *count > 0))
5668}
5669
5670/// Closure-based variant of `lookup_meaning` — avoids the per-call
5671/// `Stored::clone()` when the caller only needs to *inspect* the
5672/// meaning (e.g. extract a CS Token from an Expandable/Primitive
5673/// definition). Stored::clone is ~1% of total instructions on
5674/// siunitx-heavy fixtures (5M+ calls per run, each cloning a full
5675/// Stored enum). This helper borrows the stored value instead.
5676///
5677/// For non-CS/ACTIVE tokens, passes `Some(Stored::Token(*token))` —
5678/// matching lookup_meaning's fallback semantics. Note this requires
5679/// a single stack allocation of Stored::Token (Copy), not a heap
5680/// clone.
5681pub fn with_meaning<R>(token: &Token, f: impl FnOnce(Option<&Stored>) -> R) -> R {
5682  let state = state!();
5683  if token.get_catcode().is_active_or_cs() && token.text != pin!("") {
5684    match state.meaning.get(&meaning_key(token)) {
5685      Some(entry) => match entry.front() {
5686        None | Some(Stored::None) => f(None),
5687        Some(other) => f(Some(other)),
5688      },
5689      None => f(None),
5690    }
5691  } else {
5692    // Non-CS/ACTIVE: the "meaning" is just the token itself. The
5693    // caller gets a borrow of a temporary here, which is safe for
5694    // the duration of the closure.
5695    let s = Stored::Token(*token);
5696    f(Some(&s))
5697  }
5698}
5699
5700/// like `lookup_value` but only recognizes `Stored::VecDequeStored`
5701pub fn lookup_vecdeque(key: &str) -> Option<VecDeque<Stored>> {
5702  match state!().lookup_value(key) {
5703    None | Some(Stored::None) => None,
5704    Some(v) => <Option<&VecDeque<Stored>>>::from(v).cloned(),
5705  }
5706}
5707
5708pub fn with_vecdeque<R, FnR>(key: &str, caller: FnR) -> R
5709where FnR: FnOnce(Option<&VecDeque<Stored>>) -> R {
5710  caller(state!().lookup_vecdeque(key))
5711}
5712
5713/// Whether `token`'s definition is locked while state is not unlocked, so `install_definition` drops a redefinition.
5714pub fn is_definition_locked(token: &Token) -> bool { token.with_cs_name(is_name_locked) }
5715
5716/// Whether `token` means nothing a lock protects: undefined, or `\relax`, what TeX's `\csname` makes of an undefined
5717/// name (tex.web §372) on the way to every `\@namedef`, `\expandafter\def\csname…` and expl3 `\cs_set:cpn`/`…:cc`
5718/// definition ([`restore_cleared_binding`]).
5719fn is_cleared_meaning(token: &Token) -> bool {
5720  matches!(lookup_meaning(token), None | Some(Stored::None))
5721    || x_equals(token, &crate::T_CS!("\\relax"))
5722}
5723
5724/// Puts back the binding of a locked name that a file's `\let` cleared ([`let_i`], `"{cs}:locked@cleared"`) when
5725/// the file's new meaning for it is refused, so that the binding's other callers (a raw `\end` running `\csname
5726/// end<env>\endcsname`, alatex.sty:105-109) still meet it rather than an undefined name.
5727fn restore_cleared_binding(token: &Token) {
5728  let cleared_key = token.with_cs_name(|cs| s!("{cs}:locked@cleared"));
5729  if arena::get(&cleared_key).is_none() || !is_cleared_meaning(token) {
5730    return;
5731  }
5732  if let Some(binding) = lookup_value(&cleared_key).filter(|b| !matches!(b, Stored::None)) {
5733    state_mut!().assign_internal(
5734      TableName::Meaning,
5735      token.get_cs_name(),
5736      binding,
5737      Some(Scope::Global),
5738    );
5739    // Once: a later `\relax` the document gives the name is its own, and a later clearing `\let` keeps the binding
5740    // aside again.
5741    assign_value(&cleared_key, Stored::None, Some(Scope::Global));
5742  }
5743}
5744
5745/// Whether the control sequence named `cs` is locked while state is not unlocked: against every definition
5746/// (`"{cs}:locked"`), or against those the class and package files make (`"{cs}:locked@files"`), which leaves the
5747/// document free to define it, as LaTeX and Perl do (the kernel minipage: sect12.rs, OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #453).
5748pub fn is_name_locked(cs: &str) -> bool { !state_is_unlocked() && name_carries_lock(cs) }
5749
5750/// Whether the control sequence named `cs` carries a lock ([`is_name_locked`]'s two), read as a value whatever the
5751/// unlocked state: Perl's `\newenvironment`/`\renewenvironment` test `LookupValue('\name:locked')` directly
5752/// (latex_constructs.pool.ltxml:2787-2803), so a class's `\renewenvironment{thebibliography}` that a binding's hook runs
5753/// unlocked (as Perl runs before/after-digest code, Primitive.pm:44/:52) still leaves the locked environment alone
5754/// (wiley2sp's w2sp-pss.clo:444 in `\AtEndOfClass`: 100 of run 336's PushbackLimit papers, 0710.1676, 0809.3379;
5755/// ptptex: 0706.0316).
5756pub fn name_carries_lock(cs: &str) -> bool { lock_holds_against(cs, || true) }
5757
5758/// [`name_carries_lock`] for a known redefinition: `copies_interface` tells whether it takes an optional argument first
5759/// with a default ([`takes_optional_first`]), which a lock against the files that holds only against copies of the
5760/// binding's interface ([`files_lock_holds`]) reads.
5761pub fn lock_holds_against(cs: &str, copies_interface: impl FnOnce() -> bool) -> bool {
5762  // Probe-only: if "{cs}:locked" was never interned it cannot be bound, so
5763  // skip both the intern (which permanently grew the arena by one ":locked"
5764  // twin per defined cs) and the table lookup (2026-08-23 audit R6).
5765  let locked = |suffix: &str| arena::get_suffixed(cs, suffix).is_some_and(lookup_bool_sym);
5766  locked(":locked")
5767    || (crate::definition::origin::current_origin()
5768      != crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Document
5769      && locked(":locked@files")
5770      && files_lock_holds(cs, copies_interface))
5771}
5772
5773/// Whether a lock against the class and package files on `cs` holds against this redefinition. Most such locks hold
5774/// against every one. One that holds only against a COPY of the binding's interface (`"{cs}:locked@copies"`: amsthm's
5775/// proof, amsthm_sty.rs) holds against a redefinition taking an optional argument with a default first
5776/// ([`takes_optional_first`]) — amsthm.sty:431's own
5777/// `{proof}[1][\proofname]`, which smfart.cls:1194, ijmart.cls:336 or thuthesis.cls:4052 re-implement over a
5778/// `\trivlist` — and lets one with a head of its own through (fascicules.sty:142 `{\textbf{D\'emonstration:} \par}`,
5779/// NotesTeX.sty:124, a paper's own `\renewenvironment{proof}{…Beweisskizze:…}{…$\blacksquare$}`), whose head text
5780/// and end mark the binding would replace. The environment's end half (`\end<name>` of a qualified `\<name>`)
5781/// follows its begin half: locked while that is still the binding's (not cleared, and LaTeXML's own,
5782/// `DefinitionOrigin::is_latexml_owned`). OXIDIZED_DESIGN_DIVERGENCES #596.
5783pub fn files_lock_holds(cs: &str, copies_interface: impl FnOnce() -> bool) -> bool {
5784  // It keeps the binding's own meaning only while that is in force (or kept aside by a file's clearing `\let`,
5785  // [`let_i`]): once the document, or a definition the lock let through, has replaced or undefined the name, the
5786  // lock has lapsed and later definitions go through as in LaTeX (amsart, the document's `\let\proof\undefined`, then
5787  // a package's copy `\newenvironment{proof}[1][Nachweis]`: `perfect_kernel_batch67ps::
5788  // proof_lock_lapses_once_the_document_undefines_it`; 0705.0545's nccthm.sty:173 `\newcommand*{\proof}[1][]{…}` after
5789  // the same `\let` goes through on the copy rule alone, its default being empty). The protected
5790  // meaning is the one the lock names (`\proof:locked@copies` = `\@proof`), not any binding's: llncs's proof that
5791  // apxproof.sty:46 puts back is not amsthm's (2311.01302).
5792  if let Some(name) = cs.strip_prefix("\\end")
5793    && !name.is_empty()
5794  {
5795    let begin = s!("\\{name}");
5796    if let Some(protected) = copies_lock(&begin) {
5797      return copies_lock_in_force(&begin, &protected) || copies_lock_kept_aside(&begin);
5798    }
5799  }
5800  if let Some(protected) = copies_lock(cs) {
5801    if !copies_lock_in_force(cs, &protected) && !copies_lock_kept_aside(cs) {
5802      return false;
5803    }
5804    return copies_interface();
5805  }
5806  true
5807}
5808
5809/// The meaning a lock that holds only against copies protects (`"{cs}:locked@copies"`, [`files_lock_holds`]).
5810pub fn copies_lock(cs: &str) -> Option<Token> {
5811  arena::get(s!("{cs}:locked@copies"))?;
5812  match lookup_value(&s!("{cs}:locked@copies")) {
5813    Some(Stored::Token(token)) => Some(token),
5814    _ => None,
5815  }
5816}
5817
5818/// Whether `cs` still has the meaning its copies lock protects.
5819fn copies_lock_in_force(cs: &str, protected: &Token) -> bool { x_equals(&T_CS!(cs), protected) }
5820
5821/// Whether a file's clearing `\let` has put the protected meaning of `cs` aside (`"{cs}:locked@cleared"`, [`let_i`]).
5822fn copies_lock_kept_aside(cs: &str) -> bool {
5823  lookup_value(&s!("{cs}:locked@cleared")).is_some_and(|b| !matches!(b, Stored::None))
5824}
5825
5826/// [`install_definition`]'s verdict, in the locked state, on a redefinition of `cs`: [`lock_holds_against`] read
5827/// against its own interface, and a redefinition the lock lets through drops what a copies lock kept aside
5828/// ([`drop_copies_lock_aside`]) — probed only where a lock against the files is set, as every copies lock is one (so
5829/// a name without one costs the two probes the lock test always made).
5830fn redefinition_locked(cs: &str, definition: &Stored) -> bool {
5831  let probe = |suffix: &str| arena::get_suffixed(cs, suffix).is_some_and(lookup_bool_sym);
5832  if probe(":locked") {
5833    return !another_interface(cs, definition);
5834  }
5835  if !probe(":locked@files") {
5836    return false;
5837  }
5838  let holds = crate::definition::origin::current_origin()
5839    != crate::definition::origin::DefinitionOrigin::Document
5840    && files_lock_holds(cs, || takes_optional_first(definition));
5841  if !holds {
5842    drop_copies_lock_aside(cs);
5843  }
5844  holds
5845}
5846
5847/// Whether `definition` declares parameters other than the interface the lock on `cs` protects
5848/// (`"{cs}:locked@arity"`), so that it is a command of its own and no copy of the binding's: sltables.sty:193's
5849/// `\def\multicolumn##1{\omit…\stmultispan…}`, its table's one-argument command, against latex.ltx's three, which
5850/// agupp.sty:599, eqnarray.sty:45 and srdp-tables.sty:1218 copy. One declaring none may read any number and stays
5851/// refused. This looks at a definition's parameters only; a `\let` never comes here, a plain `:locked` having never
5852/// refused one (`let_i`, as Perl's `Let`): `\let\multicolumn\mymc` stands.
5853fn another_interface(cs: &str, definition: &Stored) -> bool {
5854  let Some(key) = arena::get(s!("{cs}:locked@arity")) else {
5855    return false;
5856  };
5857  let arity = lookup_int_sym(key);
5858  let count = definition_parameters(definition).map_or(0, |params| params.get_num_args()) as i64;
5859  arity > 0 && count > 0 && count != arity
5860}
5861
5862/// A definition the copies lock on `cs` let through replaces the protected meaning for good: nothing kept aside is
5863/// put back, and the end half follows (`\endproof` after a file's own `\proof`).
5864fn drop_copies_lock_aside(cs: &str) {
5865  if copies_lock(cs).is_some() && copies_lock_kept_aside(cs) {
5866    assign_value(
5867      &s!("{cs}:locked@cleared"),
5868      Stored::None,
5869      Some(Scope::Global),
5870    );
5871  }
5872}
5873
5874/// Record the default of the optional argument a refused redefinition of `token` takes first, as
5875/// `"{cs}:redefined@default"` (`[1][\proofname]`), for the locked binding to title its own use with — only under a
5876/// lock that holds only against copies ([`copies_lock`]), whose binding reads it.
5877pub fn record_redefined_default(token: &Token, default: Tokens) {
5878  if token.with_cs_name(copies_lock).is_none() {
5879    return;
5880  }
5881  let key = arena::pin(token.with_cs_name(|cs| s!("{cs}:redefined@default")));
5882  state_mut!().assign_internal(
5883    TableName::Value,
5884    key,
5885    Stored::Tokens(default),
5886    Some(Scope::Global),
5887  );
5888}
5889
5890/// Whether a definition takes an optional argument first whose default is not empty — `[1][\proofname]`
5891/// (amsthm.sty:431), `[1][\thu@proof@name]` (thuthesis.cls:4052): the shape of a copy of amsthm's proof, whose
5892/// optional argument is the head's text and whose default the name, which a lock that holds only against copies reads
5893/// ([`files_lock_holds`]). An empty default (`[1][]`) adds to a head of the file's own (thmbox.sty:214
5894/// `\proofname\ #1`) or makes a command, not a proof environment (nccthm.sty:173 `\newcommand*{\proof}[1][]`).
5895///
5896/// A robust definition is judged by what it wraps, as [`owns_calls`] judges a robust binding: its own meaning is the
5897/// parameterless `\protect \cs␣` (`robust_wrapper_body`), defined after the inner `\cs␣` that takes the arguments
5898/// (`\DeclareRobustCommand\proof[1][\proofname]{…}`).
5899pub fn takes_optional_first(definition: &Stored) -> bool {
5900  copy_default(definition).is_some_and(|default| !default.is_empty())
5901}
5902
5903/// The parameters `definition` declares.
5904fn definition_parameters(definition: &Stored) -> Option<&crate::parameter::Parameters> {
5905  match definition {
5906    Stored::Expandable(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5907    Stored::Constructor(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5908    Stored::Primitive(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5909    Stored::MathPrimitive(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5910    _ => None,
5911  }
5912}
5913
5914/// The default of the optional argument `definition` takes first, if it takes one first ([`takes_optional_first`];
5915/// a robust definition's by its inner `\cs␣`).
5916fn copy_default(definition: &Stored) -> Option<Tokens> {
5917  if let Stored::Expandable(defn) = definition
5918    && let Some(inner) = robust_wrapper_body(defn, &defn.get_cs())
5919  {
5920    return lookup_meaning(&inner).and_then(|inner| copy_default(&inner));
5921  }
5922  definition_parameters(definition)
5923    .and_then(|params| params.get_parameters().into_iter().next())
5924    .filter(|param| param.optional)
5925    .and_then(|param| param.extra.first().cloned())
5926}
5927
5928/// Whether the locked `binding` of `cs` owns its calls, so that the document's own definition of `cs` takes its other
5929/// uses ([`install_definition`]): its signature reads a braced argument — its first mandatory parameter is one of
5930/// [`BRACED_ARGUMENT_TYPES`], after optional ones only (`\text{}`, `\title[]{}`, `\cite OptionalMatch:* [][]
5931/// Semiverbatim`) — or it is itself such an override (a loop redefining it on every iteration). Derived from the
5932/// binding's own parameter list; a binding that reads its argument downstream (`\section`'s `\@startsection`,
5933/// `\footnote`, `\url`) or none (`\maketitle`) stays absolutely locked. So does the machinery (user ruling
5934/// 2026-10-11: the machinery stays absolutely locked): LaTeX's internal namespace, a name with `@`, which LaTeX's
5935/// convention keeps out of a document's reach until `\makeatletter` (clsguide.tex:187, latex.ltx:1797).
5936/// Its locks keep LaTeXML's engine model against code that re-implements it (`\alloc@{}{}{}{}{}`, called
5937/// `\alloc@0\count\countdef\insc@unt`; `\@startsection`, `\@caption`, `\@lbibitem`).
5938pub fn owns_calls(cs: &Token, binding: &Stored) -> bool {
5939  if cs.with_cs_name(|name| name.contains('@')) {
5940    return false;
5941  }
5942  // A robust binding is judged by what it wraps (user ruling 2026-10-15, D1 extends to robust bindings): its own
5943  // meaning is the parameterless `\protect \cs␣` ([`robust_wrapper_body`]), its signature the inner `\cs␣`'s — the
5944  // kernel `\cite` (sect11.rs) reads `[] Semiverbatim` there. Witnesses math0308041, math0612670, 0705.2148,
5945  // 0802.3843, 1207.1752, 1401.4197 (eplain.tex:346 `\edef\cite{\the\catcode`@}`; SYNC_STATUS 67n).
5946  if let Stored::Expandable(defn) = binding
5947    && !defn.yields_calls()
5948    && let Some(inner) = robust_wrapper_body(defn, cs)
5949  {
5950    return lookup_meaning(&inner).is_some_and(|inner_binding| owns_calls(&inner, &inner_binding));
5951  }
5952  let params = match binding {
5953    Stored::Expandable(defn) if defn.yields_calls() => return true,
5954    Stored::Expandable(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5955    Stored::Constructor(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5956    Stored::Primitive(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5957    Stored::MathPrimitive(defn) => defn.get_parameters(),
5958    _ => None,
5959  };
5960  params
5961    .and_then(|params| {
5962      params
5963        .get_parameters()
5964        .into_iter()
5965        .find(|param| !param.optional)
5966    })
5967    .is_some_and(|param| arena::with(param.name, |name| BRACED_ARGUMENT_TYPES.contains(&name)))
5968}
5969
5970/// Whether the document's definition `defn` of `cs` is a VALUE — no parameters — rather than a command
5971/// ([`install_definition`]): neither it nor what it wraps reads an argument. A robust wrapper (`\protect \cs␣`,
5972/// [`robust_wrapper_body`]: `\DeclareRobustCommand\mathit[1]{…}`) is judged by its inner `\cs␣`, an ltcmd wrapper
5973/// (`\__cmd_start_optimized: \cs code`, `\__cmd_start:nNNnnn {o m}…`, `\__cmd_start_expandable:nNNNNn {…}…`:
5974/// `\RenewDocumentCommand\mathbf{m}{…}`) by its signature: written out (braced) in the general and expandable wrappers,
5975/// and for the optimized one the argument count ltcmd generates `\cs code` from right after the wrapper
5976/// (`\l__cmd_current_arg_int`, latex.ltx:1963-1983 `\__cmd_declare_cmd_optimized:Nnn`) (2306.08511, 2306.10719,
5977/// 2306.05287).
5978fn defines_value(cs: &Token, defn: &Expandable) -> bool {
5979  let reads_arguments = |params: Option<&crate::parameter::Parameters>| {
5980    params.is_some_and(|params| !params.get_parameters().is_empty())
5981  };
5982  let wrapped_reads_arguments = |inner: &Token| {
5983    lookup_definition(inner)
5984      .ok()
5985      .flatten()
5986      .is_some_and(|inner| reads_arguments(inner.get_parameters()))
5987  };
5988  if reads_arguments(defn.get_parameters()) {
5989    return false;
5990  }
5991  if let Some(inner) = robust_wrapper_body(defn, cs) {
5992    return !wrapped_reads_arguments(&inner);
5993  }
5994  let Some(ExpansionBody::Tokens(body)) = defn.get_expansion() else {
5995    return true;
5996  };
5997  let body = body.unlist_ref();
5998  if !body
5999    .first()
6000    .is_some_and(|first| first.with_str(|name| name.starts_with("\\__cmd_start")))
6001  {
6002    return true;
6003  }
6004  // `\__cmd_start:nNNnnn {<signature>}…`: a non-empty signature reads arguments.
6005  let signature_reads = body
6006    .get(1)
6007    .is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() == Catcode::BEGIN)
6008    && body.get(2).is_some_and(|t| t.get_catcode() != Catcode::END);
6009  let optimized_count = lookup_definition(&T_CS!("\\l__cmd_current_arg_int"))
6010    .ok()
6011    .flatten()
6012    .and_then(|count| count.value_of(Vec::new()))
6013    .is_some_and(|count| matches!(count, RegisterValue::Number(n) if n.value_of() > 0));
6014  let optimized = body
6015    .first()
6016    .is_some_and(|first| first.with_str(|name| name == "\\__cmd_start_optimized:"));
6017  // Not `\cs code`: ltcmd installs the wrapper before it rebuilds `\cs code`, which is still the previous
6018  // declaration's (`\RenewDocumentCommand\text{m}{…}` then `\RenewDocumentCommand\text{}{V}` is a value).
6019  !signature_reads && !(optimized && optimized_count)
6020}
6021
6022/// The parameter types that read one TeX argument, a braced group (`read_arg`/`read_balanced`, base_parameter_types.rs):
6023/// a binding whose first mandatory parameter is one of them takes a braced argument ([`owns_calls`]).
6024pub const BRACED_ARGUMENT_TYPES: &[&str] = &[
6025  "Plain",
6026  "Position",
6027  "Semiverbatim",
6028  "ExpandedSemiverbatim",
6029  "UnexpandedSemiverbatim",
6030  "Undigested",
6031  "Digested",
6032  "Expanded",
6033  "ExpandedPartially",
6034  "ProtectedExpanded",
6035  "Verbatim",
6036  "HyperVerbatim",
6037  "RequiredKeyVals",
6038  "RequiredKeyValsStar",
6039  "RequiredKeyValsPlus",
6040  "RequiredKeyValsStarPlus",
6041  "DisplayStyle",
6042  "TextStyle",
6043  "ScriptStyle",
6044  "ScriptscriptStyle",
6045  "InFractionStyle",
6046  "InScriptStyle",
6047];
6048
6049/// `\lx@locked@<cs>`: where a document's override of the locked `cs` keeps the binding that handles its calls
6050/// ([`install_definition`]). The binding object itself, so its reversion still names `cs`.
6051pub fn locked_binding_token(cs: &Token) -> Token {
6052  T_CS!(cs.with_cs_name(|name| s!("\\lx@locked@{}", name.trim_start_matches('\\'))))
6053}
6054
6055/// The inner `\cs␣` that the robust command `cs` wraps (its meaning the parameterless `\protect \cs␣`:
6056/// `\DeclareRobustCommand`, a binding's `robust => true`, [`let_i`]'s copy of either), whose definition takes the
6057/// arguments; `None` for any other meaning.
6058pub fn robust_inner(cs: &Token) -> Option<Token> {
6059  match lookup_meaning(cs) {
6060    Some(Stored::Expandable(ref wrapper)) => robust_wrapper_body(wrapper, &wrapper.get_cs()),
6061    _ => None,
6062  }
6063}
6064
6065/// The other tokens that open a call of `binding` (a [`locked_binding_token`]) besides an explicit `{`: the `[` of
6066/// each optional argument its signature opens with, and the token of an `OptionalMatch:*`. A robust binding's
6067/// signature is its inner `\cs␣`'s, as in [`owns_calls`]: `\cite[p.~3]{x}` opens the kernel `\cite`.
6068pub fn call_openers(binding: &Token) -> Vec<Token> {
6069  let inner = robust_inner(binding);
6070  let Ok(Some(defn)) = lookup_definition(inner.as_ref().unwrap_or(binding)) else {
6071    return Vec::new();
6072  };
6073  let mut openers = Vec::new();
6074  if let Some(params) = defn.get_parameters() {
6075    for param in params
6076      .get_parameters()
6077      .into_iter()
6078      .take_while(|param| param.optional)
6079    {
6080      if arena::with(param.name, |name| name == "OptionalMatch") {
6081        openers.extend(
6082          param
6083            .extra
6084            .first()
6085            .and_then(|extra| extra.unlist_ref().first().copied()),
6086        );
6087      } else if !param.novalue {
6088        openers.push(T_OTHER!("["));
6089      }
6090    }
6091  }
6092  openers
6093}

6095/// Every control sequence carrying a `"{cs}:locked"` lock, sorted: for the census of which own their calls
6096/// ([`owns_calls`]).
6097pub fn locked_control_sequences() -> Vec<String> {
6098  let state = state!();
6099  let mut names: Vec<String> = state
6100    .value
6101    .iter()
6102    .filter(|(_, values)| matches!(values.front(), Some(Stored::Bool(true))))
6103    .filter_map(|(key, _)| {
6104      arena::with(*key, |key| {
6105        key.strip_suffix(":locked").map(ToString::to_string)
6106      })
6107    })
6108    .collect();
6109  names.sort();
6110  names
6111}
6112
6113/// $meaning should be a definition (for defining active control sequences)
6114/// or another token, for \let
6115pub fn assign_meaning<T: Into<Stored>>(token: &Token, meaning: T, scope: Option<Scope>) {
6116  let mut meaning = meaning.into();
6117  // short-circuit guard to avoid e.g. T_MATH let to itself
6118  if let Stored::Token(ref mt) = meaning
6119    && token == mt
6120  {
6121    return;
6122  }
6123  // For \let chains: if the target token has an expandable/primitive definition,
6124  // store that definition directly instead of the Token indirection.
6125  // This ensures `\let \foo \bar` where \bar is expandable makes \foo expandable too.
6126  // Follow at most 50 \let links to avoid cycles.
6127  if let Stored::Token(ref target) = meaning {
6128    let mut current = *target;
6129    for _ in 0..50 {
6130      match lookup_meaning(&current) {
6131        Some(Stored::Token(next)) => {
6132          current = next; // follow chain
6133        },
6134        Some(Stored::None) | None => break, // dead end — keep as Token
6135        Some(defn) => {
6136          // Found a real definition — use it directly
6137          meaning = defn;
6138          break;
6139        },
6140      }
6141    }
6142  }
6143  note_copy_provides_assignment(token);
6144  let csname_sym = token.pin_cs_name();
6145  state_mut!().assign_internal(TableName::Meaning, csname_sym, meaning, scope);
6146}
6147
6148/// Re-point every `\let` copy of `cs`'s current definition — any other meaning bound to that very
6149/// definition (the same `Rc`: taken while it was in force, and not redefined since) — to `new`.
6150/// Each binding is replaced in place, so a copy keeps its scope: a local copy stays local, and one
6151/// a group shadows is `new` again when the group ends. Returns how many bindings were re-pointed;
6152/// `cs` itself is left to the caller, which installs `new` for it. For a definition TeX treats as
6153/// fixed before any code runs but installed late here: the job's `\jobname`
6154/// (`core_interface::install_jobname`).
6155///
6156/// The walk over the meaning table is bounded by the definition's holders. Each holder besides
6157/// `cs`'s own binding and this lookup may be a copy, so a definition nobody else holds needs no
6158/// walk (no preload ran before the job), and the walk stops once as many copies as holders are
6159/// found. A holder outside the table (a snapshot) leaves the bound loose, never short.
6160pub fn rebind_let_copies(cs: &Token, new: &Stored) -> usize {
6161  let Some(Stored::Expandable(old_def)) = lookup_meaning(cs) else {
6162    return 0;
6163  };
6164  let mut unseen = Rc::strong_count(&old_def).saturating_sub(2);
6165  if unseen == 0 {
6166    return 0;
6167  }
6168  let own = meaning_key(cs);
6169  let mut rebound = 0;
6170  let mut state = state_mut!();
6171  // (the keys holding a copy, journaled — and recorded — before they are rewritten in place)
6172  if state.journal.is_some() || !state.meaning_recorders.is_empty() {
6173    let copies: Vec<SymStr> = state
6174      .meaning
6175      .iter()
6176      .filter(|(key, values)| {
6177        **key != own
6178          && values
6179            .iter()
6180            .any(|value| matches!(value, Stored::Expandable(def) if Rc::ptr_eq(def, &old_def)))
6181      })
6182      .map(|(key, _)| *key)
6183      .collect();
6184    for key in copies {
6185      state.journal_touch(TableName::Meaning, key);
6186    }
6187  }
6188  'walk: for (key, values) in state.table_mut(TableName::Meaning).iter_mut() {
6189    if *key == own {
6190      continue;
6191    }
6192    for value in values.iter_mut() {
6193      if matches!(value, Stored::Expandable(def) if Rc::ptr_eq(def, &old_def)) {
6194        *value = new.clone();
6195        rebound += 1;
6196        unseen -= 1;
6197        if unseen == 0 {
6198          break 'walk;
6199        }
6200      }
6201    }
6202  }
6203  rebound
6204}
6205
6206/// Remove a token's meaning entirely — the token becomes undefined, as if it
6207/// had never been defined, so a later use takes the normal undefined-CS error
6208/// path naming the token itself. Bypasses the group-undo journal: intended
6209/// ONLY for format-bootstrap time (no user groups open), where a format layer
6210/// retracts a definition inherited from a lower layer that the emulated
6211/// format must not expose (e.g. plain.tex's `\+` in a LaTeX session — real
6212/// LaTeX is INITEX-based and never defines it).
6213pub fn remove_meaning_global(token: &Token) {
6214  let key = meaning_key(token);
6215  let mut state = state_mut!();
6216  state.journal_touch(TableName::Meaning, key);
6217  state.meaning.remove(&key);
6218}
6219
6220/// Begin recording what assignments change, to undo them all ([`AssignmentJournalGuard::rollback`]) or keep them
6221/// ([`AssignmentJournalGuard::keep`]; also on drop, so a panic leaves no journal open): a transaction over the binding
6222/// tables, of every assignment however scoped and of the in-place edits of the tables (`push_value`,
6223/// `assign_mapping`, `lookup_value_mut`, `checkout_value`/`checkin_value`, named-scope activation,
6224/// `rebind_let_copies`, the graphics path list), of a font's parameter array (`set_font_parameter`, saved as a copy), an input
6225/// stream's mouth (`input_stream_to_change`) and a box register's dimensions (`journal_box_dimension`), each edited in place,
6226/// and of a name's standing in the fallback-guess set
6227/// (`AssignmentJournal::fallback`, FM S2a.2 r9: siamart's `\AND`, 2409.10200). Not of what lives outside the tables: the
6228/// provide-only and stand-in name sets, which change only while a package or class loads. Made for the replay of a refused
6229/// frontmatter setter (sect05.rs `replay_class_setter`), which a setter that errs half-way must not leave half-done
6230/// (jsty3-author.sty:542's expl3 author list, which its printer then looped over: 2607.24171). `None`, recording
6231/// nothing, while one is already open (journals do not nest); a journal its guard could not close (the state was
6232/// borrowed when the guard dropped) is discarded here.
6233pub fn begin_assignment_journal() -> Option<AssignmentJournalGuard> {
6234  let mut state = state_mut!();
6235  if state.journal.is_some() && JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.get() {
6236    return None;
6237  }
6238  let frames = state.undo.iter().rev().map(|frame| frame.id).collect();
6239  state.journal = Some(AssignmentJournal {
6240    meaning_log: None,
6241    frames,
6242    saved: rustc_hash::FxHashMap::default(),
6243    fallback: rustc_hash::FxHashMap::default(),
6244    box_dimensions: Vec::new(),
6245    watch: WATCH.get(),
6246  });
6247  JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.set(true);
6248  WRITES_WATCHED.set(true);
6249  Some(AssignmentJournalGuard { open: true })
6250}
6251
6252/// Begin recording which meanings are assigned, each with its value before its first assignment since
6253/// ([`end_meaning_recorder`]): what [`AssignmentJournalGuard::assigned`] reads of [`TableName::Meaning`], without the
6254/// journal — recorders nest, and a journal (a speculative run's, a replay's) may open and close inside one. Made for a
6255/// displaced setter's call (frontmatter_stores.rs `\lx@displaced@begin`), whose own code may run a speculative label
6256/// read (queue_digest.rs `speculative_label_ink`: utarticle.cls:125's `\address` prints its `\@makeadmark{\@tempb}`,
6257/// 1209.6020). Kept in the state, so a document that stops inside a call leaves none to the next. Returns its depth:
6258/// how many were open before it, which [`end_meaning_recorder_at`] closes it by.
6259pub fn begin_meaning_recorder() -> usize {
6260  let recorders = &mut state_mut!().meaning_recorders;
6261  recorders.push(rustc_hash::FxHashMap::default());
6262  recorders.len() - 1
6263}
6264
6265/// Close the innermost meaning recorder ([`begin_meaning_recorder`]): the meanings assigned since it began, each with
6266/// its value then (`None`: unbound). Empty when none is open.
6267pub fn end_meaning_recorder() -> Vec<(SymStr, Option<Stored>)> {
6268  state_mut!()
6269    .meaning_recorders
6270    .pop()
6271    .map(|recorded| recorded.into_iter().collect())
6272    .unwrap_or_default()
6273}
6274
6275/// Close the meaning recorder [`begin_meaning_recorder`] opened at `depth`, and every one opened inside it and left open
6276/// (an `Err` out of a displaced call between its begin and its end): the meanings it recorded, as
6277/// [`end_meaning_recorder`]; empty when it is no longer open.
6278pub fn end_meaning_recorder_at(depth: usize) -> Vec<(SymStr, Option<Stored>)> {
6279  let recorders = &mut state_mut!().meaning_recorders;
6280  if recorders.len() <= depth {
6281    return Vec::new();
6282  }
6283  recorders.truncate(depth + 1);
6284  recorders
6285    .pop()
6286    .map(|recorded| recorded.into_iter().collect())
6287    .unwrap_or_default()
6288}
6289
6290/// A meaning assigned in the meaning log ([`begin_meaning_log`]).
6291#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
6292pub struct MeaningAssigned {
6293  /// the meaning's key
6294  pub key:    SymStr,
6295  /// whether globally
6296  pub global: bool,
6297  /// the token being invoked
6298  pub by:     Token,
6299}
6300
6301/// Begin logging the meanings assigned on the open journal by a token with a field provenance (`Token::prov`), in order:
6302/// what a rehearsal reads a definition in its field by (the engine's `field_notes`), since a
6303/// definition leaves no box. What a token of no provenance assigns is no invocation of the field's, wherever it stands:
6304/// `\begin{minipage}`'s hooks, the `\size@update` that `\selectfont` puts back. One rehearsal at a time, journals not nesting
6305/// ([`begin_assignment_journal`]); the log goes with the journal.
6306pub fn begin_meaning_log() {
6307  if let Some(journal) = state_mut!().journal.as_mut() {
6308    journal.meaning_log = Some(Vec::new());
6309  }
6310}
6311
6312/// Whether the meaning log ([`begin_meaning_log`]) holds nothing since it was last read.
6313pub fn meaning_log_is_empty() -> bool {
6314  state!()
6315    .journal
6316    .as_ref()
6317    .and_then(|journal| journal.meaning_log.as_ref())
6318    .is_none_or(Vec::is_empty)
6319}
6320
6321/// Exchange what the meaning log ([`begin_meaning_log`]) holds since it was last read with `buffer`, an empty one: a field
6322/// token's read of the log, so it allocates nothing.
6323pub fn swap_meaning_log(buffer: &mut Vec<MeaningAssigned>) {
6324  if let Some(log) = state_mut!()
6325    .journal
6326    .as_mut()
6327    .and_then(|journal| journal.meaning_log.as_mut())
6328  {
6329    std::mem::swap(log, buffer);
6330  }
6331}
6332
6333#[thread_local]
6334/// Whether an [`AssignmentJournalGuard`] is live: one that dropped while the state was borrowed leaves its journal
6335/// for the next [`begin_assignment_journal`] to discard.
6336static JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE: std::cell::Cell<bool> = std::cell::Cell::new(false);
6337
6338#[thread_local]
6339/// The watch held on the state ([`watch_state`]): its number, 0 when none is or the state changed since.
6340static WATCH: std::cell::Cell<u64> = std::cell::Cell::new(0);
6341
6342#[thread_local]
6343/// Whether a write is noted: a journal's guard is live or a watch is held. The one test a write pays.
6344static WRITES_WATCHED: std::cell::Cell<bool> = std::cell::Cell::new(false);
6345
6346/// Set the watch held ([`WATCH`]), 0 to end it.
6347fn set_watch(watch: u64) {
6348  WATCH.set(watch);
6349  WRITES_WATCHED.set(watch != 0 || JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.get());
6350}
6351
6352/// The journal's guard is gone ([`JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE`]).
6353fn end_journal_guard() {
6354  JOURNAL_GUARD_LIVE.set(false);
6355  WRITES_WATCHED.set(WATCH.get() != 0);
6356}
6357
6358#[thread_local]
6359/// The last watch begun: numbers only grow, so none is begun twice.
6360static LAST_WATCH: std::cell::Cell<u64> = std::cell::Cell::new(0);
6361
6362/// A write to `key` of `table_name`, in place or not ([`State::journal_touch`]): it ends the watch held, but a
6363/// LaTeXML-internal value's (a key `lx…` in the value table: a frontmatter flag, which no TeX code reads).
6364#[inline]
6365fn note_state_change(table_name: TableName, key: SymStr) {
6366  if WATCH.get() != 0
6367    && (table_name != TableName::Value || arena::first_bytes::<2>(key) != Some(*b"lx"))
6368  {
6369    set_watch(0);
6370  }
6371}
6372
6373/// Begin a watch on the state, ending the one held (the title-page reading's key, `latex_constructs::title_page_reading`).
6374/// Every write ends it (an assignment, a write in place, a box's `\wd`, a group's end restoring a value) but an `lx…`
6375/// value's; a rollback ([`AssignmentJournalGuard::rollback`]) puts back the watch it began with, with the state.
6376pub fn watch_state() -> u64 {
6377  let watch = LAST_WATCH.get() + 1;
6378  LAST_WATCH.set(watch);
6379  set_watch(watch);
6380  watch
6381}
6382
6383/// Is the state unchanged since `watch` began ([`watch_state`])?
6384pub fn state_unchanged_since(watch: u64) -> bool { watch != 0 && WATCH.get() == watch }
6385
6386/// The open assignment journal ([`begin_assignment_journal`]).
6387#[must_use = "an assignment journal is kept or rolled back"]
6388pub struct AssignmentJournalGuard {
6389  open: bool,
6390}
6391
6392impl AssignmentJournalGuard {
6393  /// The keys of `table` the journal saw assigned since it began, each with its value then (the front of its stack;
6394  /// `None`: unbound).
6395  pub fn assigned(&self, table: TableName) -> Vec<(SymStr, Option<Stored>)> {
6396    let state = state!();
6397    let Some(journal) = state.journal.as_ref() else {
6398      return Vec::new();
6399    };
6400    journal
6401      .saved
6402      .iter()
6403      .filter(|((t, _), _)| *t == table)
6404      .map(|((_, key), (values, _))| (*key, values.as_ref().and_then(|v| v.front().cloned())))
6405      .collect()
6406  }
6407
6408  /// Close the journal, keeping every assignment made since it began.
6409  pub fn keep(mut self) {
6410    self.open = false;
6411    end_journal_guard();
6412    state_mut!().journal = None;
6413  }
6414
6415  /// Close the journal, undoing every assignment made since it began: each key's values and its counts in the frames
6416  /// open then are put back as they were. Only when exactly those frames are open again — the same frames, by id,
6417  /// each one opened since closed and none of them closed: false, undoing nothing (the assignments stay), otherwise.
6418  pub fn rollback(mut self) -> bool {
6419    self.open = false;
6420    end_journal_guard();
6421    let mut state = state_mut!();
6422    let Some(journal) = state.journal.take() else {
6423      return false;
6424    };
6425    if !state
6426      .undo
6427      .iter()
6428      .rev()
6429      .map(|frame| frame.id)
6430      .eq(journal.frames.iter().copied())
6431    {
6432      return false;
6433    }
6434    for ((table_name, key), (values, counts)) in journal.saved {
6435      match values {
6436        Some(values) => {
6437          state.table_mut(table_name).insert(key, values);
6438        },
6439        None => {
6440          state.table_mut(table_name).remove(&key);
6441        },
6442      }
6443      for frame in state.undo.iter_mut() {
6444        let id = frame.id;
6445        let frame_table = frame.table_mut(table_name);
6446        frame_table.remove(&key);
6447        if let Some((_, n)) = counts.iter().find(|(at, _)| *at == id) {
6448          frame_table.insert(key, *n);
6449        }
6450      }
6451    }
6452    for (key, standing) in journal.fallback {
6453      match standing {
6454        Some(origin) => {
6455          state.fallback_meanings.insert(key, origin);
6456        },
6457        None => {
6458          state.fallback_meanings.remove(&key);
6459        },
6460      }
6461    }
6462    for (a_box, property, value) in journal.box_dimensions.into_iter().rev() {
6463      match value {
6464        Some(value) => {
6465          a_box.try_set_property(property, value);
6466        },
6467        None => {
6468          a_box.try_remove_property(property);
6469        },
6470      }
6471    }
6472    set_watch(journal.watch);
6473    true
6474  }
6475}
6476
6477impl Drop for AssignmentJournalGuard {
6478  fn drop(&mut self) {
6479    if self.open {
6480      end_journal_guard();
6481      if let Ok(mut state) = STATE.try_borrow_mut() {
6482        state.journal = None;
6483      }
6484    }
6485  }
6486}
6487
6488/// Every control sequence whose current meaning satisfies `keep`, with that meaning: a whole-table scan, for a
6489/// one-off survey of what a loaded file defined (K11's reader scan, `frontmatter_stores.rs`), never a hot path.
6490pub fn current_meanings_where(keep: impl Fn(&Stored) -> bool) -> Vec<(SymStr, Stored)> {
6491  state!()
6492    .meaning
6493    .iter()
6494    .filter_map(|(key, entry)| match entry.front() {
6495      Some(stored) if !matches!(stored, Stored::None) && keep(stored) => {
6496        Some((*key, stored.clone()))
6497      },
6498      _ => None,
6499    })
6500    .collect()
6501}
6502
6503/// Whether the meaning key `key` (a [`meaning_key`]) is bound now.
6504pub fn has_meaning_key(key: SymStr) -> bool { !matches!(meaning_by_key(key), Stored::None) }
6505
6506// keep this in sync with `lookup_meaning`, it is copied over for optimization purposes
6507pub fn has_meaning(token: &Token) -> bool {
6508  if token.get_catcode().is_active_or_cs() && token.text != pin!("") {
6509    match state!().meaning.get(&meaning_key(token)) {
6510      Some(entry) => match entry.front() {
6511        None | Some(Stored::None) => false,
6512        Some(_) => true,
6513      },
6514      None => false,
6515    }
6516  } else {
6517    true
6518  }
6519}
6520
6521/// used for expansion & various queries
6522/// Since we're not doing digestion here, we don't need to handle mathactive,
6523/// nor cs let to executable tokens
6524/// This returns a definition object, or undef
6525pub fn lookup_definition(key: &Token) -> Result<Option<Rc<dyn Definition>>> {
6526  Ok(
6527    if let Some(defs) = state!().lookup_definition_internal(key) {
6528      match defs.front() {
6529        Some(Stored::Conditional(entry)) => Some(entry.clone()),
6530        Some(Stored::Constructor(entry)) => Some(entry.clone()),
6531        Some(Stored::Expandable(entry)) => Some(entry.clone()),
6532        Some(Stored::MathPrimitive(entry)) => Some(entry.clone()),
6533        Some(Stored::Primitive(entry)) => Some(entry.clone()),
6534        Some(Stored::Register(entry)) => Some(entry.clone()),
6535        Some(Stored::None) | Some(Stored::Token(_)) | None => None,
6536        Some(v) => {
6537          let message = s!("in lookup_definition for {:?}. Value was: {:?}", key, v);
6538          Error!("unexpected", "value", message);
6539          None
6540        },
6541      }
6542    } else {
6543      None
6544    },
6545  )
6546}
6547
6548/// Returns a definition as `Stored` so that one can call `.read_arguments`
6549///
6550/// This can't be specialized during compile-time over a trait object?
6551/// Instead we'll dispatch via `Stored` at runtime, to allow generic calls.
6552pub fn lookup_definition_stored(key: &Token) -> Result<Option<Stored>> {
6553  Ok(match state!().lookup_definition_internal(key) {
6554    Some(defs) => match defs.front() {
6555      // Still, good time to handle the Token case and catch weird storage errors
6556      Some(Stored::Conditional(entry)) => Some(Stored::Conditional(Rc::clone(entry))),
6557      Some(Stored::Constructor(entry)) => Some(Stored::Constructor(Rc::clone(entry))),
6558      Some(Stored::Expandable(entry)) => Some(Stored::Expandable(Rc::clone(entry))),
6559      Some(Stored::MathPrimitive(entry)) => Some(Stored::MathPrimitive(Rc::clone(entry))),
6560      Some(Stored::Primitive(entry)) => Some(Stored::Primitive(Rc::clone(entry))),
6561      Some(Stored::Register(entry)) => Some(Stored::Register(Rc::clone(entry))),
6562      Some(Stored::Token(entry)) => Some(Stored::Expandable(Rc::new(Expandable {
6563        cs: key.with_str(|k| T_CS!(k)),
6564        paramlist: None,
6565        expansion: (*entry).into(),
6566        ..Expandable::default()
6567      }))),
6568      // A control sequence `\let` to an undefined one (`let_i`) has no definition, as in
6569      // `lookup_definition`.
6570      Some(Stored::None) => None,
6571      Some(v) => {
6572        let message = s!("in lookup_definition for {:?}. Value was: {:?}", key, v);
6573        Error!("unexpected", "value", message);
6574        None
6575      },
6576      None => None,
6577    },
6578    _ => None,
6579  })
6580}
6581
6582/// A specialized version of `lookup_definition` for registers, since we can't adequately perform
6583/// multi-dispatch when we have a "Self: Sized" for the Definition trait object.
6584pub fn lookup_register_definition(key: &Token) -> Option<Rc<Register>> {
6585  match state!().lookup_definition_internal(key) {
6586    Some(defs) => match defs.front() {
6587      Some(Stored::Register(entry)) => Some(Rc::clone(entry)),
6588      _ => None,
6589    },
6590    _ => None,
6591  }
6592}
6593/// Recognizes mathactive tokens in math mode and also looks for
6594/// cs that have been let to other `executable' tokens.
6595/// Returns a definition object, or a "self inserting" token.
6596/// Used for digestion.
6597pub fn lookup_digestable_definition(token: &Token) -> Option<Stored> {
6598  let cc = token.get_catcode();
6599  let t_sym = token.get_sym();
6600  let is_active_or_cs = cc.is_active_or_cs();
6601  let lookup_sym = if is_active_or_cs
6602    || ((cc == Catcode::LETTER || (cc == Catcode::OTHER))
6603      && lookup_bool_sym(crate::pin!("IN_MATH"))
6604      && (lookup_mathcode_sym(t_sym).unwrap_or(0) == 0x8000)
6605      && !crate::stomach::digesting_delimiter())
6606  {
6607    // `\special_relax`-family tokens digest under the bare `\special_relax` no-op.
6608    meaning_key(token)
6609  } else {
6610    // Use cached SymStr from `Catcode::name_sym` instead of re-interning
6611    // `cc.name()` (a &'static str) on every non-active-or-cs token —
6612    // saves a hashmap probe per token on the digest hot path.
6613    cc.name_sym()
6614  };
6615  // Debug!("Looking up digestable {:?}", lookupname);
6616  let state = state!();
6617  let entry_opt = state.meaning.get(&lookup_sym);
6618  if lookup_sym != pin!("") && entry_opt.is_some() && !entry_opt.as_ref().unwrap().is_empty() {
6619    // Debug!("Found definition for: {:?}", lookupname);
6620    if let Some(entry) = entry_opt
6621      && let Some(front) = entry.front()
6622    {
6623      if let Stored::Token(t) = front {
6624        if let Some(lookup_name) = t.get_executable_primitive_name() {
6625          let lookup_sym = arena::pin(lookup_name);
6626          if let Some(retry_entry) = state!().meaning.get(&lookup_sym) {
6627            // special case,
6628            // If a cs has been let to an executable token, lookup ITS defn.
6629            return retry_entry.front().cloned();
6630          }
6631        }
6632        // Also follow \let chains for CS tokens: if \foo is \let to \bar,
6633        // resolve \bar's definition. This handles expl3 aliases like
6634        // \tex_long:D → \long, \tex_gdef:D → \gdef.
6635        if t.get_catcode() == Catcode::CS
6636          && let Some(target_entry) = state.meaning.get(&t.text)
6637          && let Some(target_front) = target_entry.front()
6638          && !matches!(target_front, Stored::Token(_) | Stored::None)
6639        {
6640          return Some(target_front.clone());
6641        }
6642      }
6643      // Perl State.pm:474 lookupDigestableDefinition: the guard
6644      // `($defn = $$entry[0])` is FALSE when the entry's value is undef, so
6645      // execution falls through to `return $token` (self-inserting) for a
6646      // LETTER/OTHER token and to `return undef` for an active/CS one. A
6647      // math-active LETTER/OTHER character whose active meaning was `\let`
6648      // to an undefined CS hits exactly this case — e.g. braket-style
6649      // `\Pr{A|B}`: the macro body does `\mathcode`\|=32768 \let|\SetVert`
6650      // with `\SetVert` itself undefined (neither our nor Perl's braket
6651      // binding defines it), leaving `|`'s meaning an explicit
6652      // `Stored::None`. Returning `Some(Stored::None)` here routed the `|`
6653      // to generateErrorStub ("The token T_OTHER[|] is not defined"); Perl
6654      // instead self-inserts the literal char. Mirror Perl: a None-valued
6655      // entry for a non-active/CS (math-active) char self-inserts; active/CS
6656      // tokens still fall to the `None` return below. Witness 1602.01342.
6657      if matches!(front, Stored::None) && !is_active_or_cs {
6658        return Some(token.into());
6659      }
6660      // if a regular definition, just return.
6661      return Some(front.clone());
6662    }
6663  } else if is_active_or_cs {
6664    return None;
6665  }
6666  Some(token.into())
6667}

6669// NOTE: Common usage patterns seem to be to lookup
6670//   expandable definitions
6671//   register values
6672//   conditionals
6673//   digestibles
6674// or just variants on testing defined-ness
6675// May be will introduce more clarity (possibly efficiency)
6676// to collect those more uniformly and implement here, or in Package

6678//======================================================================
6679/// Starts a new level of grouping.
6680/// Note that this is lower level than C<\bgroup>;
6681/// Diagnostic helper: dump the keys in undo`0`'s value table.
6682/// For temporary instrumentation only — no production callers should rely on this.
6683/// The newest `n` values bound to `key` in the Value table, newest first —
6684/// one entry per frame that bound it (`LXML_TRACE_FRAMES` group-stack dump).
6685pub fn value_history(key: &str, n: usize) -> Vec<String> {
6686  let key_sym = arena::pin(key);
6687  state!()
6688    .value
6689    .get(&key_sym)
6690    .map(|d| d.iter().take(n).map(|v| v.to_string()).collect())
6691    .unwrap_or_default()
6692}
6693
6694pub fn dump_top_frame_keys() -> String {
6695  let state = state!();
6696  let f0 = state.undo.front().expect("undo is non-empty");
6697  let mut entries: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
6698  for (k, v) in f0.table(TableName::Value).iter() {
6699    let val = state
6700      .value
6701      .get(k)
6702      .and_then(|vec| vec.front())
6703      .map(|s| format!("{s:?}"))
6704      .unwrap_or_else(|| "<none>".into());
6705    let ks: String = arena::with(*k, |s| s.to_string());
6706    entries.push(format!("{ks}=[{v}, {val}]"));
6707  }
6708  entries.sort();
6709  entries.join(", ")
6710}
6711
6712pub fn push_frame() {
6713  // Easy: just push a new undo frame.
6714  if *crate::stomach::TRACE_FRAMES {
6715    eprintln!(
6716      "[state] push_frame depth {} -> {}",
6717      get_frame_depth(),
6718      get_frame_depth() + 1
6719    );
6720  }
6721  state_mut!().undo.push_front(UndoFrame::default());
6722}
6723
6724/// Snapshot of the keys currently bound at the topmost (calling) undo frame
6725/// for the Meaning table. Used by Perl-style autoload triggers that need to
6726/// promote everything a package's load just installed at this scope to
6727/// GLOBAL — without that promotion, sibling autoload triggers fired AFTER
6728/// a group pop would re-fire on a now-undefined sibling CS (the canonical
6729/// case is `\begin{subequations}` triggering amsmath autoload at depth=N,
6730/// then a later `\begin{align}` at depth=0 finding `\align` undefined
6731/// because amsmath's depth=N install was popped on `\end{subequations}`).
6732pub fn snapshot_top_frame_meaning_keys() -> Vec<SymStr> {
6733  state!()
6734    .undo
6735    .front()
6736    .map(|f| f.meaning.keys().copied().collect())
6737    .unwrap_or_default()
6738}
6739
6740/// The meanings first made in the innermost group: the control sequences bound at the topmost frame and nowhere
6741/// outside it (every binding they have is the frame's), with their current binding (a key bound there to nothing is
6742/// left out).
6743pub fn top_frame_new_meanings() -> Vec<(SymStr, Stored)> {
6744  let state = state!();
6745  let Some(frame) = state.undo.front() else {
6746    return Vec::new();
6747  };
6748  frame
6749    .meaning
6750    .iter()
6751    .filter_map(|(key, count)| {
6752      let values = state.meaning.get(key)?;
6753      if values.len() > *count {
6754        return None;
6755      }
6756      values
6757        .front()
6758        .filter(|value| !matches!(value, Stored::None))
6759        .map(|value| (*key, value.clone()))
6760    })
6761    .collect()
6762}
6763
6764/// The meanings the topmost `n` frames bind — new ones and changes to what an outer frame defined — that are plain
6765/// groups (`{…}`, `\begingroup`): a frame an environment or a mode opened (it binds `current_environment` or `MODE`)
6766/// is skipped, its bindings being that construct's own machinery. Each key with the binding of the innermost such frame
6767/// that binds it (a key bound there to nothing is left out). For a replayed environment begin code (sect05.rs
6768/// `replay_class_setter`).
6769pub fn top_group_frames_meanings(n: usize) -> Vec<(SymStr, Stored)> {
6770  let state = state!();
6771  let environment = crate::pin!("current_environment");
6772  let mode = crate::pin!("MODE");
6773  // how many bindings of a key the frames above the current one hold: the current frame's is at that position
6774  let mut above: HashMap<SymStr, usize> = HashMap::default();
6775  let mut chosen: Vec<(SymStr, Stored)> = Vec::new();
6776  let mut seen: HashSet<SymStr> = HashSet::default();
6777  for frame in state.undo.iter().take(n) {
6778    let plain_group = !frame.value.contains_key(&environment) && !frame.value.contains_key(&mode);
6779    for (key, count) in frame.meaning.iter() {
6780      let offset = above.entry(*key).or_insert(0);
6781      if plain_group
6782        && *count > 0
6783        && seen.insert(*key)
6784        && let Some(value) = state
6785          .meaning
6786          .get(key)
6787          .and_then(|values| values.get(*offset))
6788        && !matches!(value, Stored::None)
6789      {
6790        chosen.push((*key, value.clone()));
6791      }
6792      *offset += *count;
6793    }
6794  }
6795  chosen
6796}
6797
6798/// The keys bound at the topmost frame for the Meaning and Value tables, taken
6799/// before an autoloaded package loads; see [`hoist_top_frame_package_load`].
6800pub struct TopFrameKeys {
6801  meaning: Vec<SymStr>,
6802  value:   Vec<SymStr>,
6803}
6804pub fn snapshot_top_frame_keys() -> TopFrameKeys {
6805  let state = state!();
6806  let frame = state.undo.front();
6807  TopFrameKeys {
6808    meaning: frame
6809      .map(|f| f.meaning.keys().copied().collect())
6810      .unwrap_or_default(),
6811    value:   frame
6812      .map(|f| f.value.keys().copied().collect())
6813      .unwrap_or_default(),
6814  }
6815}
6816
6817/// Hoist what an autoloaded package installed at the topmost frame since
6818/// `pre` was taken — its meanings and its values — to GLOBAL scope. The
6819/// trigger (`def_autoload`, OmniBus's `\lx@late@usepackage`, the LaTeX-pool
6820/// autoload) is cleared and the package's loaded-flag set globally, so what the
6821/// package defines must live as long. Fired inside a group, natbib's locked
6822/// `\citep` was otherwise popped while its lock survived, every later `\citep`
6823/// undefined (arXiv 2605.08349, 2605.10423, 2605.14513, 2605.04028:
6824/// `Fatal:TooManyErrors`), and its citation style reverted after the group.
6825pub fn hoist_top_frame_package_load(pre: &TopFrameKeys) {
6826  hoist_top_frame_delta(TableName::Meaning, &pre.meaning, false);
6827  hoist_top_frame_delta(TableName::Value, &pre.value, false);
6828}
6829
6830/// Hoist the conditionals installed at the topmost frame since `pre_snapshot`
6831/// was taken to GLOBAL scope: for a package loaded inside a LaTeXML subfile
6832/// bracket (`require_package`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN #65). Idempotent: keys already
6833/// in `pre_snapshot` are skipped.
6834pub fn hoist_top_frame_conditional_delta(pre_snapshot: &[SymStr]) {
6835  hoist_top_frame_delta(TableName::Meaning, pre_snapshot, true);
6836}
6837
6838fn hoist_top_frame_delta(table: TableName, pre_snapshot: &[SymStr], conditionals_only: bool) {
6839  let pre: rustc_hash::FxHashSet<SymStr> = pre_snapshot.iter().copied().collect();
6840  let new_keys: Vec<SymStr> = {
6841    let state = state!();
6842    state
6843      .undo
6844      .front()
6845      .map(|f| {
6846        f.table(table)
6847          .keys()
6848          .copied()
6849          .filter(|k| !pre.contains(k))
6850          .collect()
6851      })
6852      .unwrap_or_default()
6853  };
6854  for key in new_keys {
6855    let current = {
6856      let state = state!();
6857      state
6858        .table(table)
6859        .get(&key)
6860        .and_then(|stack| stack.front().cloned())
6861    };
6862    if let Some(value) = current {
6863      // CONDITIONALS ONLY, for a subfile bracket. The failure this exists for is a definition destroyed
6864      // while a GLOBAL document hook still reads it, and every witness is a
6865      // `\newif` conditional (`\ifpgf@external@grabshipout`, OXIDIZED_DESIGN
6866      // #65). Hoisting a package's ordinary macros too is what makes a second
6867      // sibling subfile render the FIRST one's content: promoting pkgA's
6868      // `\newcommand` to global makes pkgB's same-named `\newcommand` a silent
6869      // no-op, so sibling B shows A's body — silent wrong content, and worse
6870      // than Perl, which scopes both. `\newif` installs `\ifX` as a Conditional
6871      // (`\Xtrue`/`\Xfalse` are plain macros the hooks do not read), so this
6872      // filter keeps every witness working while leaving macros scoped.
6873      if conditionals_only && !matches!(value, Stored::Conditional(_)) {
6874        continue;
6875      }
6876      // Direct re-bind via assign_internal so we don't need to round-trip a
6877      // full Token. The Meaning table is keyed by SymStr (the CS name);
6878      // any future read via `assign_meaning(token, ...)` would reach the
6879      // same cell. Scope::Global removes higher-frame undo entries and
6880      // installs at the lowest non-locked frame. A scope move, not a new
6881      // definition: whatever origin is current, a meaning keeps its standing
6882      // as a fallback class's guess or not (`fallback_meanings`; the lazy
6883      // LaTeX.pool hoist had made a pool convenience ours and refused the
6884      // document's `\newcommand`). Guard:
6885      // `reentrancy_tests::package_load_hoist_keeps_fallback_standing`.
6886      let fallback = if table == TableName::Meaning {
6887        state!().fallback_meanings.get(&key).copied()
6888      } else {
6889        None
6890      };
6891      let mut state = state_mut!();
6892      state.assign_internal(table, key, value, Some(Scope::Global));
6893      if table == TableName::Meaning {
6894        if let Some(origin) = fallback {
6895          state.fallback_meanings.insert(key, origin);
6896        } else {
6897          state.fallback_meanings.remove(&key);
6898        }
6899      }
6900    }
6901  }
6902}
6903/// Ends the current level of grouping.
6904/// Note that this is lower level than `\egroup`;
6905pub fn pop_frame() -> Result<()> {
6906  if *crate::stomach::TRACE_FRAMES {
6907    eprintln!(
6908      "[state] pop_frame depth {} -> {}",
6909      get_frame_depth(),
6910      get_frame_depth().saturating_sub(1)
6911    );
6912  }
6913  let mut state = state_mut!();
6914  if state.undo.front().as_ref().unwrap().locked {
6915    fatal!(
6916      TargetUnexpected,
6917      Endgroup,
6918      "attempt to pop last locked stack frame"
6919    );
6920  // Fatal('unexpected', '<endgroup>', $self->getStomach,
6921  // "Attempt to pop last locked stack frame"); }
6922  } else {
6923    let popped_frame = state.undo.pop_front().unwrap();
6924    let watched = WATCH.get() != 0;
6925    for table_name in TableName::variants() {
6926      let undo_table = popped_frame.table(*table_name);
6927      let state_table = state.table_mut(*table_name);
6928      for (key, undo_count) in undo_table.iter() {
6929        // (a value restored is a change of the state's: [`watch_state`])
6930        if watched && *undo_count > 0 {
6931          note_state_change(*table_name, *key);
6932        }
6933        // Typically only 1 value to shift off the table, unless scopes have been activated.
6934        let named_table = state_table.get_mut(key).unwrap();
6935        for _ in 0..*undo_count {
6936          named_table.pop_front();
6937        }
6938      }
6939    }
6940  }
6941  Ok(())
6942}
6943
6944/// Determine depth of group nesting.
6945///
6946/// nesting created by {,},\bgroup,\egroup,\begingroup,\endgroup
6947/// by counting all frames which are not Daemon frames (and thus don't possess _FRAME_LOCK_).
6948/// This may give incorrect results for some special environments (e.g. minipage)
6949pub fn get_frame_depth() -> usize { state!().undo.iter().filter(|frame| !frame.locked).count() }
6950
6951/// `true` when the CURRENT (front) stack frame is the locked bottom frame —
6952/// i.e. there is no openable group/mode frame to pop. Popping it would FATAL.
6953pub fn current_frame_locked() -> bool { state!().undo.front().map(|f| f.locked).unwrap_or(true) }
6954/// begins a semiverbatim frame, neutralizing the usual + requested characters
6955pub fn begin_semiverbatim(extraspecials: Option<&[char]>) {
6956  // Is this a good/safe enough shorthand, or should we really be doing beginMode?
6957  push_frame();
6958  assign_value("MODE", "restricted_horizontal", None);
6959  assign_value("IN_MATH", false, None);
6960  let mut all_specials: Vec<char> = Vec::new();
6961  if let Some(extra) = extraspecials {
6962    for special in extra {
6963      all_specials.push(*special);
6964    }
6965  }
6966  {
6967    if let Some(Stored::Chars(specials_store)) = state!().lookup_value("SPECIALS") {
6968      for special_char in &**specials_store {
6969        all_specials.push(*special_char);
6970      }
6971    }
6972  }
6973
6974  for special_char in all_specials {
6975    assign_catcode(special_char, Catcode::OTHER, Some(Scope::Local));
6976  }
6977  // `\dospecials` carries every ACTIVE shorthand a language registered
6978  // (babel.def `\bbl@add@special`), so `\url`'s `\@makeother` loop inertizes
6979  // babel-czech's `-`, babel-german's `"` … A live active `-` in a hyperref
6980  // url reached the `href` attribute's expansion, where czech.ldf:243-322's
6981  // `\futurelet` word scanner cannot assign and spun to PushbackLimit
6982  // (csbulletin `\nolinkurl{a-b}`; pdflatex/Perl clean). Guard:
6983  // `perfect_kernel_batch54::semiverbatim_inertizes_babel_shorthands`.
6984  for code in 0x21u8..=0x7Eu8 {
6985    let ch = code as char;
6986    if lookup_catcode(ch) == Some(Catcode::ACTIVE) {
6987      assign_catcode(ch, Catcode::OTHER, Some(Scope::Local));
6988    }
6989  }
6990  assign_mathcode('\'', 0x8000u16, Some(Scope::Local));
6991  // try to stay as ASCII as possible
6992  if let Some(ref current_font) = lookup_font() {
6993    let local_font = current_font.merge(fontmap!(encoding => "ASCII"));
6994    assign_font(Rc::new(local_font), Some(Scope::Local));
6995  }
6996}
6997/// end by just calling `pop_frame`
6998pub fn end_semiverbatim() -> Result<()> { pop_frame() }
6999
7000//   #======================================================================
7001
7002// PARTIAL port of Perl `LaTeXML::Core::State::push/popDaemonFrame`
7003// (used by the Perl `latexmls` daemon to reset bindings between runs while
7004// keeping the loaded Pool). `pop_daemon_frame` is faithful (pop unlocked
7005// frames, unlock + pop the daemon frame, Fatal on the last frame).
7006// `push_daemon_frame` is NOT yet: Perl (State.pm L607-627) additionally
7007// `daemon_copy`s every mutable HASH/ARRAY value binding into the new frame —
7008// so IN-PLACE mutations under the daemon frame (Rust: `with_value_mut` on
7009// `VecDequeStored`/`HashTagData`/... values, and a font's parameter array,
7010// `Stored::FontDimens`, written through its own `Rc<RefCell>` without any
7011// state borrow) can't corrupt the pre-frame
7012// state — and records `_PRELOADED_POOL_`. Without that copy, a daemon reset
7013// only undoes frame-tracked ASSIGNMENTS, not in-place mutations. The Rust
7014// persistent server (`latexml_oxide --server`) instead isolates each
7015// conversion in a `fork()`ed child, so these are not currently wired into a
7016// caller — kept (with the round-trip test in `tests/00_unit_state.rs`) as the
7017// seed of an in-process reset primitive for a future thread-reusing daemon
7018// mode, which MUST add the deep-copy semantics before relying on it. See
7019// `lsp_server` for the chosen fork-isolation design.
7020pub fn push_daemon_frame() {
7021  let daemon_frame = UndoFrame {
7022    locked: true,
7023    ..UndoFrame::default()
7024  };
7025  state_mut!().undo.push_front(daemon_frame);
7026}
7027
7028pub fn pop_daemon_frame() -> Result<()> {
7029  let mut state = state_mut!();
7030  // `is_some_and(!locked)` rather than `unwrap()`: an (impossible-in-practice)
7031  // empty undo stack must fall through to the Fatal below, not panic.
7032  while state.undo.front().is_some_and(|f| !f.locked) {
7033    drop(state);
7034    pop_frame()?;
7035    state = state_mut!();
7036  }
7037  if state.undo.len() > 1 {
7038    state.undo.front_mut().unwrap().locked = false;
7039    drop(state);
7040    pop_frame()?;
7041  } else {
7042    fatal!(
7043      TargetUnexpected,
7044      Endgroup,
7045      "Daemon Attempt to pop last stack frame"
7046    );
7047  }
7048  Ok(())
7049}

7051// ======================================================================
7052/// Set one of the definition prefixes global, etc (only global matters!)
7053pub fn set_prefix(prefix: &str) { state_mut!().prefixes.insert(arena::pin(prefix), true); }
7054/// gets the current value of a named prefix
7055pub fn get_prefix(prefix: &str) -> bool { state!().get_prefix(prefix) }
7056/// `get_prefix` with a pre-pinned SymStr key (see `crate::pin!`) — for the
7057/// per-`\def` prefix probes in `Expandable::new` and friends.
7058pub fn get_prefix_sym(prefix: SymStr) -> bool { state!().get_prefix_sym(prefix) }
7059
7060/// clears the global prefixes
7061pub fn clear_prefixes() { state_mut!().prefixes = HashMap::default(); }
7062/// Whether one of TeX's own prefixes (`\global`, `\long`, `\outer`,
7063/// `\protected`) is waiting for its command. LaTeXML's internal prefixes (the
7064/// algorithm2e binding's `didpar`) are not TeX's and keep Perl's clearing.
7065pub fn has_tex_prefixes() -> bool {
7066  // Probed for every digested primitive: pre-pinned keys, no per-call interning.
7067  let state = state!();
7068  !state.prefixes.is_empty()
7069    && [
7070      pin!("global"),
7071      pin!("long"),
7072      pin!("outer"),
7073      pin!("protected"),
7074    ]
7075    .into_iter()
7076    .any(|p| state.get_prefix_sym(p))
7077}
